# Tagging, part A (applying tags): implementation plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Tag the `audio-router` backlog quickly and safely: Finder-style selection in the Library list, a tag picker (`T`, **+**, drop), × on chips, drag and drop onto sidebar tags, a Tags page grid for triage, Undo for everything, and a confirmation before any recording loses its last private tag. It works in the real app, in `make demo` (saved to the demo's copy) and on the Pages demo (in memory).

**Architecture:**
- **The core gains pure tag operations** (`recordings/tagops.py`, importing only `recordings.models`): `AddTags`, `RemoveTags`, `RestoreTags`, and the effect-recording wrapper `Recorded`. The privacy gate lives inside the operations, so `Writer.mutate` writes nothing when a removal would expose a recording that wasn't confirmed.
- **One tag service** (`recordings_ui/tagging.py`) serves every window. Each Shiny session gets a `TagSession`: it checks a request on the event loop, then applies it one recording at a time as a per-session `reactive.extended_task`, replying with deltas through shinyreact's `send_message`.
  - **Two stores:** `ArchiveStore` writes through the process's one `Writer`, owned by one daemon thread (`recordings_ui/writing.py`). `MemoryStore` keeps the Pages demo's recordings in memory, and is chosen only under Pyodide.
- **The browser** keeps one request in flight (`lib/queue.ts`), merges replies into the `library` value it holds (`lib/library.ts`), and draws the Library list and the Tags grid as virtualised ARIA grids. Handlers, inputs, the queue and the toasts live in `App`, which never unmounts.

**Tech Stack:**
- **Python 3.14** (the server) and **Python 3.12 on Pyodide 0.27.7** (the Pages demo), pydantic 2.14.1 (2.10.5 on Pyodide), shiny 1.8.0, shinyreact 0.1.0, markdown-it-py 4.2.0. **No new Python dependencies.**
- **The browser:** React 19.2 (external, from `window.shinyreact`), Tailwind v4, shadcn/ui (radix-nova style) with its Command, Popover, Alert Dialog and Sonner components, **cmdk 1.1.1**, **sonner 2.0.8**, **@tanstack/react-virtual 3.14.13**, and **axe-core 4.13.0** (dev). Every version is at least two weeks old on 2026-10-15.
- **Testing:** pytest 9.1.1 (with anyio's plugin, already installed with Starlette), Vitest 3.2.7, pytest-playwright 0.10.0 (Playwright 1.63).

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-15-tagging-a-design.md` (binding; §12 records the council's rulings), with its parent `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-08-recordings-design.md` (§6.4, §6.8, §7.3–7.5, §12, §15, §17, §19, §20). Read the spec alongside this plan.

## Decisions made while planning (review these)

Each is a reading of the spec where it leaves a choice open. Code comments cite them as "tagging plan, decision N".

1. **`AddTags` clears an exclusion only for a `notes/<type>` tag added `by: you`.** The UI always adds `by: you`. An `auto` tag keeps the exclusion, so a duplicate merge (`MergeIncoming`, which reuses `AddTags`) never overrides "your choice wins" (main spec §7.3).
2. **The lookalike rule is a little stricter than the spec's sentence.** A new tag whose first folder part *reads* `private` (after NFKC, dropping format characters, `casefold()`, dropping accents and mapping the confusables table) must be spelled `private` in plain letters, in any case. So the fullwidth `ｐｒｉｖａｔｅ` is refused with "Did you mean `private`?", as §9's test list requires, although the hardened `is_private_tag` would call it private.
3. **The case-variant rule is enforced on the server too,** against the tags in the session's view, not only in the picker.
4. **`T` targets the selection, or the focused row when nothing is selected.** Cmd/Ctrl+Up and Down can leave focus outside the selection; `T` then still targets only the selection.
5. **Create offers the typed text trimmed and in NFC.** Shift+Enter sends exactly that text. Text pasted in a decomposed form (NFD, as macOS file names give) is therefore created, not refused (Review Focus 1).
6. **Reply statuses:** `done` when the batch ran to its end, whatever per-recording failures it lists; `failed` when the request was refused or the batch stopped. Every reply also carries `message`: the refusal or the "Stopped: …" phrase.
7. **An Undo's reply carries a token too.** The toast offers it as **Redo**. Cmd/Ctrl+Z's stack holds only the tokens of actions that aren't undos, so pressing it twice undoes two actions, never undo-then-redo.
8. **`refresh` and `tag_request` mount with `null`** and are read with `@reactive.event` (whose default `ignore_none=True` skips the mount value). `library()` depends on a server-side counter that the refresh effect bumps, so the mount default never rebuilds the view a second time at load.
9. **Toasts keep their own timers.** Sonner pauses on hover and pointer down, but not on keyboard focus (checked in its source through context7, `src/index.tsx`'s pause logic). So action toasts are created with `duration: Infinity`, and `lib/dismiss.ts` dismisses them after 8 s, pausing while hovered **or focused**.
10. **Progress replies are throttled** to one every 0.25 s, so a 20,000-recording batch doesn't send 20,000 messages.
11. **A snippet reads one file.** The transcript is `chosen.transcript`, or else the newest `renditions/transcript-*.json` by the UTC stamp at the end of its name; the Plaud note is the oldest `notes-plaud-*.json`.
12. **`pages_store()` picks `MemoryStore`, and refuses outside Pyodide.** `create_app` never refers to `MemoryStore`. Tests construct `MemoryStore` directly.
13. **Demo mode adopts only once:** `create_app` runs `adopt_archive` only when `<tmp>/state/state.db` doesn't exist yet.
14. **An unexpected exception on one recording stops the batch** ("Stopped: an unexpected error (the server log has its class)"): a bug must not fail every recording one by one.
15. **Library rows gain `duration_ms`,** for the grid's Duration sort.
16. **axe-core runs in the e2e tests** by injecting `node_modules/axe-core/axe.min.js` into the page. No Python package is added.
17. **The list focuses rows; the grid focuses cells.** In the list, `role="row"` elements take the roving `tabindex`. In the grid, the pinned `rowheader` cell and each tag cell's `role="checkbox"` do.

## Global Constraints

Every task's requirements include these.

- **Python runs only through uv:** `uv sync`, `uv run …`. Never pip, never the system or Homebrew Python. This plan adds no Python dependency.
- **Node 22,** from `packages/ui/frontend/.nvmrc` (`nvm use`). Install with `npm ci`; add a package with `npm install --save-exact <name>@<version>`. No global installs.
- **Pin npm packages exactly,** as `devDependencies` already are: `cmdk` 1.1.1, `sonner` 2.0.8, `@tanstack/react-virtual` 3.14.13, `axe-core` 4.13.0 (dev). `next-themes` is never installed.
- **React stays external except the `react-dom` index.** The build maps only `react` and `react-dom/client` to `window.shinyreact.React` and `window.shinyreact.ReactDOM`; `react-dom` itself is bundled (spec §6). Never bundle `react`.
- **`www/ui.js` is build output.** Never edit it. Rebuild with `make build`.
- **Every output is subscribed in `App`, and every message handler lives in `App`** (or in a hook `App` calls), because shinyreact drops a message with no handler and `App` never unmounts (`docs/upstream-issues.md`).
- **New Python must import on Pyodide 3.12.** ruff's `target-version = "py312"` checks the syntax; `make lint` must pass. `recordings/tagops.py` imports only `recordings.models` and the standard library. `recordings_ui/tagging.py` imports `sqlite3`, `fcntl`, `recordings.writer`, `recordings.state`, `recordings.index` and `recordings_ui.writing` only inside `ArchiveStore.__init__`.
- **All archive writes go through `Writer`** (`Writer.mutate` for `recording.json`). Tests make archives with `init_archive` and the `Writer`; never write archive files any other way, except a test that simulates an outside edit.
- **Fixtures are synthetic.** Invented titles only ("Week 4", "Synthetic 042"). Never read a real archive.
- **Never read a private recording's transcript or notes** while developing. The app may show them to Dan; you never open them.
- **No tag names, titles or exception text in logs.** Logs carry the exception's class name, recording IDs, the action and tag *counts*. Never `log.exception`, never `exc_info=True` in `tagging.py` or `writing.py`.
- **Fixed phrases** (spec §8), shown and tested verbatim:
  - "edited outside the app: run `recordings reindex`"
  - "the file doesn't validate"
  - "busy: another job holds this recording; try again"
  - "the recording is gone"
  - "not confirmed: would become visible"
  - "already undone"
  - "Undo is no longer available (the page was reloaded)"
  - "Stopped: <fixed phrase>"
- **Caps** (spec §5, §6):
  - at most **20 tags** per request
  - at most **20,000 IDs** per request
  - at most **200 IDs** per snippet request; a snippet stops at **160 characters**
  - the session keeps the last **20** Undo actions
  - the service's locks wait **2 seconds**; **5** consecutive busy recordings stop a batch
  - "Still working…" after **30 seconds** with no reply and no progress
  - a chip's × has a hit area of at least **24×24 px** (44 px in the header on devices that can't hover)
  - a toast stays about **8 seconds**
- **No new HTTP route.** Tag and snippet requests travel only as Shiny inputs on the websocket. Shiny's reconnect stays off (`allow_reconnect` is never called); on `shiny:disconnected` the browser drops its queue and says so.
- **No write from a URL, its hash, or storage.** No request is sent on load. `localStorage` holds only the theme and the grid's non-private column choices, read and written in `try`/`catch`.
- **Check the docs, never write from memory** (CLAUDE.md). Each task names what it checked; say it again in the commit message.

  | What | Where to check |
  |---|---|
  | shinyreact | `.claude/skills/shinyreact-build-app/` (after `make skills`); the installed `@posit-dev/shinyreact` types in `node_modules/@posit-dev/shinyreact/dist-npm/types/` |
  | shiny 1.8.0 | `.venv/lib/python3.14/site-packages/shiny/` (`reactive/_extended_task.py`, `reactive/_reactives.py` `event`, `session/_session.py` `send_custom_message`, `testserver/`) |
  | shadcn/ui | the shadcn skill (`.claude/skills/shadcn/`), and `npx shadcn@4.21.4 view <component>` / `docs <component>` |
  | TanStack Virtual | context7 `/websites/tanstack_virtual` (`rangeExtractor`, `defaultRangeExtractor`, `useFlushSync`) |
  | Sonner | context7 `/websites/sonner_emilkowal_ski` and `/emilkowalski/sonner` |
  | cmdk | context7 `/dip/cmdk` (root `onKeyDown` honours `defaultPrevented`; `shouldFilter={false}`) |
  | Tailwind v4 | https://tailwindcss.com/docs |
  | Playwright | https://playwright.dev/python/docs/api/class-locator (`drag_to`), `class-keyboard` (`ControlOrMeta`) |

  Facts checked on 2026-10-15 that this plan relies on:
  - shinyreact's `window.shinyreact.ReactDOM` is `react-dom/client` only. React's `react-dom` index (`cjs/react-dom.production.js`) builds `createPortal` as a plain element and its `flushSync` reads the external React's shared internals, so a bundled copy works beside the global one.
  - shiny 1.8.0's `ExtendedTask` runs outside the reactive lock, in a context that refuses reactive reads, and queues a second invocation behind a running one. `send_custom_message` writes to the websocket at once, outside any flush.
  - `@reactive.event(x)` reads `x()` first: an unset input raises a silent exception before `ignore_init` counts its first run, and the default `ignore_none=True` skips a `None` value.
  - shiny's `Value.is_set()` registers on a separate dependents list, so reading it doesn't take a dependency on the value.
  - cmdk's root `onKeyDown` calls the user handler first and skips its own handling when the event is `defaultPrevented` or composing.
  - TanStack Virtual's `useFlushSync: false` disables `flushSync`; a custom `rangeExtractor` composed with `defaultRangeExtractor` keeps extra indexes rendered.
  - Sonner pauses a toast's timer only while expanded (hover), interacting (pointer down) or the document is hidden.
  - shadcn's Sonner template imports `next-themes`; Command depends on `cmdk`, Dialog and Input Group; Popover and Alert Dialog on `radix-ui`.
  - Radix's dismissable layer calls `preventDefault()` on the Escape it handles, from a capturing document listener, so React handlers below see `defaultPrevented`.
- **Shell commands:** pytest's temporary folders on macOS resolve under `/private/var`; never paste one into a shell command. In a `grep` pattern write `privat`, not the full word followed by a slash.
- **Commits** use Conventional Commits and end with `Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>`.
- **Format before linting:** `uv run ruff format .` rewraps the plan's Python to the 100-column limit; accept its output. `make lint` checks both.
- **Tests to run before every commit that touches code:** `make lint`, `make test`. Tasks that change the browser also run `make e2e`; tasks that change the Pages path run `make pages pages-test`.

## Review Focus

The five inputs the spec implies but its test list doesn't exercise, most likely first. Each has its test in the task that owns the code.

1. **A new tag typed or pasted in a decomposed form** (NFD: `cafe` plus a combining accent, as macOS file names and some clipboards give). A person expects `café` to be created, not refused with a Unicode message. The picker normalises to NFC before it offers Create (decision 5). Test: Task 9, `picker.test.ts`.
2. **The connection drops in the middle of a batch.** The server must finish the batch or stop between recordings, never raise out of the task, and a reload must show exactly what landed. Test: Task 5, `test_a_send_that_fails_mid_batch_still_finishes_and_never_raises`.
3. **Undo after Refresh.** Refresh rebuilds the view but must keep the session's Undo tokens; a reload is what ends them. Test: Task 5, `test_undo_tokens_survive_a_refresh`.
4. **The active tag filter's tag disappears** after its last removal. The sidebar drops a tag whose count reaches 0, which would hide the filter that is still applied. The sidebar keeps showing the active filter's item, with 0, until the filter changes. Test: Task 13, `Sidebar.test.ts`.
5. **A very long tag** (200 characters, or many folders). The row, its chips and the grid header must stay usable: chips and headers truncate, and the full tag stays in the accessible name. Test: Task 14, `test_a_long_tag_truncates_and_keeps_its_name`.

## Execution

The execution method is **subagent-driven development**, as in stage 2a: a fresh implementer per task, a task review, the mechanical gate (the full suite's passed count never drops), then a three-lens council (reverse, forward, and the task's own **Checkpoint lens**) before the next task. The ledger is `progress.md` in the subagent-driven-development workspace.

**Checkpoint lens:** security and privacy (the spec's privacy and §15 changes).

Spec §10 lists the changes part A makes to the main spec and the runbook. They go in first, so every later task argues from documents that agree. Each change to the main spec is marked "(Dan, 2026-10-15)".

- Modify: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-08-recordings-design.md` (§6.8, §7.3, §7.4, §12, §12.2, §12.3, §15, §17, §17.1, §19, §20)

```
- **Demo mode and tests** use a temporary state folder. Demo mode's holds a `Writer`'s

  2026-10-15). **The Pages demo takes no lock at all:** it tags in memory and saves nothing

  2026-10-15). A session keeps its last 20 actions; a reload ends them. Each revision a tag
  write commits records `origin` as `<op>:<request_id>` (for example `add-tags:3f2a…`), so a

  changed. A full journal, with Undo across reloads, waits for the CLI in the rest of 3a.
```

**§12.2,** replace:
```
- **No confirmation dialogs** in the UI. Every action shows an Undo toast. There are two
  exceptions, both about privacy:
```
with:
```
- **+ and ×** (Dan, 2026-10-15): a **+** after each row's chips, and in the header, opens the
  picker for that recording; each chip has an **×** that removes that tag from that recording.
- **In the picker** (Dan, 2026-10-15), Enter only ever adds, Shift+Enter creates exactly the
  typed text, and Shift+Delete removes the highlighted tag. Create never makes a `notes/` tag or
  a case-variant of an existing tag.
- **No confirmation dialogs** in the UI. Every action shows an Undo toast. There are three
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Add the upstream-issues entry**

In `docs/upstream-issues.md`, insert before `## shinyreact-build-app skill: what it doesn't cover`:
```markdown
## shinyreact 0.1.0: its ReactDOM global is `react-dom/client` only

Found 2026-10-15, while designing tagging part A (spec §6).

**What shinyreact does.** `window.shinyreact.ReactDOM` is React's `react-dom/client` module:
`createRoot` and `hydrateRoot`. It has no `createPortal` and no `flushSync`, which live in the
`react-dom` index module.

**Why it matters.** Radix (Popover, Alert Dialog, Dialog, Tooltip), Sonner and TanStack Virtual
import both from `react-dom`. Mapped to the global, as the shinyreact-build-app skill's Vite
config does for `react-dom`, they find `undefined` and throw on first use.

**The workaround.** `packages/ui/frontend/vite.config.js` maps only `react` and
`react-dom/client` to the globals, and bundles the `react-dom` index. That module requires
`react`, which stays external, so there is still one React. Its `createPortal` builds a plain
element, which the global copy renders. Its `flushSync` runs the callback but can't force the
other copy to flush synchronously; that is acceptable for these libraries, and TanStack Virtual
is set to `useFlushSync: false`. `src/build.test.ts` pins the mapping, and the e2e test
`test_the_built_bundle_portals_toasts_dialogs_and_scrolls` checks the built bundle with a clean
console.

**When it can go.** When shinyreact's global gains `createPortal` (check
`grep -o 'ReactDOM:[A-Za-z]*' …/shinyreact/www/shinyreact.js` and what that object exports), map
`react-dom` back to it, delete the line in `vite.config.js` and `build.test.ts`'s expectation,
and run `make e2e`.

**The skill doesn't say this either.** Its Vite config maps `react-dom` to the global without
noting that the global lacks the index module's exports.
```

### Task 2: The tag operations and the hardened privacy rule (`recordings/tagops.py`)

**Checkpoint lens:** security and privacy.

The operations are pure: each takes a `Recording` and returns the changed one. The privacy gate is inside the operations that can remove a tag, so every caller (the service, part B, the later CLI) gets it. The module imports only `recordings.models` and the standard library, because the Pages demo can't import `sqlite3` or `fcntl` (stage-2a decision 6).

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/core/src/recordings/tagops.py`
- Modify: `packages/core/src/recordings/models.py:169-175` (`is_private_tag`)
- Test: `packages/core/tests/test_tagops.py` (new), `packages/core/tests/test_models.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `recordings.models.Recording`, `TagRef`, `is_private`, `is_private_tag`.
- Produces, in **`recordings.tagops`:**
  - `class WouldExpose(RuntimeError)` with `.recording_id: str`
  - `@dataclass(frozen=True) class LostTag: tag: str; by: Literal["you", "auto"]; index: int`
  - `@dataclass(frozen=True) class Effect: recording_id: str; gained: tuple[str, ...]; lost: tuple[LostTag, ...]; excluded_added: tuple[str, ...]; excluded_removed: tuple[str, ...]`, with the property `empty: bool`
  - `def effect_of(before: Recording, after: Recording) -> Effect`
  - `@dataclass(frozen=True) class AddTags: tags: tuple[TagRef, ...]; name: str = "add-tags"` with `apply(rec) -> Recording`
  - `@dataclass(frozen=True) class RemoveTags: tags: tuple[str, ...]; may_expose: frozenset[str] = frozenset(); name: str = "remove-tags"` with `apply`
  - `@dataclass(frozen=True) class RestoreTags: effect: Effect; may_expose: frozenset[str] = frozenset(); name: str = "restore-tags"` with `apply`
  - `@dataclass class Recorded: op: Op; request_id: str; before: Recording | None = None; after: Recording | None = None`, with the property `name -> f"{op.name}:{request_id}"` and `apply`
  - `def removes_last_private(before: Recording, after: Recording) -> bool`
  - `def validate_new_tag(tag: str, *, note_types: Collection[str] = (), existing: Collection[str] = ()) -> str | None` (None: allowed; otherwise a message to show)
  - the message constants `NOT_NFC`, `INVISIBLE`, `EMPTY_PART`, `TOO_LONG`, `PATH_LIKE`, `NOTE_TYPE`, `LOOKS_PRIVATE`, `CASE_VARIANT`
- Changes, in **`recordings.models`:** `is_private_tag` applies NFKC and drops format characters (category `Cf`) before `casefold()`.

**Done when:** the full suite passes, with the new tests counted. Record the count in the ledger.

```python
    [
        "private",

        "private/health",

        "pri\u200bvate/notes",  # a zero-width space is a format character, dropped
        "\u200dPrivate",  # a zero-width joiner in front
        "private\u00ad",  # a soft hyphen
        "private／journal",  # a fullwidth solidus is a folder separator after NFKC
    ],
)

)
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Harden `is_private_tag`**

In `packages/core/src/recordings/models.py`, add `import unicodedata` beside `from typing import …` (standard-library imports first), and replace `is_private_tag`:
```python
def is_private_tag(tag: str) -> bool:
    """Spec §7.4: the tag's first folder is `private`, in any capitalisation.

    The one place the rule lives. `Private/health` is private; `privateer` and
    `notes/private` are not. It errs towards private: the tag is folded with Unicode NFKC
    (so a fullwidth `ｐｒｉｖａｔｅ`, or a fullwidth solidus, counts) and format characters
    such as a zero-width space are dropped, before `casefold()` (tagging spec §4).
    """
    folded = unicodedata.normalize("NFKC", tag)
    first = folded.split("/", 1)[0]
    first = "".join(c for c in first if unicodedata.category(c) != "Cf")
    return first.casefold() == "private"
```

```python
"""The tag operations (tagging spec §4): pure, with the privacy gate inside them."""

import subprocess
import sys
from datetime import datetime

import pytest
from recordings.models import Recording, TagRef
from recordings.tagops import (
    CASE_VARIANT,
    EMPTY_PART,
    INVISIBLE,
    LOOKS_PRIVATE,
    NOT_NFC,
    NOTE_TYPE,
    PATH_LIKE,
    TOO_LONG,
    AddTags,
    Effect,
    LostTag,
    Recorded,
    RemoveTags,
    RestoreTags,
    WouldExpose,
    effect_of,
    removes_last_private,
    validate_new_tag,
)

RID = "20261006T140003-0700_3fa91c2e"
SHA = "3fa91c2e" + "0" * 56


def rec(*tags: str | tuple[str, str], excluded: tuple[str, ...] = ()) -> Recording:
    """A recording with these tags: "name" is `by: you`, ("name", "auto") is `by: auto`."""
    refs = [
        TagRef(tag=t, by="you") if isinstance(t, str) else TagRef(tag=t[0], by=t[1]) for t in tags
    ]
    return Recording(
        id=RID,
        title="Week 4",
        recorded_at=datetime.fromisoformat("2026-10-06T14:00:03-07:00"),
        timezone="America/Vancouver",
        time_source="plaud",
        media={"file": f"{RID}.mp3", "sha256": SHA, "kind": "audio"},
        tags=refs,
        excluded_note_types=list(excluded),
    )


def names(r: Recording) -> list[str]:
    return [t.tag for t in r.tags]


def you(*tags: str) -> tuple[TagRef, ...]:
    return tuple(TagRef(tag=t, by="you") for t in tags)


# ---- AddTags ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------
def test_add_appends_only_the_missing_tags_and_keeps_an_existing_ones_by():
    out = AddTags(you("talks", "work", "work")).apply(rec(("talks", "auto"), "school"))
    assert names(out) == ["talks", "school", "work"]
    assert out.tags[0].by == "auto"  # already there: left as it is (spec §4)


def test_adding_nothing_new_returns_an_equal_recording_so_mutate_writes_nothing():
    before = rec("talks")
    assert AddTags(you("talks")).apply(before) == before


def test_adding_a_note_type_yourself_clears_its_exclusion_but_an_auto_one_does_not():
    # why: §7.3 "your choice wins" both ways; tagging plan, decision 1.
    excluded = rec("talks", excluded=("lecture", "summary"))
    assert AddTags(you("notes/lecture")).apply(excluded).excluded_note_types == ["summary"]
    auto = AddTags((TagRef(tag="notes/lecture", by="auto"),)).apply(excluded)
    assert auto.excluded_note_types == ["lecture", "summary"]


# ---- RemoveTags ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
def test_remove_drops_the_named_tags_and_skips_ones_the_recording_lacks():
    out = RemoveTags(("talks", "absent")).apply(rec("talks", "school"))
    assert names(out) == ["school"]


def test_removing_a_note_type_records_the_exclusion_whoever_added_it():
    out = RemoveTags(("notes/lecture", "notes/summary")).apply(
        rec(("notes/lecture", "auto"), "notes/summary", "talks", excluded=("summary",))
    )
    assert names(out) == ["talks"]
    assert out.excluded_note_types == ["summary", "lecture"]


def test_a_removal_accepts_an_existing_odd_spelling():
    # why: spec §4, "Removal accepts any existing spelling". New tags with it are refused.
    odd = "talks\u200b"
    assert names(RemoveTags((odd,)).apply(rec(odd, "school"))) == ["school"]


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "spelling", ["private", "Private/health", "PRIVATE", "ｐｒｉｖａｔｅ", "pri\u200bvate"]
)
def test_removing_the_last_private_tag_is_refused_without_may_expose(spelling):
    before = rec(spelling, "talks")
    with pytest.raises(WouldExpose) as caught:
        RemoveTags((spelling,)).apply(before)
    assert caught.value.recording_id == RID
    assert names(RemoveTags((spelling,), frozenset({RID})).apply(before)) == ["talks"]


def test_clearing_every_tag_of_a_private_recording_is_refused():
    with pytest.raises(WouldExpose):
        RemoveTags(("private", "talks")).apply(rec("private", "talks"))


def test_removing_one_of_two_private_tags_needs_no_confirmation():
    out = RemoveTags(("private",)).apply(rec("private/journal", "private"))
    assert names(out) == ["private/journal"]


def test_the_gate_decides_on_the_recording_it_is_given():
    # why: the gate runs under the lock, on the state written (spec §4). A recording that wasn't
    # private when the browser looked, and is now, is refused.
    with pytest.raises(WouldExpose):
        RemoveTags(("private",), frozenset({"20261007T140003-0700_bbbbbbbb"})).apply(rec("private"))


# ---- effects, Recorded and RestoreTags -------------------------------------------------------
def test_an_effect_lists_gains_losses_with_index_and_by_and_exclusions_both_ways():
    before = rec("a", ("notes/lecture", "auto"), "b", "c", excluded=("summary",))
    after = RemoveTags(("notes/lecture", "c")).apply(AddTags(you("notes/summary", "d")).apply(before))
    effect = effect_of(before, after)
    assert effect == Effect(
        recording_id=RID,
        gained=("notes/summary", "d"),
        lost=(LostTag("notes/lecture", "auto", 1), LostTag("c", "you", 3)),
        excluded_added=("lecture",),
        excluded_removed=("summary",),
    )
    assert not effect.empty and effect_of(before, before).empty


def test_recorded_keeps_the_recording_apply_was_given_and_names_the_request():
    op = Recorded(AddTags(you("work")), "req0001abcd")
    before = rec("talks")
    after = op.apply(before)
    assert op.name == "add-tags:req0001abcd"  # revisions.origin (spec §5, "The record")
    assert op.before is before and op.after is after


def test_restore_puts_lost_tags_back_at_their_index_with_their_by_and_drops_gained_ones():
    before = rec("a", ("b", "auto"), "c", "d", excluded=("summary",))
    after = AddTags(you("e", "notes/summary")).apply(RemoveTags(("b", "d")).apply(before))
    restored = RestoreTags(effect_of(before, after)).apply(after)
    assert restored.tags == before.tags
    assert restored.excluded_note_types == before.excluded_note_types


def test_undo_leaves_a_tag_another_writer_added_alone():
    # why: spec §4. The effect is taken from what apply was given, so a tag that landed between
    # the browser's view and the write is never in it.
    op = Recorded(AddTags(you("work")), "req0001abcd")
    op.apply(rec("talks", "school"))  # "school" came from another window, before this write
    effect = effect_of(op.before, op.after)
    assert effect.gained == ("work",)
    later = AddTags(you("later")).apply(op.after)
    assert names(RestoreTags(effect).apply(later)) == ["talks", "school", "later"]


def test_an_undo_that_takes_private_away_needs_confirmation():
    before = rec()
    after = AddTags(you("private")).apply(before)
    effect = effect_of(before, after)
    with pytest.raises(WouldExpose):
        RestoreTags(effect).apply(after)
    assert names(RestoreTags(effect, frozenset({RID})).apply(after)) == []

    assert removes_last_private(rec("private", "talks"), rec("talks"))
    assert removes_last_private(rec("Private/health"), rec())
    assert not removes_last_private(rec("private/a", "private/b"), rec("private/b"))
    assert not removes_last_private(rec("talks"), rec())

@pytest.mark.parametrize(

        ("pr\u0130vate", LOOKS_PRIVATE),  # capital I with a dot
        ("pr\u00edvate", LOOKS_PRIVATE),  # an accent
        ("..", PATH_LIKE),
        ("school/../x", PATH_LIKE),
        ("a\\b", PATH_LIKE),
        ("a\x00b", INVISIBLE),
        ("a\nb", INVISIBLE),
        ("a\u2028b", INVISIBLE),  # line separator
        ("x" * 65, TOO_LONG),
        ("/".join(["x" * 60] * 4), TOO_LONG),  # 243 characters
        ("cafe\u0301", NOT_NFC),
        ("a//b", EMPTY_PART),
        (" a", EMPTY_PART),
        ("notes/unknown", NOTE_TYPE),  # not a type the archive has
        ("notes/Lecture", NOTE_TYPE),
        ("notes/lecture/x", NOTE_TYPE),
        ("Notes/lecture", NOTE_TYPE),
        ("notes", NOTE_TYPE),
    ],
)
def test_a_new_tag_that_breaks_a_rule_is_refused(tag, message):
    assert validate_new_tag(tag, note_types={"lecture"}) == message


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "tag",
    ["school/course-101", "private", "Private/health", "caf\u00e9", "notes/lecture", "日本語/メモ", "my talks"],
)
def test_a_new_tag_that_meets_the_rules_is_allowed(tag):
    assert validate_new_tag(tag, note_types={"lecture"}) is None


def test_a_case_variant_of_an_existing_tag_is_refused():
    assert validate_new_tag("Talks", existing={"talks"}) == CASE_VARIANT
    assert validate_new_tag("talks", existing={"talks"}) is None  # the same tag, not a variant


def test_the_messages_never_repeat_the_tag():
    # why: a refusal is shown to Dan, but it is also the kind of text that ends up in a report.
    for message in (NOT_NFC, INVISIBLE, EMPTY_PART, TOO_LONG, PATH_LIKE, NOTE_TYPE, CASE_VARIANT):
        assert "{" not in message and "%" not in message
    assert LOOKS_PRIVATE == "Did you mean `private`?"


def test_tagops_imports_with_sqlite3_and_fcntl_blocked():
    # why: the Pages demo runs these operations on Pyodide, which has neither (stage-2a
    # decision 6). A module-level import of either would break tagging there.
    code = (
        "import sys; sys.modules['sqlite3'] = None; sys.modules['fcntl'] = None\n"
        "import recordings.tagops\n"
        "assert 'recordings.writer' not in sys.modules\n"
    )
    done = subprocess.run([sys.executable, "-c", code], capture_output=True, text=True)
    assert done.returncode == 0, done.stderr
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Write the operations**

`packages/core/src/recordings/tagops.py`:
```python
"""Tag operations (tagging spec §4): pure functions of a Recording, with the privacy gate inside.

Each operation takes a Recording and returns the changed one; an unchanged result is equal to its
input, so `Writer.mutate` writes nothing. On the real archive they run through `Writer.mutate`,
under the recording's lock, with `rev` and the refusal of files edited outside the app (spec
§6.4). The Pages demo runs the same operations in memory.

This module imports only `recordings.models` and the standard library: the Pages demo can't
import sqlite3 or fcntl (stage-2a decision 6).

**The privacy gate.** An operation that can remove a tag raises `WouldExpose` when its result
would leave a recording that had a private tag with none, unless the recording's ID is in
`may_expose`. It decides on the Recording it is given, which under `mutate` is the file as it is
on disk, read under the lock.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import re
import unicodedata
from collections.abc import Collection
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Literal, Protocol

from recordings.models import Recording, TagRef, is_private

NOTES = "notes/"

# Why a new tag is refused (spec §4). Shown to Dan as they are; none repeats the tag.
NOT_NFC = "Use the composed form of these letters (Unicode NFC)."
INVISIBLE = "A tag can't hold invisible, control or line-separator characters."
EMPTY_PART = "A folder part is empty, or starts or ends with a space."
TOO_LONG = "Each folder part is at most 64 characters, and the whole tag at most 200."
PATH_LIKE = "A folder part can't be . or .., and a tag can't hold a backslash."
NOTE_TYPE = "Note types come from prompt files: notes/<type> must name one the archive has."
LOOKS_PRIVATE = "Did you mean `private`?"
CASE_VARIANT = "A tag with the same letters in another case exists already."

MAX_PART = 64
MAX_TAG = 200
_REFUSED = frozenset({"Cc", "Cf", "Zl", "Zp", "Co", "Cs"})
_NOTE_TYPE = re.compile(r"[a-z0-9-]+")
# Cyrillic and Greek letters that look like the Latin letters of "private" (spec §4).
_CONFUSABLES = str.maketrans(
    {
        "\u0440": "p",  # Cyrillic er
        "\u03c1": "p",  # Greek rho
        "\u0433": "r",  # Cyrillic ghe
        "\u0456": "i",  # Cyrillic byelorussian-ukrainian i
        "\u03b9": "i",  # Greek iota
        "\u0131": "i",  # dotless i
        "\u04cf": "i",  # Cyrillic palochka
        "\u0475": "v",  # Cyrillic izhitsa
        "\u03bd": "v",  # Greek nu
        "\u0430": "a",  # Cyrillic a
        "\u03b1": "a",  # Greek alpha
        "\u0442": "t",  # Cyrillic te
        "\u03c4": "t",  # Greek tau
        "\u0435": "e",  # Cyrillic ie
        "\u03b5": "e",  # Greek epsilon
    }
)


class WouldExpose(RuntimeError):
    """Applying this would leave a recording that had a private tag with none, and its ID
    wasn't in `may_expose`. Raised from `apply`, so `mutate` writes nothing."""

    def __init__(self, recording_id: str) -> None:
        super().__init__(recording_id)
        self.recording_id = recording_id


class Op(Protocol):
    @property
    def name(self) -> str: ...

    def apply(self, rec: Recording) -> Recording: ...


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class LostTag:
    tag: str
    by: Literal["you", "auto"]
    index: int  # where it was in the recording's tags, so Undo puts it back there


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Effect:
    """What one write changed on one recording: what its Undo reverses (spec §4)."""

    recording_id: str
    gained: tuple[str, ...]
    lost: tuple[LostTag, ...]
    excluded_added: tuple[str, ...]
    excluded_removed: tuple[str, ...]

    @property
    def empty(self) -> bool:
        return not (self.gained or self.lost or self.excluded_added or self.excluded_removed)


def effect_of(before: Recording, after: Recording) -> Effect:
    had, has = {t.tag for t in before.tags}, {t.tag for t in after.tags}
    old, new = before.excluded_note_types, after.excluded_note_types
    return Effect(
        recording_id=before.id,
        gained=tuple(t.tag for t in after.tags if t.tag not in had),
        lost=tuple(LostTag(t.tag, t.by, i) for i, t in enumerate(before.tags) if t.tag not in has),
        excluded_added=tuple(n for n in new if n not in old),
        excluded_removed=tuple(n for n in old if n not in new),
    )


def removes_last_private(before: Recording, after: Recording) -> bool:
    """True when `before` has a private tag and `after` has none (spec §4)."""
    return is_private(before) and not is_private(after)


def _gate(before: Recording, after: Recording, may_expose: frozenset[str]) -> Recording:
    if removes_last_private(before, after) and before.id not in may_expose:
        raise WouldExpose(before.id)
    return after


def _note_type(tag: str) -> str | None:
    return tag[len(NOTES) :] if tag.startswith(NOTES) else None


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class AddTags:
    """Adds the tags the recording doesn't have. A tag already there is left as it is, `by`
    included. Adding `notes/<type>` yourself takes `<type>` out of `excluded_note_types`
    (tagging plan, decision 1)."""

    tags: tuple[TagRef, ...]
    name: str = "add-tags"

    def apply(self, rec: Recording) -> Recording:
        tags, have = list(rec.tags), {t.tag for t in rec.tags}
        for tag in self.tags:
            if tag.tag not in have:
                tags.append(tag)
                have.add(tag.tag)
        yours = {_note_type(t.tag) for t in self.tags if t.by == "you"} - {None}
        excluded = [n for n in rec.excluded_note_types if n not in yours]
        update: dict = {"tags": tags}
        if excluded != rec.excluded_note_types:
            update["excluded_note_types"] = excluded
        if tags == rec.tags and "excluded_note_types" not in update:
            return rec
        return rec.model_copy(update=update)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class RemoveTags:
    """Removes the named tags; a tag the recording doesn't have is skipped. Removing any
    `notes/<type>`, `auto` or `you`, adds `<type>` to `excluded_note_types`, so auto-pick never
    adds it back (main spec §7.3). The privacy gate applies (spec §4)."""

    tags: tuple[str, ...]
    may_expose: frozenset[str] = frozenset()
    name: str = "remove-tags"

    def apply(self, rec: Recording) -> Recording:
        drop = set(self.tags)
        kept = [t for t in rec.tags if t.tag not in drop]
        if len(kept) == len(rec.tags):
            return rec
        removed = [n for t in rec.tags if t.tag in drop and (n := _note_type(t.tag))]
        excluded = list(rec.excluded_note_types)
        excluded += [n for n in dict.fromkeys(removed) if n not in excluded]
        after = rec.model_copy(update={"tags": kept, "excluded_note_types": excluded})
        return _gate(rec, after, self.may_expose)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class RestoreTags:
    """Undo's operation (spec §4): puts lost tags back at their original index with their
    original `by`, removes gained tags, and reverses the exclusion changes. The privacy gate
    applies: an Undo that takes `private` away asks first."""

    effect: Effect
    may_expose: frozenset[str] = frozenset()
    name: str = "restore-tags"

    def apply(self, rec: Recording) -> Recording:
        gained = set(self.effect.gained)
        tags = [t for t in rec.tags if t.tag not in gained]
        have = {t.tag for t in tags}
        for lost in sorted(self.effect.lost, key=lambda x: x.index):
            if lost.tag not in have:
                tags.insert(min(lost.index, len(tags)), TagRef(tag=lost.tag, by=lost.by))
                have.add(lost.tag)
        excluded = [n for n in rec.excluded_note_types if n not in self.effect.excluded_added]
        excluded += [n for n in self.effect.excluded_removed if n not in excluded]
        if tags == rec.tags and excluded == rec.excluded_note_types:
            return rec
        after = rec.model_copy(update={"tags": tags, "excluded_note_types": excluded})
        return _gate(rec, after, self.may_expose)


@dataclass
class Recorded:
    """Wraps an operation so the caller sees the Recording `apply` was given (before) and the one
    it returned (after), both taken under the recording's lock (spec §4). `mutate` itself is
    unchanged. Its name is `<op>:<request_id>`, which `mutate` records as the revision's origin
    in state.db (spec §5, "The record")."""

    op: Op
    request_id: str
    before: Recording | None = None
    after: Recording | None = None

    @property
    def name(self) -> str:
        return f"{self.op.name}:{self.request_id}"

    def apply(self, rec: Recording) -> Recording:
        self.before = rec
        self.after = self.op.apply(rec)
        return self.after


def _reads_as(part: str) -> str:
    """How a folder part reads, for the lookalike check: NFKC, format characters dropped,
    casefolded, accents dropped, and the confusable letters mapped to Latin ones."""
    text = unicodedata.normalize("NFKC", part)
    text = "".join(c for c in text if unicodedata.category(c) != "Cf").casefold()
    text = "".join(c for c in unicodedata.normalize("NFKD", text) if unicodedata.category(c) != "Mn")
    return text.translate(_CONFUSABLES)


def validate_new_tag(
    tag: str, *, note_types: Collection[str] = (), existing: Collection[str] = ()
) -> str | None:
    """Why a *new* tag can't be made, or None (spec §4). A tag that already exists in the archive
    is never checked: adding it again, or removing it, accepts any spelling."""
    if not unicodedata.is_normalized("NFC", tag):
        return NOT_NFC
    if any(unicodedata.category(c) in _REFUSED for c in tag):
        return INVISIBLE
    parts = tag.split("/")
    if any(not p or p != p.strip() for p in parts):
        return EMPTY_PART
    if len(tag) > MAX_TAG or any(len(p) > MAX_PART for p in parts):
        return TOO_LONG
    if "\\" in tag or any(p in (".", "..") for p in parts):
        return PATH_LIKE
    if parts[0].casefold() == "notes":
        # Note types come from prompt files (main spec §7.3): the picker can't invent one.
        ok = (
            parts[0] == "notes"
            and len(parts) == 2
            and _NOTE_TYPE.fullmatch(parts[1]) is not None
            and parts[1] in note_types
        )
        if not ok:
            return NOTE_TYPE
    if _reads_as(parts[0]) == "private" and parts[0].casefold() != "private":
        return LOOKS_PRIVATE  # tagging plan, decision 2
    folded = tag.casefold()
    if any(other != tag and other.casefold() == folded for other in existing):
        return CASE_VARIANT
    return None

```

### Task 3: The Writer: a lock timeout of its own, `AddTags` from `tagops`, and the record of a request

**Checkpoint lens:** data integrity and write safety.

Three small changes to `writer.py`, each checked against the code that reads what it changes:
- **`AddTags` moves to `tagops`** and is re-exported, so `from recordings.writer import AddTags` keeps working (`MergeIncoming`, `SetPlaudFields`, the import, the tests).
- **The lock timeout becomes the `Writer`'s own** (`Writer(…, lock_timeout=30.0)`, `Writer.open(cfg, …, lock_timeout=30.0)`), so the tag service can wait 2 s while the CLI and the import keep 30 s (spec §5, §11).
- **`revisions.origin` becomes `<op>:<request_id>`** for a tag write. `mutate` already records `op.name` as the origin, and `Recorded.name` (Task 2) is `<op>:<request_id>`, so `mutate` doesn't change. **Who reads `origin`** (spec §11, checked 2026-10-15 with `grep -rn "origin" packages/core/src`): only `State.begin`, which writes it, and the `Revision` dataclass, which carries it. `reindex`, `_check_fresh`, `_accept` and the merge bases compare `sha256` and `body`, never `origin`. The test below proves `reindex` and the next write still work after a tag write.

**Files:**
- Modify: `packages/core/src/recordings/writer.py:103-120` (`Op`, `AddTags`), `:272-312` (`Writer.__init__`), `:339-371` (`Writer.open`)
- Modify: `packages/core/src/recordings/state.py:101` (the `origin` comment)
- Test: `packages/core/tests/test_writer.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `recordings.tagops.AddTags`, `Recorded`, `RemoveTags`, `WouldExpose` (Task 2).
- Produces:
  - `Writer(root, state, index, *, writer_id, rebuild=True, auto_private=…, lock_timeout: float = 30.0)`; `writer.locks.timeout == lock_timeout`
  - `Writer.open(cfg, *, docs=True, rebuild=True, lock_timeout: float = 30.0) -> Writer`
  - `recordings.writer.AddTags is recordings.tagops.AddTags`
  - a write through `Recorded(op, request_id)` commits a revision whose `origin` is `f"{op.name}:{request_id}"`
  - `WouldExpose` raised inside `mutate` leaves `recording.json`, its `rev` and `state.db`'s revisions untouched

```python
def test_add_tags_is_the_tagops_one():
    # why: tagging spec §4. The operations live in tagops, which Pages can import; writer.py
    # re-exports AddTags so every existing caller keeps working.
    from recordings import tagops

    assert AddTags is tagops.AddTags


def test_the_lock_timeout_is_each_writers_own(make_writer, make_incoming):
    # why: tagging spec §5 and §11. The tag service waits 2 s; the CLI and the import keep 30 s.
    writer = make_writer()
    rec = writer.add(make_incoming()).recording
    quick = Writer(
        writer.root, writer.state, writer.index, writer_id="test", lock_timeout=0.1
    )
    assert quick.locks.timeout == 0.1 and writer.locks.timeout == 30.0
    with Locks(writer.state.locks_dir).hold(rec.id), pytest.raises(LockTimeout):
        quick.mutate(rec.id, AddTags((TagRef(tag="talks"),)))


def test_writer_open_passes_the_lock_timeout_through(writer, tmp_path):
    cfg_file = tmp_path / "config.toml"
    cfg_file.write_text(
        f'[archive]\npath = "{writer.root}"\nwriter_id = "test"\n'
        f'[state]\npath = "{writer.state.path}"\n'
        f'[index]\npath = "{writer.index.path}"\n',
        encoding="utf-8",
    )
    opened = Writer.open(load_config({"RECORDINGS_CONFIG": str(cfg_file)}), lock_timeout=2.0)
    assert opened.locks.timeout == 2.0


def test_a_tag_write_records_its_request_and_the_merge_base_still_works(writer, make_incoming):
    # why: tagging spec §5, "The record", and §11: revisions.origin becomes <op>:<request_id>,
    # and nothing that reads revisions (reindex, the merge bases) may break.
    rec = writer.add(make_incoming()).recording
    rel = writer._rel(rec.id)
    writer.mutate(rec.id, Recorded(AddTags((TagRef(tag="talks"),)), "req0001abcd"))
    assert writer.state.last_committed(rel).origin == "add-tags:req0001abcd"
    report = writer.reindex()
    assert report.adopted == () and report.invalid == ()
    assert writer.mutate(rec.id, AddTags((TagRef(tag="next"),))).rev == 3


def test_would_expose_leaves_the_file_and_its_revisions_untouched(writer, make_incoming):
    # why: tagging spec §4. The gate raises inside apply, under the lock, before anything is
    # written: no file, no rev, no pending revision.
    rec = writer.add(make_incoming(tags=(TagRef(tag="private"),))).recording
    rel = writer._rel(rec.id)
    path = writer.root / rel
    before = path.read_bytes()
    with pytest.raises(WouldExpose):
        writer.mutate(rec.id, Recorded(RemoveTags(("private",)), "req0002abcd"))
    assert path.read_bytes() == before
    assert writer.state.pending(rel) is None
    assert writer.state.last_committed(rel).rev == 1
    allowed = writer.mutate(rec.id, RemoveTags(("private",), frozenset({rec.id})))
    assert allowed.tags == [] and allowed.rev == 2
```

### Task 4: The library view's parts, the two stores and the writer thread

**Checkpoint lens:** data integrity and write safety.

The tag service (Task 5) needs three things this task builds:
- **The library view in parts** (`views.row`, `views.summarise`, `views.library_view_from`), so the service can patch one row and recount without rebuilding the view, and `MemoryStore` can build a view from its overlay.
- **The two stores** (spec §5). `ArchiveStore` writes through the process's one `Writer`; `MemoryStore` keeps the Pages demo's changes in memory and never writes a file, takes a lock or starts a thread.
- **The writer thread** (`recordings_ui/writing.py`). One daemon thread owns the process's one `Writer`, because lock re-entrancy is per `Locks` instance *and* per thread, a lock must never be held across an `await`, the event loop must never block on a file write, and every window's writes must be serialised. A second one-thread reader serves snippets (Task 7).

**Files:**
- Modify: `packages/ui/src/recordings_ui/views.py` (`_row` becomes `row`, with `duration_ms`; `summarise`; `library_view_from`)
- Create: `packages/ui/src/recordings_ui/writing.py`
- Create: `packages/ui/src/recordings_ui/tagging.py`
- Modify: `packages/ui/tests/conftest.py` (the `tag_archive` fixture)
- Test: `packages/ui/tests/test_views.py`, `packages/ui/tests/test_tag_stores.py` (new)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `recordings.tagops.Recorded` (Task 2); `Writer(…, lock_timeout=)` (Task 3).
- Produces, in **`recordings_ui.views`:**
  - `row(rec: Recording) -> dict`: the library row, now with `"duration_ms": int | None`
  - `summarise(rows: Iterable[dict]) -> dict` with keys `counts`, `tags`, `note_types`
  - `library_view_from(recordings: Iterable[Recording], problems: Iterable[Problem]) -> dict`
  - `library_view(archive)` unchanged in shape (plus `duration_ms` in rows)
- Produces, in **`recordings_ui.writing`** (imported only by `ArchiveStore.__init__`):
  - `class ThreadStopped(RuntimeError)`
  - `class OneThread(concurrent.futures.Executor)`: `OneThread(name)`, `submit`, `alive() -> bool`, `shutdown(wait=True, *, cancel_futures=False)`
  - `class WriterThread`: `WriterThread(open_writer: Callable[[], Writer])`, attributes `executor: OneThread`, `writer: Writer | None`, `reason: str | None`, `opened: Future`; methods `ensure_open() -> bool` (on the thread), `status() -> tuple[bool, str | None]`, `close()`
  - the phrases `OPENING`, `STOPPED`, `TRANSIENT`
- Produces, in **`recordings_ui.tagging`:**
  - the fixed phrases `GONE`, `STALE`, `INVALID`, `BUSY`, `NOT_CONFIRMED`, `STOP_NOT_WRITER`, `STOP_FULL`, `STOP_READ_ONLY`, `STOP_IO`, `STOP_STATE`, `STOP_BUSY`, `STOP_SHUTDOWN`, `STOP_THREAD`, `STOP_UNEXPECTED`; `LOCK_WAIT = 2.0`
  - `class Classified(NamedTuple): reason: str; stops: bool`
  - `def under_pyodide() -> bool`
  - `class Store(Protocol)` with `saves`, `archive`, `library()`, `read(rid)`, `apply(rid, op)`, `async write(fn)`, `async read_off_loop(fn)`, `async open() -> tuple[bool, str | None]`, `classify(exc) -> Classified | None`, `close()`
  - `class MemoryStore(archive: Archive)` (also `changed() -> dict[str, Recording]` for tests)
  - `class ArchiveStore(archive: Archive, open_writer: Callable[[], Writer])`, plus `wait_open(timeout: float) -> tuple[bool, str | None]`
  - `def pages_store(archive: Archive) -> MemoryStore` (raises `RuntimeError` outside Pyodide)
- Produces, in **`packages/ui/tests/conftest.py`:** the fixture `tag_archive -> TagArchive` with `root`, `state`, `index`, `ids: dict[str, str]` (slugs `plain`, `private`, `journal`, `untagged`, `noted`, `excluded`), `open_writer(*, lock_timeout=2.0, writer_id="test") -> Writer`, and `tags(slug) -> list[str]`.

```python
def test_rows_carry_the_duration_in_milliseconds(demo_archive, demo_ids):
    # why: the Tags grid sorts by duration (tagging spec §7); the label alone can't sort.
    rows = {r["id"]: r for r in library_view(Archive(demo_archive))["recordings"]}
    assert rows[demo_ids["jfk-rice"]]["duration_ms"] == 90009
    assert rows[demo_ids["jfk-rice"]]["duration"] == "1 min"


def test_summarise_recounts_from_the_rows_alone(demo_archive):
    view = library_view(Archive(demo_archive))
    again = summarise(view["recordings"])
    assert again == {k: view[k] for k in ("counts", "tags", "note_types")}
    tagged = [{**r, "tags": [*r["tags"], {"tag": "Private/x", "by": "you"}]} for r in view["recordings"]]
    counted = summarise(tagged)
    assert counted["counts"] == {"all": 4, "untagged": 0}
    assert {"tag": "Private/x", "count": 4, "private": True} in counted["tags"]


def test_a_view_from_recordings_matches_the_archives_own(demo_archive):
    archive = Archive(demo_archive)
    recordings = list(archive.iter_recordings())
    assert library_view_from(recordings, archive.problems) == library_view(archive)
    assert row(recordings[0])["id"] == recordings[0].id
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Split the library view**

In `packages/ui/src/recordings_ui/views.py`, add `from collections.abc import Iterable` to the imports, rename `_row` to `row`, add `duration_ms`, and replace `library_view`:
```python
def row(rec: Recording) -> dict:
    """One Library row. The tag service patches rows with it after a tag change (spec §5)."""
    return {
        "id": rec.id,
        "title": rec.title,
        "recorded_at": rec.recorded_at.isoformat(),
        "when": when_label(rec.recorded_at),
        "duration": duration_label(rec.media.duration_ms),
        "duration_ms": rec.media.duration_ms,
        "kind": rec.media.kind,
        "sources": sorted({s.kind for s in rec.sources}),
        "tags": _tags(rec),
        "private": is_private(rec),
        "untagged": is_untagged(rec),
    }


def summarise(rows: Iterable[dict]) -> dict:
    """The library view's counts, tags (each with its `private` flag) and note types, from its
    rows alone. The tag service recounts them after every change (spec §5); the browser never
    re-implements the privacy rule."""
    rows = list(rows)
    counts = Counter(t["tag"] for r in rows for t in r["tags"])
    return {
        "counts": {"all": len(rows), "untagged": sum(not r["tags"] for r in rows)},
        "tags": [
            {"tag": tag, "count": n, "private": is_private_tag(tag)}
            for tag, n in sorted(counts.items())
            if not tag.startswith("notes/")
        ],
        "note_types": [
            {"note_type": tag.removeprefix("notes/"), "count": n}
            for tag, n in sorted(counts.items())
            if tag.startswith("notes/")
        ],
    }


def library_view_from(recordings: Iterable[Recording], problems: Iterable[Problem]) -> dict:
    rows = [row(r) for r in sorted(recordings, key=lambda r: r.recorded_at, reverse=True)]
    return {
        "recordings": rows,
        **summarise(rows),
        "problems": [{"path": str(p.path), "message": p.message} for p in problems],
    }


def library_view(archive: Archive) -> dict:
    recordings = list(archive.iter_recordings())  # fills archive.problems as it goes
    return library_view_from(recordings, archive.problems)
```

```python
T0 = datetime.fromisoformat("2026-10-06T14:00:00-07:00")
# slug: (minutes after T0, tags as (tag, by)). Invented titles, synthetic media.
TAGGED: dict[str, tuple[int, list[tuple[str, str]]]] = {
    "plain": (0, [("talks", "you")]),
    "private": (1, [("private", "you")]),
    "journal": (2, [("private/journal", "you"), ("private", "you")]),
    "untagged": (3, []),
    "noted": (4, [("notes/lecture", "auto"), ("school/course-101", "you")]),
    "excluded": (5, [("talks", "you")]),
}


@dataclass
class TagArchive:
    """A synthetic archive for the tagging tests, made through a Writer, and the folders an
    ArchiveStore opens its own Writer on (tagging spec §9)."""

    root: Path
    state: Path
    index: Path
    ids: dict[str, str] = field(default_factory=dict)

    def open_writer(self, *, lock_timeout: float = 2.0, writer_id: str = "test") -> Writer:
        return Writer(
            self.root,
            State.open(self.state),
            Index(self.index),
            writer_id=writer_id,
            lock_timeout=lock_timeout,
        )

    def tags(self, slug: str) -> list[str]:
        return [t.tag for t in Archive(self.root).load(self.ids[slug]).tags]


@pytest.fixture
def tag_archive(tmp_path) -> TagArchive:
    made = TagArchive(tmp_path / "tag-archive", tmp_path / "tag-state", tmp_path / "tag-index.db")
    init_archive(made.root, made.state, writer_id="test")
    writer = made.open_writer(lock_timeout=30.0)  # setup only; each store opens its own
    media = tmp_path / "tag-media"
    media.mkdir()
    for slug, (minutes, tags) in TAGGED.items():
        path = media / f"{slug}.mp3"
        path.write_bytes(f"ID3 synthetic audio {slug}".encode())
        incoming = Incoming(

            recorded_at=T0 + timedelta(minutes=minutes),
            timezone_name="America/Vancouver",
            time_source="metadata",
            title=f"Week {minutes}",
            kind="audio",
            sources=(),
            tags=tuple(TagRef(tag=t, by=b) for t, b in tags),
            excluded_note_types=("summary",) if slug == "excluded" else (),
        )
        made.ids[slug] = writer.add(incoming).recording.id
```

```python
"""The tag service's stores and the writer thread (tagging spec §5)."""

import asyncio
import fcntl
import subprocess
import sys
import threading
import time

import pytest
from pydantic import BaseModel
from recordings.archive import Archive
from recordings.index import Index
from recordings.init import init_archive
from recordings.locks import LockTimeout
from recordings.models import TagRef
from recordings.sentinel import MIRROR, SENTINEL
from recordings.state import State
from recordings.tagops import AddTags, Recorded
from recordings.writer import InvalidFile, NotTheWriter, StaleFile, Writer
from recordings_ui import tagging
from recordings_ui.tagging import (
    BUSY,
    INVALID,
    STALE,
    STOP_NOT_WRITER,
    STOP_STATE,
    STOP_THREAD,
    ArchiveStore,
    Classified,
    MemoryStore,
    pages_store,
)
from recordings_ui.writing import STOPPED, OneThread, ThreadStopped


def add(tag: str) -> Recorded:
    return Recorded(AddTags((TagRef(tag=tag, by="you"),)), "req0001abcd")


def run(coro):
    return asyncio.run(coro)


@pytest.fixture(params=["archive", "memory"])
def store(request, tag_archive):
    if request.param == "archive":
        made = ArchiveStore(Archive(tag_archive.root), tag_archive.open_writer)
    else:
        made = MemoryStore(Archive(tag_archive.root))
    yield made
    made.close()


def test_both_stores_apply_an_operation_bump_rev_and_show_it(store, tag_archive):
    rid = tag_archive.ids["untagged"]
    after = run(store.write(lambda: store.apply(rid, add("talks"))))
    assert [t.tag for t in after.tags] == ["talks"] and after.rev == 2
    assert [t.tag for t in store.read(rid).tags] == ["talks"]
    shown = {r["id"]: r for r in store.library()["recordings"]}[rid]
    assert shown["tags"] == [{"tag": "talks", "by": "you"}] and shown["untagged"] is False


def test_an_unchanged_result_writes_nothing(store, tag_archive):
    rid = tag_archive.ids["plain"]
    after = run(store.write(lambda: store.apply(rid, add("talks"))))
    assert after.rev == 1


def test_the_archive_store_writes_the_file(tag_archive):
    store = ArchiveStore(Archive(tag_archive.root), tag_archive.open_writer)
    try:
        run(store.write(lambda: store.apply(tag_archive.ids["untagged"], add("talks"))))
        assert tag_archive.tags("untagged") == ["talks"]
    finally:
        store.close()


def test_the_memory_store_writes_no_file_takes_no_lock_and_starts_no_thread(
    tag_archive, monkeypatch
):
    # why: spec §5. Pages can't run threads or locks (stage-2a decisions 6 and 7), and its
    # pydantic can't serialise these models faithfully (stage-2a decision 7).
    def refuse(*_args, **_kwargs):
        raise AssertionError("the memory store must not do this")

    before = {p: p.read_bytes() for p in tag_archive.root.rglob("*") if p.is_file()}
    threads = threading.active_count()
    monkeypatch.setattr(BaseModel, "model_dump_json", refuse)
    monkeypatch.setattr(fcntl, "flock", refuse)
    store = MemoryStore(Archive(tag_archive.root))
    rid = tag_archive.ids["untagged"]
    run(store.write(lambda: store.apply(rid, add("talks"))))
    assert [t.tag for t in store.read(rid).tags] == ["talks"]
    assert list(store.changed()) == [rid]
    assert {p: p.read_bytes() for p in tag_archive.root.rglob("*") if p.is_file()} == before
    assert threading.active_count() <= threads  # none started (others may have ended)
    assert run(store.open()) == (True, None) and store.saves is False


def test_the_pages_store_is_memory_and_only_under_pyodide(tag_archive, monkeypatch):
    # why: spec §5. Outside Pyodide the service never falls back to memory, so `make demo` and
    # the real app can't silently stop saving.
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="Pyodide"):
        pages_store(Archive(tag_archive.root))
    monkeypatch.setattr(tagging, "under_pyodide", lambda: True)
    assert isinstance(pages_store(Archive(tag_archive.root)), MemoryStore)


def test_the_tagging_module_imports_without_sqlite3_fcntl_or_the_writer(tag_archive):
    # why: spec §5. recordings_ui.tagging is vendored into the Pages demo, whose Pyodide has no
    # sqlite3 or fcntl; only ArchiveStore may bring them in.
    code = (
        "import sys; sys.modules['sqlite3'] = None; sys.modules['fcntl'] = None\n"
        "from pathlib import Path\n"
        "from recordings.archive import Archive\n"
        "from recordings_ui.tagging import MemoryStore\n"
        f"view = MemoryStore(Archive(Path({str(tag_archive.root)!r}))).library()\n"
        "assert view['counts']['all'] == 6\n"
        "assert 'recordings.writer' not in sys.modules\n"
        "assert 'recordings_ui.writing' not in sys.modules\n"
    )
    done = subprocess.run([sys.executable, "-c", code], capture_output=True, text=True)
    assert done.returncode == 0, done.stderr


# ---- can_tag and its reasons (spec §5) -------------------------------------------------------
def _status(tag_archive, open_writer) -> tuple[bool, str | None]:
    store = ArchiveStore(Archive(tag_archive.root), open_writer)
    try:
        store.wait_open(30)
        return run(store.open())
    finally:
        store.close()


def test_the_writer_can_tag(tag_archive):
    assert _status(tag_archive, tag_archive.open_writer) == (True, None)


def test_the_mirrors_copy_cannot_tag(tag_archive):
    (tag_archive.root / MIRROR).write_text("the mirror's copy\n", encoding="utf-8")
    ok, reason = _status(tag_archive, tag_archive.open_writer)
    assert not ok and "mirror" in reason


def test_an_archive_without_its_sentinel_cannot_tag(tag_archive):
    (tag_archive.root / SENTINEL).rename(tag_archive.root.parent / "sentinel-aside.json")
    ok, reason = _status(tag_archive, tag_archive.open_writer)
    assert not ok and SENTINEL in reason


def test_a_foreign_state_db_cannot_tag(tag_archive, tmp_path):
    init_archive(tmp_path / "other", tmp_path / "other-state", writer_id="test")

    def foreign() -> Writer:
        return Writer(
            tag_archive.root, State.open(tmp_path / "other-state"), Index(tag_archive.index),
            writer_id="test",
        )

    ok, reason = _status(tag_archive, foreign)
    assert not ok and "another archive" in reason


def test_an_unreadable_state_db_cannot_tag(tag_archive):
    for leftover in tag_archive.state.glob("state.db-*"):
        leftover.unlink()
    (tag_archive.state / "state.db").write_bytes(b"this is not a database\n" * 64)
    ok, reason = _status(tag_archive, tag_archive.open_writer)
    assert not ok and "restore state.db" in reason.lower()


def test_another_machines_writer_id_cannot_tag(tag_archive):
    ok, reason = _status(tag_archive, lambda: tag_archive.open_writer(writer_id="other"))
    assert not ok and "not the archive's writer" in reason


def test_a_transient_failure_is_retried_on_the_next_open(tag_archive):
    # why: spec §5. A busy moment at start must not disable tagging until a restart.
    calls = []

    def flaky() -> Writer:
        calls.append(1)
        if len(calls) == 1:
            raise LockTimeout("docs is held by another writer; gave up after 2 s")
        return tag_archive.open_writer()

    store = ArchiveStore(Archive(tag_archive.root), flaky)
    try:
        ok, reason = store.wait_open(30)
        assert not ok and "LockTimeout" in reason and "Refresh" in reason
        assert run(store.open()) == (True, None) and len(calls) == 2
    finally:
        store.close()


def test_a_stopped_writer_thread_turns_tagging_off_and_refuses_work(tag_archive):
    store = ArchiveStore(Archive(tag_archive.root), tag_archive.open_writer)
    store.wait_open(30)
    store.close()
    deadline = time.monotonic() + 10
    while store._thread.executor.alive() and time.monotonic() < deadline:
        time.sleep(0.01)
    assert run(store.open()) == (False, STOPPED)
    with pytest.raises(ThreadStopped):
        run(store.write(lambda: None))


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("exc", "found"),
    [
        (NotTheWriter("x"), Classified(STOP_NOT_WRITER, True)),
        (ThreadStopped("x"), Classified(STOP_THREAD, True)),
        (StaleFile("x"), Classified(STALE, False)),
        (InvalidFile("x"), Classified(INVALID, False)),
        (LockTimeout("x"), Classified(BUSY, False)),
        (ValueError("x"), None),
    ],
)
def test_the_archive_store_names_the_writers_refusals(tag_archive, exc, found):
    store = ArchiveStore(Archive(tag_archive.root), tag_archive.open_writer)
    try:
        assert store.classify(exc) == found
    finally:
        store.close()


def test_a_sqlite_error_is_busy_or_stops_the_batch(tag_archive):
    import sqlite3

    store = ArchiveStore(Archive(tag_archive.root), tag_archive.open_writer)
    try:
        busy = sqlite3.OperationalError("database is locked")
        busy.sqlite_errorname = "SQLITE_BUSY"
        assert store.classify(busy) == Classified(BUSY, False)
        assert store.classify(sqlite3.DatabaseError("x")) == Classified(STOP_STATE, True)
    finally:
        store.close()


# ---- the one-thread executor -----------------------------------------------------------------
def test_one_thread_runs_jobs_in_order_and_survives_a_failing_job():
    pool = OneThread("test-one-thread")
    try:
        seen: list[int] = []
        first = pool.submit(seen.append, 1)
        bad = pool.submit(lambda: 1 / 0)
        last = pool.submit(seen.append, 2)
        assert first.result(5) is None and last.result(5) is None and seen == [1, 2]
        with pytest.raises(ZeroDivisionError):
            bad.result(5)
        assert pool._thread.daemon and pool.alive()
    finally:
        pool.shutdown()
    assert not pool.alive()
    with pytest.raises(ThreadStopped):
        pool.submit(seen.append, 3)


def test_one_thread_finishes_what_was_queued_then_refuses_more():
    pool = OneThread("test-one-thread")
    gate = threading.Event()
    blocked = pool.submit(gate.wait, 10)
    queued = [pool.submit(lambda: "never") for _ in range(3)]
    pool.shutdown(wait=False)
    gate.set()
    assert blocked.result(5) is True
    assert all(f.result(5) == "never" for f in queued)  # queued before the stop: they still run
    with pytest.raises(ThreadStopped):
        pool.submit(lambda: "after")

```
