- [ ] **Step 8: Write the writer thread**

`packages/ui/src/recordings_ui/writing.py`:
```python
"""The process's one writer thread (tagging spec §5).

Why one thread owns the process's one Writer:
- Lock re-entrancy is per `Locks` instance *and* per thread, so two Writer instances nested on
  one thread deadlock until the timeout.
- A lock must never be held across an `await`.
- The event loop must never block on a file write.
- Every window's writes are serialised.

So every tag write runs here, one recording at a time, in order, and the event loop awaits it.
A second one-thread executor, the reader, serves the Tags page's snippets, so a snippet never
waits behind a write.

Both threads are daemons: the interpreter doesn't wait for them at exit, and a batch stops
between recordings (TagService.stop). Writes are atomic, so stopping mid-batch is safe.

Imported only by `ArchiveStore.__init__`: the Pages demo's Pyodide has no threads, sqlite3 or
fcntl (stage-2a decisions 6 and 7).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import logging
import queue
import sqlite3
import threading
from collections.abc import Callable
from concurrent.futures import Executor, Future
from typing import Any

from recordings.init import InitError
from recordings.locks import LockTimeout
from recordings.state import StateError
from recordings.writer import NotTheWriter, Writer

log = logging.getLogger(__name__)

OPENING = "the archive is being opened"
STOPPED = "the writer thread stopped: restart the app"
TRANSIENT = "the archive's index or state.db was busy ({}): press Refresh to try again"


class ThreadStopped(RuntimeError):
    """The thread has exited, so nothing more runs on it."""


class OneThread(Executor):
    """An executor with one daemon thread. Jobs run in the order they were submitted. A job's
    exception goes to its future, and the thread carries on. When it stops, anything still queued
    fails with ThreadStopped, and so does any later submit."""

    def __init__(self, name: str) -> None:
        self._jobs: queue.SimpleQueue = queue.SimpleQueue()
        self._closed = False
        self._lock = threading.Lock()
        self._thread = threading.Thread(target=self._run, name=name, daemon=True)
        self._thread.start()

    def submit(self, fn: Callable[..., Any], /, *args: Any, **kwargs: Any) -> Future:
        with self._lock:
            if self._closed or not self._thread.is_alive():
                raise ThreadStopped(STOPPED)
            future: Future = Future()
            self._jobs.put((future, fn, args, kwargs))
            return future

    def alive(self) -> bool:
        return self._thread.is_alive() and not self._closed

    def shutdown(self, wait: bool = True, *, cancel_futures: bool = False) -> None:
        with self._lock:
            if not self._closed:
                self._closed = True
                self._jobs.put(None)  # after every job already queued
        if wait:
            self._thread.join()

    def _run(self) -> None:
        try:
            while (job := self._jobs.get()) is not None:
                future, fn, args, kwargs = job
                if not future.set_running_or_notify_cancel():
                    continue
                try:
                    future.set_result(fn(*args, **kwargs))
                except BaseException as exc:  # noqa: BLE001  the job's failure goes to its caller
                    future.set_exception(exc)
        finally:
            with self._lock:
                self._closed = True
            self._fail_queued()

    def _fail_queued(self) -> None:
        while True:
            try:
                job = self._jobs.get_nowait()
            except queue.Empty:
                return
            if job is not None and job[0].set_running_or_notify_cancel():
                job[0].set_exception(ThreadStopped(STOPPED))


class WriterThread:
    """The process's one Writer, on its one thread. Opening it writes the archive's docs (§6.7),
    as `write_docs_at_startup` did; a failed open is tried again by `ensure_open`."""

    def __init__(self, open_writer: Callable[[], Writer]) -> None:
        self.executor = OneThread("recordings-writer")
        self._open_writer = open_writer
        self.writer: Writer | None = None
        self.reason: str | None = OPENING
        self.opened: Future = self.executor.submit(self.ensure_open)

    def ensure_open(self) -> bool:
        """On the thread. True once the Writer is open."""
        if self.writer is not None:
            return True
        try:
            self.writer = self._open_writer()
        except (NotTheWriter, StateError, InitError) as exc:
            # Written for display: they name paths and IDs, never a title (spec §5).
            self.reason = str(exc)
        except (LockTimeout, sqlite3.Error, OSError) as exc:
            self.reason = TRANSIENT.format(type(exc).__name__)
        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001  any other failure must leave the app serving
            self.reason = f"the archive couldn't be opened ({type(exc).__name__})"
        else:
            self.reason = None
            return True
        log.warning("not writing the archive's docs, and tagging is off: %s", self.reason)
        return False

    def status(self) -> tuple[bool, str | None]:
        if not self.executor.alive():
            return False, STOPPED
        if self.writer is None:
            return False, self.reason
        return True, None

    def close(self) -> None:
        self.executor.shutdown(wait=False)
```

- [ ] **Step 9: Write the stores**

`packages/ui/src/recordings_ui/tagging.py`:
```python
"""The tag service (tagging spec §5): one write path for tags, with two stores.

- `ArchiveStore`, for the real app and `make demo`, writes through the process's one `Writer`,
  owned by one thread (`recordings_ui.writing`). Its reads go straight to the archive, on the
  event loop: recording.json is replaced atomically, so a read needs no lock, and reads never
  queue behind writes.
- `MemoryStore`, for the Pages demo, keeps the demo's recordings in memory. It applies the same
  operations and validates their results as `mutate` does, but never serialises a model, never
  writes a file and starts no thread. `pages_store()` chooses it, and only under Pyodide.

This module must import on Pyodide (Python 3.12, no sqlite3, fcntl or threads): everything that
needs them is imported inside `ArchiveStore.__init__`, as `status.py` does.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import contextlib
import sys
from collections.abc import Callable
from typing import TYPE_CHECKING, NamedTuple, Protocol, TypeVar

from recordings.archive import Archive
from recordings.models import Recording
from recordings.tagops import Recorded

from recordings_ui import views

if TYPE_CHECKING:
    from recordings.writer import Writer

T = TypeVar("T")

# Fixed phrases (spec §8): why one recording failed, and why a batch stopped. Never exception
# text, which can carry a title.
GONE = "the recording is gone"
STALE = "edited outside the app: run `recordings reindex`"
INVALID = "the file doesn't validate"
BUSY = "busy: another job holds this recording; try again"
NOT_CONFIRMED = "not confirmed: would become visible"
STOP_NOT_WRITER = "Stopped: this app can't write the archive now"
STOP_FULL = "Stopped: the disk is full"
STOP_READ_ONLY = "Stopped: the disk is read-only"
STOP_IO = "Stopped: a disk error"
STOP_STATE = "Stopped: state.db can't be used"
STOP_BUSY = "Stopped: busy: other jobs hold these recordings; try again"
STOP_SHUTDOWN = "Stopped: the app is shutting down"
STOP_THREAD = "Stopped: the writer thread stopped; restart the app"
STOP_UNEXPECTED = "Stopped: an unexpected error (the server log has its class)"

LOCK_WAIT = 2.0  # seconds: the service's locks, not the Writer's default 30 (spec §5)


class Classified(NamedTuple):
    reason: str  # a fixed phrase
    stops: bool  # an archive-level error: the batch stops (spec §5)


def under_pyodide() -> bool:
    return sys.platform == "emscripten"


class Store(Protocol):
    """Where tags are kept, as the tag service sees it."""

    saves: bool  # False only on Pages, where the UI says "Demo: changes aren't saved"
    archive: Archive  # read-only: the library view and snippets

    def library(self) -> dict: ...

    def read(self, recording_id: str) -> Recording: ...

    def apply(self, recording_id: str, op: Recorded) -> Recording: ...

    async def write(self, fn: Callable[[], T]) -> T: ...

    async def read_off_loop(self, fn: Callable[[], T]) -> T: ...

    async def open(self) -> tuple[bool, str | None]: ...

    def classify(self, exc: BaseException) -> Classified | None: ...

    def close(self) -> None: ...


class MemoryStore:
    """The Pages demo's recordings, with this page's changes kept in memory (spec §5). It takes
    no lock at all, and a reload brings the demo back unchanged."""

    saves = False

    def __init__(self, archive: Archive) -> None:
        self.archive = archive
        self._changed: dict[str, Recording] = {}

    def changed(self) -> dict[str, Recording]:
        return dict(self._changed)

    def library(self) -> dict:
        recordings = [self._changed.get(r.id, r) for r in self.archive.iter_recordings()]
        return views.library_view_from(recordings, self.archive.problems)

    def read(self, recording_id: str) -> Recording:
        if recording_id in self._changed:
            return self._changed[recording_id]
        return self.archive.load(recording_id)

    def apply(self, recording_id: str, op: Recorded) -> Recording:
        rec = self.read(recording_id)
        new = op.apply(rec)
        if new == rec:
            return rec
        # As mutate does (spec §6.4): rev goes up, and the result must validate. model_dump and
        # model_validate only: never dump_json, whose output Pyodide's pydantic 2.10 gets wrong
        # (stage-2a decision 7).
        new = Recording.model_validate({**new.model_dump(by_alias=True), "rev": (rec.rev or 0) + 1})
        self._changed[recording_id] = new
        return new

    async def write(self, fn: Callable[[], T]) -> T:
        return fn()  # inline: Pyodide has no threads

    async def read_off_loop(self, fn: Callable[[], T]) -> T:
        return fn()

    async def open(self) -> tuple[bool, str | None]:
        return True, None

    def classify(self, exc: BaseException) -> Classified | None:
        return None  # nothing of its own: the service's common rules cover it

    def close(self) -> None:
        pass


def pages_store(archive: Archive) -> MemoryStore:
    """The Pages demo's store. Only under Pyodide: outside it the app never falls back to memory,
    so `make demo` and the real app can't silently stop saving (spec §5)."""
    if not under_pyodide():
        raise RuntimeError("the in-memory store is for the Pages demo, under Pyodide only")
    return MemoryStore(archive)


class ArchiveStore:
    """The real archive and `make demo`'s copy (spec §5)."""

    saves = True

    def __init__(self, archive: Archive, open_writer: Callable[[], Writer]) -> None:
        # Here, never at the top: these bring sqlite3, fcntl and threads, which the Pages demo's
        # Pyodide lacks (stage-2a decision 6; spec §5).
        import sqlite3

        from recordings.locks import LockTimeout
        from recordings.writer import InvalidFile, NotTheWriter, StaleFile

        from recordings_ui.writing import OneThread, ThreadStopped, WriterThread

        self.archive = archive
        self._sqlite_error = sqlite3.Error
        self._lock_timeout = LockTimeout
        self._invalid, self._not_the_writer, self._stale = InvalidFile, NotTheWriter, StaleFile
        self._thread_stopped = ThreadStopped
        self._thread = WriterThread(open_writer)
        self._reader = OneThread("recordings-reader")

    def library(self) -> dict:
        return views.library_view(self.archive)

    def read(self, recording_id: str) -> Recording:
        return self.archive.load(recording_id)

    def apply(self, recording_id: str, op: Recorded) -> Recording:
        """On the writer thread only: call it through `write`."""
        writer = self._thread.writer
        if writer is None:
            raise self._not_the_writer(self._thread.reason or "the archive isn't open")
        return writer.mutate(recording_id, op)

    async def write(self, fn: Callable[[], T]) -> T:
        return await asyncio.get_running_loop().run_in_executor(self._thread.executor, fn)

    async def read_off_loop(self, fn: Callable[[], T]) -> T:
        return await asyncio.get_running_loop().run_in_executor(self._reader, fn)

    async def open(self) -> tuple[bool, str | None]:
        """`can_tag` and, when it is false, why. A failed open is tried again here, so a busy
        moment at start doesn't disable tagging until a restart (spec §5)."""
        with contextlib.suppress(self._thread_stopped):
            await self.write(self._thread.ensure_open)
        return self._thread.status()

    def wait_open(self, timeout: float) -> tuple[bool, str | None]:
        """Wait for the open the thread starts with, then report (tests, and the docs check)."""
        self._thread.opened.result(timeout)
        return self._thread.status()

    def classify(self, exc: BaseException) -> Classified | None:
        """The Writer's own refusals, as fixed phrases (spec §8)."""
        if isinstance(exc, self._not_the_writer):
            return Classified(STOP_NOT_WRITER, True)
        if isinstance(exc, self._thread_stopped):
            return Classified(STOP_THREAD, True)
        if isinstance(exc, self._stale):
            return Classified(STALE, False)
        if isinstance(exc, self._invalid):
            return Classified(INVALID, False)
        if isinstance(exc, self._lock_timeout):
            return Classified(BUSY, False)
        if isinstance(exc, self._sqlite_error):
            busy = getattr(exc, "sqlite_errorname", "") in ("SQLITE_BUSY", "SQLITE_LOCKED")
            return Classified(BUSY, False) if busy else Classified(STOP_STATE, True)
        return None

    def close(self) -> None:
        self._thread.close()
        self._reader.shutdown(wait=False)
```

git commit -m "feat(ui): the tag service's two stores and the process's one writer thread

ArchiveStore writes through one Writer owned by one daemon thread (re-entrancy is per Locks
instance and thread; no lock across an await; no file write on the loop); a failed open is tried
again, and each can_tag reason reads as the Writer wrote it. MemoryStore keeps the Pages demo's
changes in memory: no file, no lock, no thread, no dump_json. The library view splits into row,
summarise and library_view_from, and rows carry duration_ms. Checked: concurrent.futures.Executor
(submit, set_running_or_notify_cancel), asyncio run_in_executor with a custom executor.

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>"

---
### Task 5: The tag service: checks, the confirmation, batches, Undo and deltas

**Checkpoint lens:** security and privacy (the confirmation, the gate, the logs).

`TagService` is one per process; `TagSession` is one per browser window (spec §5). A session checks a request on the event loop, with no I/O (`check`), then applies it as the session's extended task (`run`, wired in Task 7). Every test here drives the service directly, with an injected `send(reply)` that records each reply, and runs against **both stores**.

**Files:**
- Modify: `packages/ui/src/recordings_ui/tagging.py` (append the service)
- Test: `packages/ui/tests/test_tag_service.py` (new)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: the stores and phrases of Task 4; `recordings.tagops` (Task 2); `views.row`, `views.summarise` (Task 4).
- Produces, in **`recordings_ui.tagging`:**
  - constants `MAX_TAGS = 20`, `MAX_IDS = 20_000`, `UNDO_KEEP = 20`, `MAX_BUSY = 5`, `REQUEST_IDS_KEPT = 1_000`, `PREPASS_CHUNK = 200`
  - messages `UNREADABLE`, `NO_RECORDINGS`, `BAD_ID`, `NO_TAGS`, `TOO_MANY_IDS`, `TOO_MANY_TAGS`, `ALREADY_UNDONE`, `UNDO_GONE`
  - `def classify_common(exc: BaseException) -> Classified`
  - `@dataclass(frozen=True) class Job: request_id: str; action: str; ids: tuple[str, ...] = (); tags: tuple[str, ...] = (); undo: str | None = None; confirm_ids: frozenset[str] = frozenset()`
  - `@dataclass(frozen=True) class Refusal: request_id: str | None; message: str`
  - `Send = Callable[[dict], Awaitable[None]]`
  - `class TagService(store: Store, *, progress_every: float = 0.25)`: `.store`, `.stopping`, `session() -> TagSession`, `stop()`, `claim(request_id) -> bool`, `release(request_id)`
  - `class TagSession(service)`: `.rows: dict[str, dict]`; `async library() -> dict` (the view plus `can_tag`, `cannot_tag_reason`, `saves`); `check(raw: object) -> Job | Refusal` (never raises); `async run(job: Job | Refusal, send: Send) -> None` (never raises)
  - **The reply** (every key always present): `request_id`, `status` (`"done" | "confirm" | "failed" | "progress"`), `changed`, `unchanged`, `done_so_far`, `total`, `loses_private` (`{"visible", "untagged", "ids"}` or None), `now_visible`, `failures` (`[{"id", "reason"}]`), `undo` (token or None), `rows`, `counts`, `tags`, `note_types` (the last three None on a refusal or a `confirm`), `message` (None, a refusal or "Stopped: …")

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing service tests**

`packages/ui/tests/test_tag_service.py`:
```python
"""The tag service (tagging spec §5), driven directly with an injected send, against both
stores. The races are spec §9's: each puts another window's change between this request's check
and its write."""

import asyncio
import errno
import logging
import secrets

import pytest
from recordings.archive import Archive
from recordings.locks import Locks
from recordings.models import TagRef
from recordings.tagops import (
    CASE_VARIANT,
    LOOKS_PRIVATE,
    NOTE_TYPE,
    AddTags,
    Recorded,
    RemoveTags,
)
from recordings_ui.tagging import (
    ALREADY_UNDONE,
    BAD_ID,
    BUSY,
    GONE,
    INVALID,
    NO_RECORDINGS,
    NO_TAGS,
    NOT_CONFIRMED,
    STALE,
    STOP_BUSY,
    STOP_FULL,
    STOP_IO,
    STOP_SHUTDOWN,
    STOP_UNEXPECTED,
    TOO_MANY_IDS,
    TOO_MANY_TAGS,
    UNDO_GONE,
    UNREADABLE,
    ArchiveStore,
    MemoryStore,
    TagService,
    TagSession,
)
from recordings_ui.writing import STOPPED

GOOD_ID = "20200101T000000+0000_0000beef"  # the right shape; no such recording


def run(coro):
    return asyncio.run(coro)


@pytest.fixture(params=["archive", "memory"])
def store(request, tag_archive):
    if request.param == "archive":
        made = ArchiveStore(Archive(tag_archive.root), tag_archive.open_writer)
    else:
        made = MemoryStore(Archive(tag_archive.root))
    yield made
    made.close()


@pytest.fixture
def service(store) -> TagService:
    return TagService(store, progress_every=0)  # progress between every two recordings


class Replies(list):
    """Records each reply, as shinyreact's send_message would deliver it."""

    async def __call__(self, reply: dict) -> None:
        self.append(reply)

    @property
    def final(self) -> dict:
        return [r for r in self if r["status"] != "progress"][-1]


def session_of(service: TagService) -> TagSession:
    session = service.session()
    run(session.library())
    return session


def ask(session: TagSession, raw: object) -> Replies:
    replies = Replies()
    run(session.run(session.check(raw), replies))
    return replies


def req(action, ids=(), tags=(), *, undo=None, confirm=(), request_id=None) -> dict:
    return {
        "request_id": request_id or secrets.token_hex(8),
        "action": action,
        "ids": list(ids),
        "tags": list(tags),
        "undo": undo,
        "confirm_ids": list(confirm),
    }


def tags_of(store, rid: str) -> list[str]:
    return [t.tag for t in store.read(rid).tags]


def elsewhere(op) -> Recorded:
    return Recorded(op, "another-window")


def before_the_first_write(store, monkeypatch, other) -> None:
    """Run `other(real_apply)` once, just before this request's first write: another window's
    change landing between this request's check and its write."""
    real = store.apply
    pending = [other]

    def apply(rid, op):
        while pending:
            pending.pop()(real)
        return real(rid, op)

    monkeypatch.setattr(store, "apply", apply)


# ---- applying, and the view ------------------------------------------------------------------
def test_the_view_says_whether_tagging_works_and_saves(service):
    view = run(service.session().library())
    assert view["can_tag"] is True and view["cannot_tag_reason"] is None
    assert view["saves"] is isinstance(service.store, ArchiveStore)


def test_adding_applies_and_replies_with_the_changed_rows_and_counts(service, tag_archive):
    ids = tag_archive.ids
    session = session_of(service)
    reply = ask(session, req("add", [ids["untagged"], ids["plain"]], ["work"])).final
    assert reply["status"] == "done" and reply["changed"] == 2 and reply["unchanged"] == 0
    assert {r["id"] for r in reply["rows"]} == {ids["untagged"], ids["plain"]}
    assert reply["counts"] == {"all": 6, "untagged": 0}
    assert {"tag": "work", "count": 2, "private": False} in reply["tags"]
    assert reply["undo"] and reply["failures"] == [] and reply["message"] is None
    assert tags_of(service.store, ids["plain"]) == ["talks", "work"]


def test_a_tag_already_there_counts_as_unchanged(service, tag_archive):
    session = session_of(service)
    reply = ask(session, req("add", [tag_archive.ids["plain"]], ["talks"])).final
    assert reply["status"] == "done" and reply["changed"] == 0 and reply["unchanged"] == 1
    assert reply["undo"] is None and reply["rows"] == []


# ---- checks, before anything is applied ------------------------------------------------------
@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("raw", "message"),
    [
        (None, UNREADABLE),
        ("add", UNREADABLE),
        ({"request_id": "short", "action": "add", "ids": [GOOD_ID], "tags": ["x"]}, UNREADABLE),
        ({"request_id": "req0001abcd", "action": "clear", "ids": [GOOD_ID], "tags": ["x"]}, UNREADABLE),
        ({"request_id": "req0001abcd", "action": "add", "ids": GOOD_ID, "tags": ["x"]}, UNREADABLE),
        ({"request_id": "req0001abcd", "action": "add", "ids": [GOOD_ID], "tags": [1]}, UNREADABLE),
        ({"request_id": "req0001abcd", "action": "add", "ids": [], "tags": ["x"]}, NO_RECORDINGS),
        ({"request_id": "req0001abcd", "action": "add", "ids": ["../etc"], "tags": ["x"]}, BAD_ID),
        ({"request_id": "req0001abcd", "action": "remove", "ids": [GOOD_ID], "tags": []}, NO_TAGS),
        ({"request_id": "req0001abcd", "action": "undo", "undo": ""}, UNREADABLE),
    ],
)
def test_a_bad_request_is_refused_whole(service, tag_archive, raw, message):
    session = session_of(service)
    replies = ask(session, raw)
    assert [r["status"] for r in replies] == ["failed"]
    assert replies[0]["message"] == message
    assert replies[0]["rows"] == [] and replies[0]["undo"] is None
    assert tags_of(service.store, tag_archive.ids["plain"]) == ["talks"]


def test_the_caps_refuse_a_request_whole(service, tag_archive):
    session = session_of(service)
    many = [f"20200101T000000+0000_{i:08x}" for i in range(20_001)]
    assert ask(session, req("add", many, ["work"])).final["message"] == TOO_MANY_IDS
    rid = tag_archive.ids["untagged"]
    tags = [f"t{i}" for i in range(21)]
    assert ask(session, req("add", [rid], tags)).final["message"] == TOO_MANY_TAGS
    assert tags_of(service.store, rid) == []


def test_new_tags_follow_the_rules_and_existing_ones_are_taken_as_they_are(service, tag_archive):
    rid = tag_archive.ids["untagged"]
    session = session_of(service)
    assert ask(session, req("add", [rid], ["\u0440rivate"])).final["message"] == LOOKS_PRIVATE
    assert ask(session, req("add", [rid], ["Talks"])).final["message"] == CASE_VARIANT
    assert ask(session, req("add", [rid], ["notes/summary"])).final["message"] == NOTE_TYPE
    assert tags_of(service.store, rid) == []
    done = ask(session, req("add", [rid], ["notes/lecture", "talks"])).final
    assert done["status"] == "done" and tags_of(service.store, rid) == ["notes/lecture", "talks"]


# ---- the confirmation ------------------------------------------------------------------------
def test_removing_a_last_private_tag_asks_first_and_applies_nothing(service, tag_archive):
    ids = tag_archive.ids
    session = session_of(service)
    reply = ask(session, req("remove", [ids["private"], ids["journal"], ids["plain"]], ["private"]))
    final = reply.final
    assert final["status"] == "confirm" and final["total"] == 3
    assert final["loses_private"] == {"visible": 0, "untagged": 1, "ids": [ids["private"]]}
    assert final["rows"] == [] and final["undo"] is None
    assert tags_of(service.store, ids["private"]) == ["private"]
    assert tags_of(service.store, ids["journal"]) == ["private/journal", "private"]


def test_the_counts_split_visible_from_untagged(service, tag_archive):
    ids = tag_archive.ids
    session = session_of(service)
    ask(session, req("add", [ids["plain"]], ["private"]))
    final = ask(session, req("remove", [ids["plain"], ids["private"]], ["private"])).final
    assert final["loses_private"]["visible"] == 1  # plain keeps "talks"
    assert final["loses_private"]["untagged"] == 1  # private has nothing left


def test_nothing_is_applied_without_confirm_ids_even_for_a_hand_crafted_request(service, tag_archive):
    ids = tag_archive.ids
    session = session_of(service)
    crafted = req("remove", [ids["private"]], ["private"], confirm=[ids["plain"]])
    assert ask(session, crafted).final["status"] == "confirm"
    assert tags_of(service.store, ids["private"]) == ["private"]
    done = ask(session, req("remove", [ids["private"]], ["private"], confirm=[ids["private"]]))
    assert done.final["status"] == "done" and tags_of(service.store, ids["private"]) == []


# ---- the races (spec §9) ---------------------------------------------------------------------
def test_complementary_removals_in_two_windows_never_expose_a_recording(
    service, tag_archive, monkeypatch
):
    # why: each window's check sees another private tag left, so neither asks; the gate, under
    # the lock, refuses the second write.
    rid = tag_archive.ids["journal"]  # private/journal and private
    session = session_of(service)
    before_the_first_write(
        service.store, monkeypatch, lambda real: real(rid, elsewhere(RemoveTags(("private",))))
    )
    final = ask(session, req("remove", [rid], ["private/journal"])).final
    assert final["status"] == "done" and final["changed"] == 0
    assert final["failures"] == [{"id": rid, "reason": NOT_CONFIRMED}]
    assert tags_of(service.store, rid) == ["private/journal"]


def test_a_stale_window_still_asks_because_the_check_reads_the_files(service, tag_archive):
    rid = tag_archive.ids["plain"]
    session = session_of(service)  # its view: plain has only "talks"
    private = AddTags((TagRef(tag="private", by="you"),))
    run(service.store.write(lambda: service.store.apply(rid, elsewhere(private))))
    assert session.rows[rid]["tags"] == [{"tag": "talks", "by": "you"}]  # stale
    final = ask(session, req("remove", [rid], ["private"])).final
    assert final["status"] == "confirm" and final["loses_private"]["ids"] == [rid]
    assert final["loses_private"]["visible"] == 1
    assert tags_of(service.store, rid) == ["talks", "private"]


def test_a_set_that_grows_after_the_confirm_asks_again(service, tag_archive):
    ids = tag_archive.ids
    targets = [ids["private"], ids["journal"]]
    session = session_of(service)
    first = ask(session, req("remove", targets, ["private"])).final
    assert first["loses_private"]["ids"] == [ids["private"]]
    journal_only = RemoveTags(("private/journal",))
    run(service.store.write(lambda: service.store.apply(ids["journal"], elsewhere(journal_only))))
    again = ask(session, req("remove", targets, ["private"], confirm=[ids["private"]])).final
    assert again["status"] == "confirm"
    assert sorted(again["loses_private"]["ids"]) == sorted(targets)
    assert tags_of(service.store, ids["private"]) == ["private"]
    assert tags_of(service.store, ids["journal"]) == ["private"]


def test_an_add_that_lands_before_the_write_is_left_alone_by_undo(
    service, tag_archive, monkeypatch
):
    rid = tag_archive.ids["plain"]
    session = session_of(service)
    school = AddTags((TagRef(tag="school", by="you"),))
    before_the_first_write(service.store, monkeypatch, lambda real: real(rid, elsewhere(school)))
    added = ask(session, req("add", [rid], ["work"])).final
    assert tags_of(service.store, rid) == ["talks", "school", "work"]
    undone = ask(session, req("undo", undo=added["undo"])).final
    assert undone["status"] == "done" and tags_of(service.store, rid) == ["talks", "school"]


# ---- failures, stops and landings ------------------------------------------------------------
def test_a_broken_file_fails_alone_and_the_rest_are_applied_and_undoable(service, tag_archive):
    ids = tag_archive.ids
    session = session_of(service)
    broken = Archive(tag_archive.root).path_for(ids["noted"]) / "recording.json"
    broken.write_text("{", encoding="utf-8")  # a hand edit gone wrong (main spec §11)
    final = ask(session, req("add", [ids["untagged"], ids["noted"], ids["plain"]], ["work"])).final
    assert final["status"] == "done" and final["changed"] == 2
    assert final["failures"] == [{"id": ids["noted"], "reason": INVALID}]
    undone = ask(session, req("undo", undo=final["undo"])).final
    assert undone["changed"] == 2
    assert tags_of(service.store, ids["untagged"]) == []
    assert tags_of(service.store, ids["plain"]) == ["talks"]


def test_an_outside_edit_is_refused_with_its_reason(tag_archive):
    store = ArchiveStore(Archive(tag_archive.root), tag_archive.open_writer)
    service = TagService(store, progress_every=0)
    try:
        session = session_of(service)
        rid = tag_archive.ids["plain"]
        path = Archive(tag_archive.root).path_for(rid) / "recording.json"
        text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8").replace('"Week 0"', '"Edited by hand"')
        path.write_text(text, encoding="utf-8")  # an edit outside the app (main spec §6.4)
        final = ask(session, req("add", [rid, tag_archive.ids["untagged"]], ["work"])).final
        assert final["failures"] == [{"id": rid, "reason": STALE}] and final["changed"] == 1
    finally:
        store.close()


def test_a_recording_that_is_gone_fails_alone(service, tag_archive):
    session = session_of(service)
    final = ask(session, req("add", [GOOD_ID, tag_archive.ids["untagged"]], ["work"])).final
    assert final["failures"] == [{"id": GOOD_ID, "reason": GONE}] and final["changed"] == 1


def test_an_archive_level_error_stops_the_batch_with_rows_and_undo(
    service, tag_archive, monkeypatch
):
    ids = tag_archive.ids
    real = service.store.apply

    def apply(rid, op):
        if rid == ids["plain"]:
            raise OSError(errno.ENOSPC, "No space left on device: Week 0")
        return real(rid, op)

    monkeypatch.setattr(service.store, "apply", apply)
    session = session_of(service)
    final = ask(session, req("add", [ids["untagged"], ids["plain"], ids["excluded"]], ["work"])).final
    assert final["status"] == "failed" and final["message"] == STOP_FULL
    assert [r["id"] for r in final["rows"]] == [ids["untagged"]] and final["undo"]
    assert tags_of(service.store, ids["excluded"]) == ["talks"]  # never reached


def test_a_change_that_landed_before_an_error_counts_as_applied(service, tag_archive, monkeypatch):
    # why: spec §5. An fsync of the folder can fail after the rename; the change is on disk.
    rid = tag_archive.ids["untagged"]
    real = service.store.apply

    def apply(r, op):
        real(r, op)
        raise OSError(errno.EIO, "fsync of the folder failed")

    monkeypatch.setattr(service.store, "apply", apply)
    session = session_of(service)
    final = ask(session, req("add", [rid], ["work"])).final
    assert final["status"] == "failed" and final["message"] == STOP_IO
    assert final["changed"] == 1 and final["undo"] and final["rows"][0]["id"] == rid


def test_five_busy_recordings_in_a_row_stop_the_batch(tag_archive):
    store = ArchiveStore(
        Archive(tag_archive.root), lambda: tag_archive.open_writer(lock_timeout=0.05)
    )
    service = TagService(store, progress_every=0)
    ids = list(tag_archive.ids.values())
    try:
        session = session_of(service)
        with Locks(tag_archive.state / "locks").hold(*ids):  # another job holds them all
            final = ask(session, req("add", ids, ["work"])).final
        assert final["status"] == "failed" and final["message"] == STOP_BUSY
        assert [f["reason"] for f in final["failures"]] == [BUSY] * 5
    finally:
        store.close()


def test_an_unexpected_error_stops_the_batch(service, tag_archive, monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setattr(service.store, "apply", lambda rid, op: 1 / 0)
    session = session_of(service)
    final = ask(session, req("add", [tag_archive.ids["untagged"]], ["work"])).final
    assert final["status"] == "failed" and final["message"] == STOP_UNEXPECTED


def test_a_stop_at_shutdown_ends_the_batch_between_recordings(service, tag_archive, monkeypatch):
    ids = list(tag_archive.ids.values())
    real = service.store.apply

    def apply(rid, op):
        after = real(rid, op)
        service.stop()  # as the app's shutdown does, while a batch runs
        return after

    monkeypatch.setattr(service.store, "apply", apply)
    session = session_of(service)
    final = ask(session, req("add", ids, ["work"])).final
    assert final["status"] == "failed" and final["message"] == STOP_SHUTDOWN
    assert final["changed"] == 1 and final["undo"]
    assert sum("work" in tags_of(service.store, rid) for rid in ids) == 1


def test_a_request_after_the_writer_thread_stopped_fails_with_its_reason(tag_archive):
    store = ArchiveStore(Archive(tag_archive.root), tag_archive.open_writer)
    service = TagService(store, progress_every=0)
    session = session_of(service)
    store.close()
    final = ask(session, req("add", [tag_archive.ids["untagged"]], ["work"])).final
    assert final["status"] == "failed" and final["message"] == f"Stopped: {STOPPED}"


def test_a_send_that_fails_mid_batch_still_finishes_and_never_raises(service, tag_archive):
    # why: Review Focus 2. The window closes mid-batch: the batch finishes on the server, and a
    # reload shows what landed.
    ids = list(tag_archive.ids.values())
    session = session_of(service)

    async def closed(_reply):
        raise ConnectionError("the window closed")

    run(session.run(session.check(req("add", ids, ["work"])), closed))
    assert all("work" in tags_of(service.store, rid) for rid in ids)


def test_progress_is_sent_between_recordings(service, tag_archive):
    ids = list(tag_archive.ids.values())
    session = session_of(service)
    replies = ask(session, req("add", ids, ["work"]))
    progress = [r for r in replies if r["status"] == "progress"]
    assert [p["done_so_far"] for p in progress] == [1, 2, 3, 4, 5]
    assert {p["total"] for p in progress} == {6}
    assert replies[-1]["status"] == "done" and replies[-1]["done_so_far"] == 6


# ---- request IDs and Undo --------------------------------------------------------------------
def test_a_request_id_is_applied_at_most_once_and_a_gated_one_is_not_recorded(
    service, tag_archive
):
    ids = tag_archive.ids
    session = session_of(service)
    once = req("add", [ids["untagged"]], ["work"])
    assert ask(session, once).final["changed"] == 1
    assert ask(session, once) == []  # a repeat is ignored: no reply at all
    other_window = session_of(service)
    assert ask(other_window, once) == []  # kept per process, not per window
    gated = req("remove", [ids["private"]], ["private"])
    assert ask(session, gated).final["status"] == "confirm"
    assert ask(session, gated).final["status"] == "confirm"  # not recorded: asked again


def test_undo_is_single_use_and_an_unknown_token_says_so(service, tag_archive):
    rid = tag_archive.ids["untagged"]
    session = session_of(service)
    added = ask(session, req("add", [rid], ["work"])).final
    undone = ask(session, req("undo", undo=added["undo"])).final
    assert undone["status"] == "done" and tags_of(service.store, rid) == []
    assert undone["undo"]  # Redo (tagging plan, decision 7)
    again = ask(session, req("undo", undo=added["undo"])).final
    assert again["status"] == "failed" and again["message"] == ALREADY_UNDONE
    reloaded = session_of(service)
    assert ask(reloaded, req("undo", undo=undone["undo"])).final["message"] == UNDO_GONE
    redone = ask(session, req("undo", undo=undone["undo"])).final
    assert redone["status"] == "done" and tags_of(service.store, rid) == ["work"]


def test_a_gated_undo_keeps_its_token(service, tag_archive):
    rid = tag_archive.ids["untagged"]
    session = session_of(service)
    added = ask(session, req("add", [rid], ["private"])).final
    gated = ask(session, req("undo", undo=added["undo"])).final
    assert gated["status"] == "confirm"
    assert gated["loses_private"] == {"visible": 0, "untagged": 1, "ids": [rid]}
    done = ask(session, req("undo", undo=added["undo"], confirm=[rid])).final
    assert done["status"] == "done" and tags_of(service.store, rid) == []


def test_undo_tokens_survive_a_refresh(service, tag_archive):
    # why: Review Focus 3. Refresh rebuilds the view; only a reload ends the session's Undo.
    session = session_of(service)
    added = ask(session, req("add", [tag_archive.ids["untagged"]], ["work"])).final
    run(session.library())
    assert ask(session, req("undo", undo=added["undo"])).final["status"] == "done"


def test_the_session_keeps_only_the_last_twenty_actions(service, tag_archive):
    session = session_of(service)
    rid = tag_archive.ids["untagged"]
    tokens = [ask(session, req("add", [rid], [f"t{i}"])).final["undo"] for i in range(21)]
    assert ask(session, req("undo", undo=tokens[0])).final["message"] == UNDO_GONE
    assert ask(session, req("undo", undo=tokens[1])).final["status"] == "done"


def test_removing_a_note_type_records_the_exclusion_and_undo_reverses_it(service, tag_archive):
    rid = tag_archive.ids["noted"]
    session = session_of(service)
    removed = ask(session, req("remove", [rid], ["notes/lecture"])).final
    assert service.store.read(rid).excluded_note_types == ["lecture"]
    ask(session, req("undo", undo=removed["undo"]))
    rec = service.store.read(rid)
    assert [t.tag for t in rec.tags] == ["notes/lecture", "school/course-101"]
    assert rec.tags[0].by == "auto" and rec.excluded_note_types == []


# ---- deltas, now_visible and logs ------------------------------------------------------------
def test_deltas_patch_rows_without_rebuilding_the_view(service, tag_archive, monkeypatch):
    rid = tag_archive.ids["untagged"]
    session = session_of(service)
    monkeypatch.setattr(service.store, "library", lambda: pytest.fail("the view was rebuilt"))
    final = ask(session, req("add", [rid], ["Private/health"])).final
    (row,) = final["rows"]
    assert row["id"] == rid and row["private"] is True and row["untagged"] is False
    assert final["counts"] == {"all": 6, "untagged": 0}
    assert {"tag": "Private/health", "count": 1, "private": True} in final["tags"]
    assert {"note_type": "lecture", "count": 1} in final["note_types"]
    assert session.rows[rid] == row


@pytest.mark.parametrize(("tag", "visible"), [("work", 1), ("private", 0)])
def test_now_visible_counts_untagged_recordings_tagged_without_private(
    service, tag_archive, tag, visible
):
    ids = tag_archive.ids
    session = session_of(service)
    final = ask(session, req("add", [ids["untagged"], ids["plain"]], [tag])).final
    assert final["now_visible"] == visible


def test_logs_carry_no_tag_names_titles_or_exception_text(service, tag_archive, monkeypatch, caplog):
    ids = tag_archive.ids
    real = service.store.apply

    def apply(rid, op):
        if rid == ids["plain"]:
            raise RuntimeError("Week 0 is tagged private/ada")  # a title and a tag in its text
        return real(rid, op)

    monkeypatch.setattr(service.store, "apply", apply)
    session = session_of(service)
    with caplog.at_level(logging.DEBUG):
        ask(session, req("add", [ids["untagged"]], ["private/ada"]))
        ask(session, req("add", [ids["plain"]], ["private/ada"]))
        ask(session, req("add", [ids["plain"]], ["\u0440rivate"]))
    assert "private/ada" not in caplog.text and "Week" not in caplog.text
    assert "\u0440" not in caplog.text and "RuntimeError" in caplog.text
    assert ids["plain"] in caplog.text  # IDs are fine: they carry no title
```

In `packages/ui/src/recordings_ui/tagging.py`, merge these into the imports at the top:
```python
import errno
import logging
import re
import secrets
import time
from collections import OrderedDict
from collections.abc import Awaitable, Callable
from dataclasses import dataclass
from functools import partial

from pydantic import ValidationError
from recordings.archive import Archive
from recordings.ids import ID_RE
from recordings.models import Recording, TagRef, is_private
from recordings.tagops import (
    AddTags,
    Effect,
    Op,
    Recorded,
    RemoveTags,
    RestoreTags,
    WouldExpose,
    effect_of,
    removes_last_private,
    validate_new_tag,
)
```
and append:
```python
log = logging.getLogger(__name__)

MAX_TAGS = 20
MAX_IDS = 20_000  # a sanity cap (spec §5)
UNDO_KEEP = 20  # actions each session keeps for Undo
MAX_BUSY = 5  # consecutive busy recordings before a batch stops
REQUEST_IDS_KEPT = 1_000  # recent request IDs, per process
PREPASS_CHUNK = 200  # recordings per pre-pass job, so other windows' writes interleave
ACTIONS = ("add", "remove", "undo")
_REQUEST_ID = re.compile(r"[A-Za-z0-9_-]{8,64}")
_FULL = frozenset({errno.ENOSPC, getattr(errno, "EDQUOT", errno.ENOSPC)})

# A request refused whole, before anything is applied (spec §8).
UNREADABLE = "The request couldn't be read."
NO_RECORDINGS = "Choose at least one recording."
BAD_ID = "A recording ID isn't valid."
NO_TAGS = "Choose at least one tag."
TOO_MANY_IDS = f"At most {MAX_IDS:,} recordings at a time."
TOO_MANY_TAGS = f"At most {MAX_TAGS} tags at a time."
ALREADY_UNDONE = "already undone"
UNDO_GONE = "Undo is no longer available (the page was reloaded)"

Send = Callable[[dict], Awaitable[None]]


def classify_common(exc: BaseException) -> Classified:
    """The rules every store shares (spec §8), after the store's own. Anything unrecognised
    stops the batch (tagging plan, decision 14): a bug must not fail every recording in turn."""
    if isinstance(exc, WouldExpose):
        return Classified(NOT_CONFIRMED, False)
    if isinstance(exc, KeyError | FileNotFoundError):
        return Classified(GONE, False)
    if isinstance(exc, ValidationError | UnicodeDecodeError):
        return Classified(INVALID, False)
    if isinstance(exc, OSError):
        if exc.errno in _FULL:
            return Classified(STOP_FULL, True)
        if exc.errno == errno.EROFS:
            return Classified(STOP_READ_ONLY, True)
        return Classified(STOP_IO, True)
    return Classified(STOP_UNEXPECTED, True)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Job:
    """A request that passed the checks (spec §5)."""

    request_id: str
    action: str  # "add", "remove" or "undo"
    ids: tuple[str, ...] = ()
    tags: tuple[str, ...] = ()
    undo: str | None = None
    confirm_ids: frozenset[str] = frozenset()


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Refusal:
    """A request refused whole, before anything was applied (spec §8)."""

    request_id: str | None
    message: str


def _texts(value: object) -> bool:
    return isinstance(value, list) and all(isinstance(v, str) for v in value)


class TagService:
    """One per process: the store, the request IDs already handled, and shutdown (spec §5)."""

    def __init__(self, store: Store, *, progress_every: float = 0.25) -> None:
        self.store = store
        self.progress_every = progress_every  # seconds between progress replies (decision 10)
        self.stopping = False
        self._handled: OrderedDict[str, None] = OrderedDict()

    def session(self) -> TagSession:
        return TagSession(self)

    def stop(self) -> None:
        """At shutdown. A batch stops between recordings; writes are atomic, so that is safe."""
        self.stopping = True
        self.store.close()

    def claim(self, request_id: str) -> bool:
        """False for an ID already applied or failed: a repeat is ignored (spec §5)."""
        if request_id in self._handled:
            return False
        self._handled[request_id] = None
        while len(self._handled) > REQUEST_IDS_KEPT:
            self._handled.popitem(last=False)
        return True

    def release(self, request_id: str) -> None:
        """A gated request is not recorded (spec §5)."""
        self._handled.pop(request_id, None)


class TagSession:
    """One browser window: its view of the library and its Undo history (spec §5)."""

    def __init__(self, service: TagService) -> None:
        self.service = service
        self.store = service.store
        self.rows: dict[str, dict] = {}
        self._undo: OrderedDict[str, dict[str, Effect]] = OrderedDict()
        self._used: OrderedDict[str, None] = OrderedDict()

    # ---- the view ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    async def library(self) -> dict:
        """The full view, on load and on Refresh. A failed open is tried again here. Undo
        tokens survive it; only a reload, which makes a new session, ends them."""
        can_tag, reason = await self.store.open()
        view = self.store.library()
        self.rows = {r["id"]: r for r in view["recordings"]}
        return {**view, "can_tag": can_tag, "cannot_tag_reason": reason, "saves": self.store.saves}

    def _known_tags(self) -> set[str]:
        return {t["tag"] for r in self.rows.values() for t in r["tags"]}

    def _note_types(self) -> set[str]:
        return {t.removeprefix("notes/") for t in self._known_tags() if t.startswith("notes/")}

    # ---- checks, on the event loop, before anything is applied -----------------------------
    def check(self, raw: object) -> Job | Refusal:
        """Never raises: a request this can't read is refused whole."""
        try:
            return self._check(raw)
        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001  a malformed request must never close the session
            log.warning("tag request refused (%s)", type(exc).__name__)
            return Refusal(None, UNREADABLE)

    def _check(self, raw: object) -> Job | Refusal:
        if not isinstance(raw, dict):
            return Refusal(None, UNREADABLE)
        request_id = raw.get("request_id")
        if not isinstance(request_id, str) or not _REQUEST_ID.fullmatch(request_id):
            return Refusal(None, UNREADABLE)
        action, confirm = raw.get("action"), raw.get("confirm_ids") or []
        if action not in ACTIONS or not _texts(confirm):
            return Refusal(request_id, UNREADABLE)
        if action == "undo":
            token = raw.get("undo")
            if not isinstance(token, str) or not token:
                return Refusal(request_id, UNREADABLE)
            return Job(request_id, "undo", undo=token, confirm_ids=frozenset(confirm))
        ids, tags = raw.get("ids"), raw.get("tags")
        if not _texts(ids) or not _texts(tags):
            return Refusal(request_id, UNREADABLE)
        ids, tags = tuple(dict.fromkeys(ids)), tuple(dict.fromkeys(tags))
        if not ids:
            return Refusal(request_id, NO_RECORDINGS)
        if len(ids) > MAX_IDS:
            return Refusal(request_id, TOO_MANY_IDS)
        if not all(ID_RE.fullmatch(i) for i in ids):
            return Refusal(request_id, BAD_ID)
        if not tags:
            # No clear-all: an empty removal is refused, so a bug can never become one.
            return Refusal(request_id, NO_TAGS)
        if len(tags) > MAX_TAGS:
            return Refusal(request_id, TOO_MANY_TAGS)
        if action == "add":
            known, note_types = self._known_tags(), self._note_types()
            for tag in tags:
                if tag in known:
                    continue  # an existing tag is taken as it is, whatever its spelling
                problem = validate_new_tag(tag, note_types=note_types, existing=known)
                if problem is not None:
                    return Refusal(request_id, problem)
        return Job(request_id, action, ids, tags, confirm_ids=frozenset(confirm))

    # ---- running a request -----------------------------------------------------------------
    async def run(self, job: Job | Refusal, send: Send) -> None:
        """Apply one request and reply. It runs as the session's extended task, outside Shiny's
        reactive lock, and never raises: a tagging error never closes the session (spec §5)."""
        request_id = job.request_id
        try:
            if isinstance(job, Refusal):
                if request_id is not None:
                    self.service.claim(request_id)  # failed: recorded
                await send(self._reply(request_id, "failed", message=job.message))
                return
            if not self.service.claim(request_id):
                log.info("tag %s: a repeated request, ignored", request_id)
                return
            reply = await self._apply(job, send)
            if reply["status"] == "confirm":
                self.service.release(request_id)
            await send(reply)
        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001  the session must stay up (spec §5)
            log.warning("tag %s failed (%s)", request_id, type(exc).__name__)
            try:
                await send(self._reply(request_id, "failed", message=STOP_UNEXPECTED))
            except Exception as again:  # noqa: BLE001  the window has gone: nothing to tell
                log.info("tag %s: no reply sent (%s)", request_id, type(again).__name__)

    async def _apply(self, job: Job, send: Send) -> dict:
        log.info(
            "tag %s: %s, %d recording(s), %d tag(s)",
            job.request_id,
            job.action,
            len(job.ids),
            len(job.tags),
        )
        can_tag, reason = await self.store.open()  # a failed open is tried again (spec §5)
        if not can_tag:
            return self._reply(job.request_id, "failed", message=f"Stopped: {reason}")
        if job.action == "undo":
            effects = self._undo.get(job.undo or "")
            if effects is None:
                message = ALREADY_UNDONE if job.undo in self._used else UNDO_GONE
                return self._reply(job.request_id, "failed", message=message)
            targets = tuple(effects)

            def make(rid: str, expose: frozenset[str]) -> Op:
                return RestoreTags(effects[rid], expose)

        elif job.action == "add":
            refs = tuple(TagRef(tag=t, by="you") for t in job.tags)
            targets = job.ids

            def make(rid: str, expose: frozenset[str]) -> Op:
                return AddTags(refs)

        else:
            targets = job.ids

            def make(rid: str, expose: frozenset[str]) -> Op:
                return RemoveTags(job.tags, expose)

        if job.action != "add":  # only a removal can expose a recording
            losing = await self._prepass(targets, make)
            if not set(losing) <= job.confirm_ids:
                visible = sum(losing.values())
                return self._reply(
                    job.request_id,
                    "confirm",
                    total=len(targets),
                    loses_private={
                        "visible": visible,
                        "untagged": len(losing) - visible,
                        "ids": list(losing),
                    },
                )
        reply = await self._batch(job, targets, make, send)
        if job.action == "undo" and job.undo is not None:
            self._undo.pop(job.undo, None)  # used up once applied, never when gated
            self._used[job.undo] = None
            while len(self._used) > REQUEST_IDS_KEPT:
                self._used.popitem(last=False)
        return reply

    async def _prepass(
        self, targets: tuple[str, ...], make: Callable[[str, frozenset[str]], Op]
    ) -> dict[str, bool]:
        """The recordings this request would leave with no private tag: True for those that keep
        other tags (visible), False for those left with none (untagged). It reads the files, on
        the writer thread, and only counts: the operation's own gate decides, under the lock,
        on the state it writes (spec §5)."""
        losing: dict[str, bool] = {}
        for start in range(0, len(targets), PREPASS_CHUNK):
            chunk = targets[start : start + PREPASS_CHUNK]
            losing |= await self.store.write(partial(self._would_lose, chunk, make))
        return losing

    def _would_lose(
        self, chunk: tuple[str, ...], make: Callable[[str, frozenset[str]], Op]
    ) -> dict[str, bool]:
        found: dict[str, bool] = {}
        for rid in chunk:
            try:
                before = self.store.read(rid)
                after = make(rid, frozenset({rid})).apply(before)
            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001  gone or unreadable: the batch reports it
                continue
            if removes_last_private(before, after):
                found[rid] = bool(after.tags)
        return found

    async def _batch(
        self,
        job: Job,
        targets: tuple[str, ...],
        make: Callable[[str, frozenset[str]], Op],
        send: Send,
    ) -> dict:
        """Recording by recording, not all-or-nothing: each gets its own write, wrapped for every
        exception (spec §5)."""
        effects: dict[str, Effect] = {}
        afters: dict[str, Recording] = {}
        failures: list[dict] = []
        unchanged = now_visible = busy = done = 0
        stopped: str | None = None
        last = time.monotonic()
        for rid in targets:
            if self.service.stopping:
                stopped = STOP_SHUTDOWN
                break
            op = Recorded(make(rid, job.confirm_ids), job.request_id)
            error: Exception | None = None
            after: Recording | None
            try:
                after = await self.store.write(partial(self.store.apply, rid, op))
            except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001  each recording is wrapped (spec §5)
                error, after = exc, self._landed(rid, op)
                log.warning(
                    "tag %s: %s failed on %s (%s)",
                    job.request_id,
                    job.action,
                    rid,
                    type(exc).__name__,
                )
            done += 1
            if after is not None and op.before is not None:
                busy = 0
                effect = effect_of(op.before, after)
                if effect.empty:
                    unchanged += 1
                else:
                    effects[rid], afters[rid] = effect, after
                    if not op.before.tags and after.tags and not is_private(after):
                        now_visible += 1
            if error is not None:
                found = self.store.classify(error) or classify_common(error)
                if found.stops:
                    stopped = found.reason
                    break
                if after is None:
                    failures.append({"id": rid, "reason": found.reason})
                    busy = busy + 1 if found.reason == BUSY else 0
                    if busy >= MAX_BUSY:
                        stopped = STOP_BUSY
                        break
            if done < len(targets) and time.monotonic() - last >= self.service.progress_every:
                last = time.monotonic()
                progress = self._reply(
                    job.request_id, "progress", done_so_far=done, total=len(targets)
                )
                await self._quietly(send, progress)
        token = None
        if effects:
            token = secrets.token_urlsafe(16)
            self._undo[token] = effects
            while len(self._undo) > UNDO_KEEP:
                self._undo.popitem(last=False)
        rows = []
        for rid, rec in afters.items():
            self.rows[rid] = views.row(rec)
            rows.append(self.rows[rid])
        return self._reply(
            job.request_id,
            "failed" if stopped else "done",
            changed=len(effects),
            unchanged=unchanged,
            done_so_far=done,
            total=len(targets),
            now_visible=now_visible,
            failures=failures,
            undo=token,
            rows=rows,
            message=stopped,
            **views.summarise(self.rows.values()),
        )

    def _landed(self, rid: str, op: Recorded) -> Recording | None:
        """After an exception, read the file again. A change can land and then fail, when an
        fsync of the folder fails after the rename, and then it counts as applied (spec §5)."""
        if op.before is None or op.after is None or op.after == op.before:
            return None
        try:
            now = self.store.read(rid)
        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001  unreadable now: not counted as applied
            return None
        same = now.tags == op.after.tags
        return now if same and now.excluded_note_types == op.after.excluded_note_types else None

    @staticmethod
    async def _quietly(send: Send, reply: dict) -> None:
        """A progress reply that can't be sent (the window closed) never stops the batch: it
        finishes on the server, and a reload shows what landed (Review Focus 2)."""
        try:
            await send(reply)
        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
            log.info("progress not sent (%s)", type(exc).__name__)

    @staticmethod
    def _reply(request_id: str | None, status: str, **fields: object) -> dict:
        reply: dict = {
            "request_id": request_id,
            "status": status,
            "changed": 0,
            "unchanged": 0,
            "done_so_far": 0,
            "total": 0,
            "loses_private": None,
            "now_visible": 0,
            "failures": [],
            "undo": None,
            "rows": [],
            "counts": None,
            "tags": None,
            "note_types": None,
            "message": None,
        }
        reply.update(fields)
        return reply
```

```bash
git add packages/ui/src/recordings_ui/tagging.py packages/ui/tests/test_tag_service.py
git commit -m "feat(ui): the tag service: checks, the privacy confirmation, batches, Undo and deltas

A request is checked on the event loop and applied recording by recording, each wrapped for every
exception, with fixed reasons and an archive-level stop. Removals pass a pre-pass that counts
recordings losing their last private tag; the operation's gate decides under the lock. Request
IDs apply at most once; Undo tokens are single use, the last 20 kept. Replies carry patched rows
and recounted counts. Logs carry IDs, counts and class names only. Both stores run every test,
spec §9's four races included. Checked: shiny 1.8.0 reactive/_extended_task.py (outside the
reactive lock, invocations queue).

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>"
```

### Task 6: The process: one Writer on its thread, demo mode's own Writer, Pages in memory

**Checkpoint lens:** security and privacy (demo isolation, and Pages never pretending to save).

`create_app` now builds the process's tag service. **The writer thread's `Writer.open(cfg)` replaces `write_docs_at_startup`,** so the process holds one `Writer` instance, and the archive's docs are still written at start, only into an archive this process may write (§6.7). Demo mode adopts its temporary copy, with its state, index and locks beside it (spec §5, "Demo mode"). The Pages entry chooses `MemoryStore` through `pages_store`.

- Modify: `packages/ui/src/recordings_ui/runtime.py` (`configure(tagging=)`, `tagging()`)

- Test: `packages/ui/tests/test_app.py`, `packages/ui/tests/test_runtime.py`, `packages/ui/tests/test_build_pages.py`, `packages/ui/tests/test_demo_guard.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `ArchiveStore`, `TagService`, `LOCK_WAIT`, `pages_store`, `MemoryStore` (Tasks 4, 5); `Writer.open(cfg, lock_timeout=)` (Task 3).

  - `create_app(settings)` configures `runtime` with `TagService(ArchiveStore(...))`, never `MemoryStore`; the FastAPI lifespan calls `tagging.stop()` at shutdown

In `packages/ui/tests/test_demo_guard.py`, the hook must read SQLite's URI form, which `State` uses (`file:///…/state.db?mode=rw`). Add `from urllib.parse import unquote, urlsplit` and replace `_hook`:
```python
def _path(value: str | bytes | os.PathLike) -> str:
    path = os.fsdecode(value)
    if path.startswith("file:"):  # State opens state.db by URI, so mode=rw never creates it
        path = unquote(urlsplit(path).path)
    return path


def _hook(event: str, args: tuple) -> None:
    if _events is None:
        return
    if event in PATH_EVENTS:
        if args and isinstance(args[0], (str, bytes, os.PathLike)):
            _events.append((event, _path(args[0])))
    elif event in ANY_EVENTS:
        _events.append((event, repr(args[:2])))
```

```python
def configure(
    archive: Archive | None,
    *,
    media_base: str | None = None,
    status: StatusSettings | None = None,
    tagging: TagService | None = None,
) -> None:
    """media_base: where the browser finds media files, as `<media_base><media.file>`.

    None (the server) links the FastAPI route /media/{id}. The static Pages demo, which has no
    FastAPI, sets "../media/" (spec §17.1). status: where Status reads its records; None in demo
    mode, which has no state folder. tagging: the process's tag service (tagging spec §5): the
    archive store from create_app, or the memory store from the Pages entry.
    """
    global _archive, _media_base, _status, _tagging
    _archive = archive
    _media_base = media_base
    _status = status
    _tagging = tagging
```

def tagging() -> TagService:
    if _tagging is None:
        raise RuntimeError("recordings_ui.runtime.configure() was not given a tag service")
    return _tagging

- [ ] **Step 4: Build the tag service in `create_app`**

Replace the top of `packages/ui/src/recordings_ui/app.py`, from the imports through `create_app`'s first lines, with:
```python
"""FastAPI app: media streaming and health here, the Shiny app mounted at "/" (spec §13)."""

from __future__ import annotations

import logging
import mimetypes
from collections.abc import AsyncIterator, Callable
from contextlib import asynccontextmanager
from pathlib import Path

from fastapi import FastAPI, HTTPException
from fastapi.responses import FileResponse
from recordings.archive import Archive
from recordings.ids import ID_RE
from recordings.index import Index
from recordings.init import InitError, adopt_archive
from recordings.state import State
from recordings.writer import NotTheWriter, Writer

from recordings_ui import runtime
from recordings_ui.hosts import HostGuard, WriteGuard
from recordings_ui.settings import Settings
from recordings_ui.status import status_settings
from recordings_ui.tagging import LOCK_WAIT, ArchiveStore, TagService

log = logging.getLogger(__name__)

NO_CONFIG = "there's no config.toml, so this app only reads the archive"


def demo_writer(copy: Path) -> Callable[[], Writer]:
    """Demo mode's Writer (tagging spec §5). Its state.db, index and locks live in the demo's
    temporary folder, beside the copy, and go with it at exit, SIGTERM included (§17). It never
    reads the config, RECORDINGS_STATE or RECORDINGS_INDEX. Called from create_app, never from
    settings.from_env, which the Pages demo also runs."""
    folder = copy.parent
    state = folder / "state"
    refused: str | None = None
    if not (state / "state.db").exists():  # tagging plan, decision 13
        try:
            adopt_archive(copy, state, writer_id="demo")
        except InitError as exc:  # the mirror's marker, or no sentinel: tagging is off
            refused = str(exc)

    def open_writer() -> Writer:
        if refused is not None:
            raise NotTheWriter(refused)
        writer = Writer(
            copy,
            State.open(state),
            Index(folder / "index.db"),
            writer_id="demo",
            lock_timeout=LOCK_WAIT,
        )
        writer.write_docs()  # §6.7, as Writer.open does for the real archive
        return writer

    return open_writer


def writer_opener(settings: Settings) -> Callable[[], Writer]:
    """How the writer thread opens the process's one Writer. It replaces write_docs_at_startup:
    opening writes the archive's docs (§6.7), and only into an archive this process may write;
    anywhere else the app still serves, read-only, with the reason as `cannot_tag_reason`."""
    if settings.demo:
        return demo_writer(settings.archive)
    cfg = settings.config
    if cfg is None:

        def refuse() -> Writer:
            raise NotTheWriter(NO_CONFIG)

        return refuse
    return lambda: Writer.open(cfg, lock_timeout=LOCK_WAIT)


def create_app(settings: Settings) -> HostGuard:
    archive = Archive(settings.archive)
    tagging = TagService(ArchiveStore(archive, writer_opener(settings)))
    runtime.configure(
        archive,
        status=status_settings(settings.config) if settings.config else None,
        tagging=tagging,
    )
    from recordings_ui.shiny_app import app as shiny_app  # after configure()

    @asynccontextmanager
    async def lifespan(_api: FastAPI) -> AsyncIterator[None]:
        yield
        tagging.stop()  # a batch stops between recordings; every write is atomic

    api = FastAPI(
        title="recordings", docs_url=None, redoc_url=None, openapi_url=None, lifespan=lifespan
    )
```
Delete `write_docs_at_startup`, and the imports it alone used (`sqlite3`, `Config`, `LockTimeout`, `WriterError`). The rest of `create_app` (from `@api.get("/healthz")` on) is unchanged.

Check FastAPI's `lifespan=` in the installed version before relying on it: `uv run python -c "import inspect, fastapi; print('lifespan' in inspect.signature(fastapi.FastAPI).parameters)"` must print `True`. `HostGuard` already passes the lifespan scope through (`hosts.py`, "lifespan").

### Task 7: Shiny wiring: `tag_request`, `refresh`, `snippet_request`, and replies by message

**Checkpoint lens:** security and privacy (a request only from an input on the websocket; nothing from a URL; the session never closes on an error).

`shiny_app.py` gains three event inputs and two message ids (spec §5):
- **`tag_request`** → the effect checks and enqueues (`TagSession.check`), then returns; the work runs as a per-session `reactive.extended_task` (`TagSession.run`), outside Shiny's process-wide reactive lock, and replies through `shinyreact.send_message(session, "tag_result", …)`.
- **`refresh`** → bumps a server-side counter that `library()` depends on, alone (decision 8).
- **`snippet_request`** → a second per-session extended task reads snippets on the reader thread, never the writer thread, and replies as `"snippets"`.

`library()` becomes async: it waits for the writer's first open, and retries a failed one, on load and on Refresh.

The extended task sets no reactive value: it only replies by message. If a later change needs one, set it inside `async with reactive.lock():` followed by `await reactive.flush()` (spec §5). Shiny's reconnect stays off: nothing calls `allow_reconnect`.

Checked before writing (CLAUDE.md): shiny 1.8.0's `reactive/_extended_task.py` (outside the reactive lock; `DenialContext` refuses reactive reads, so the task is given everything as arguments; a second `invoke` queues), `reactive/_reactives.py` `event` (an unset input is a silent exception; `ignore_none` skips `None`), `session/_session.py` `send_custom_message` (sent at once), and shinyreact's `_send_message.py` (`send_message(session, id, data)` resolves `id` in the session's namespace). The test calls `shinyreact.send_message` through the module, so a monkeypatch reaches it.

**Files:**
- Modify: `packages/ui/src/recordings_ui/views.py` (`plain_text`, `snippet`, `SNIPPET_CHARS`)
- Modify: `packages/ui/src/recordings_ui/tagging.py` (`SnippetJob`, `check_snippets`, `snippets`, `MAX_SNIPPET_IDS`)
- Modify: `packages/ui/src/recordings_ui/shiny_app.py`
- Test: `packages/ui/tests/test_views.py`, `packages/ui/tests/test_shiny_server.py`, `packages/ui/tests/test_tag_sessions.py` (new), `packages/ui/tests/test_demo_guard.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `TagSession.check/run/library` (Task 5), `runtime.tagging()` (Task 6), `Store.read_off_loop` (Task 4).
- Produces:
  - `views.SNIPPET_CHARS = 160`; `views.plain_text(markdown: str) -> str`; `views.snippet(archive: Archive, rid: str, *, limit: int = 160) -> str | None`
  - `tagging.MAX_SNIPPET_IDS = 200`; `tagging.TOO_MANY_SNIPPETS`; `@dataclass(frozen=True) class SnippetJob: request_id: str; ids: tuple[str, ...]`
  - `TagSession.check_snippets(raw: object) -> SnippetJob | Refusal`; `async TagSession.snippets(job, send) -> None`
  - **The wire:** inputs `tag_request` (the request of spec §5), `refresh` (a counter, `null` at mount), `snippet_request` (`{"request_id", "ids"}`); messages `"tag_result"` (the reply of Task 5) and `"snippets"` (`{"request_id", "snippets": {id: text | null}, "message": str | null}`)

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the snippets**

In `packages/ui/src/recordings_ui/views.py`, add `import re` and `from pathlib import Path` to the imports, and append:
```python
SNIPPET_CHARS = 160  # spec §5
_STAMP = re.compile(r"-(\d{8}T\d{6}Z)\.json$")  # rendition_filename's UTC stamp


def _stamp(name: str) -> tuple[str, str]:
    found = _STAMP.search(name)
    return (found.group(1) if found else "", name)


def plain_text(markdown: str) -> str:
    """Markdown as plain words, for a snippet: the text of each inline run, and nothing else.
    The browser draws snippets as text nodes, never as HTML (spec §5)."""
    words: list[str] = []
    for token in _md.parse(markdown):
        for child in token.children or []:
            if child.type in ("text", "code_inline"):
                words.append(child.content)
            elif child.type in ("softbreak", "hardbreak"):
                words.append(" ")
        words.append(" ")
    return " ".join("".join(words).split())


def _clip(text: str, limit: int) -> str:
    text = " ".join(text.split())
    return text if len(text) <= limit else text[: limit - 1].rstrip() + "…"


def _transcript_text(path: Path, limit: int) -> str:
    try:
        rendition = Rendition.model_validate_json(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    except (ValueError, OSError):
        return ""
    out: list[str] = []
    size = 0
    for segment in rendition.payload.get("segments", []):
        out.append(str(segment.get("text", "")))
        size += len(out[-1]) + 1
        if size > limit:
            break
    return " ".join(out)


def _notes_text(path: Path) -> str:
    try:
        rendition = Rendition.model_validate_json(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    except (ValueError, OSError):
        return ""
    if rendition.kind != "notes" or rendition.engine != "plaud":
        return ""
    return plain_text(str(rendition.payload.get("markdown", "")))


def snippet(archive: Archive, rid: str, *, limit: int = SNIPPET_CHARS) -> str | None:
    """A recording's first words, as plain text (spec §5): from its chosen transcript (or the
    newest), or else its first Plaud note. It reads that one file (tagging plan, decision 11)
    and stops at `limit` characters. None: nothing to show yet."""
    try:
        rec = archive.load(rid)
        folder = archive.path_for(rid) / "renditions"
        names = sorted(p.name for p in folder.glob("*.json"))
    except (KeyError, ValueError, OSError):
        return None
    transcripts = [n for n in names if n.startswith("transcript-")]
    chosen = (rec.chosen.transcript or "").removeprefix("renditions/")
    pick = chosen if chosen in transcripts else max(transcripts, key=_stamp, default=None)
    text = _transcript_text(folder / pick, limit) if pick else ""
    if not text:
        notes = sorted((n for n in names if n.startswith("notes-plaud-")), key=_stamp)
        text = _notes_text(folder / notes[0]) if notes else ""
    return _clip(text, limit) if text else None
```

import time

import shinyreact
from recordings.models import TagRef
from recordings.tagops import AddTags, Recorded
from recordings_ui.tagging import TOO_MANY_SNIPPETS, MemoryStore, TagService


@pytest.fixture(autouse=True)
def _archive(request, demo_archive):
    # A test can pass a media base with @pytest.mark.parametrize("_archive", [...], indirect=True).
    archive = Archive(demo_archive)
    service = TagService(MemoryStore(archive), progress_every=0)

    yield service
    runtime.configure(None)


class Sent(list):
    """Records what shinyreact.send_message would send: (message id, data)."""

    async def __call__(self, session, message_id, data):
        self.append((message_id, data))


@pytest.fixture
def sent(monkeypatch) -> Sent:
    recorder = Sent()
    monkeypatch.setattr(shinyreact, "send_message", recorder)
    return recorder


def wait_for(local_server, sent: Sent, message_id: str, request_id: str) -> dict:
    deadline = time.monotonic() + 10
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        found = [
            d
            for i, d in sent
            if i == message_id and d["request_id"] == request_id and d.get("status") != "progress"
        ]
        if found:
            return found[-1]
        local_server.flush()  # runs the loop, where the extended task does its work
        time.sleep(0.02)
    raise AssertionError(f"no {message_id} reply to {request_id}: {sent}")


def tag_request(request_id: str, rid: str) -> dict:

            "confirm_ids": []}

    view = local_server.get_output("library").value
    assert view["can_tag"] is True and view["saves"] is False  # the memory store


def test_a_tag_request_is_applied_and_answered_by_message(local_server, sent, demo_ids):
    rid = demo_ids["apollo13-problem"]
    local_server.set_inputs(tag_request=None)  # the mount default: skipped (ignore_none)

    assert reply["rows"][0]["tags"] == [{"tag": "talks", "by": "you"}]

def test_a_first_request_that_coalesced_with_the_mount_default_still_runs(
    local_server, sent, demo_ids
):

def test_too_many_snippet_ids_are_refused(local_server, sent):

`packages/ui/tests/test_tag_sessions.py`:
```python
"""Two windows on one event loop (tagging spec §9, "The writer thread"): a batch blocked on the
writer thread never holds up another window's reactive work."""

import asyncio
import threading
from pathlib import Path

import pytest
import shinyreact
from recordings.archive import Archive
from recordings_ui import runtime
from recordings_ui.tagging import ArchiveStore, TagService
from shiny.testserver import test_server_async

SHINY_APP = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "src" / "recordings_ui" / "shiny_app.py"


@pytest.fixture
def anyio_backend():
    return "asyncio"


class BlockingStore(ArchiveStore):
    """Its writes wait for `release`, as one recording of a long batch would."""

    def __init__(self, *args, **kwargs) -> None:
        super().__init__(*args, **kwargs)
        self.entered = threading.Event()
        self.release = threading.Event()

    def apply(self, recording_id, op):
        self.entered.set()
        self.release.wait(30)
        return super().apply(recording_id, op)


@pytest.mark.anyio
async def test_a_blocked_batch_in_one_window_never_holds_up_another(tag_archive, monkeypatch):
    store = BlockingStore(Archive(tag_archive.root), tag_archive.open_writer)
    archive = Archive(tag_archive.root)
    runtime.configure(archive, tagging=TagService(store, progress_every=0))
    sent: list[tuple[str, dict]] = []

    async def record(session, message_id, data):
        sent.append((message_id, data))

    monkeypatch.setattr(shinyreact, "send_message", record)
    request = {
        "request_id": "block0001test",
        "action": "add",
        "ids": [tag_archive.ids["untagged"]],
        "tags": ["work"],
        "confirm_ids": [],
    }
    try:
        async with (
            test_server_async(SHINY_APP, timeout_secs=10) as a,
            test_server_async(SHINY_APP, timeout_secs=10) as b,
        ):
            await a.set_inputs(tag_request=request)
            assert await asyncio.to_thread(store.entered.wait, 10)  # A's batch is on the thread
            rid = tag_archive.ids["plain"]
            await b.set_inputs(selected_id=rid)
            assert b.get_output("recording").value["id"] == rid  # B is not held up
            store.release.set()
            for _ in range(500):
                if any(d.get("status") == "done" for _, d in sent):
                    break
                await asyncio.sleep(0.02)
            assert [d["status"] for _, d in sent][-1] == "done"
    finally:
        store.release.set()
        store.close()
        runtime.configure(None)
```
