In `packages/ui/src/recordings_ui/tagging.py`, append:
```python
MAX_SNIPPET_IDS = 200  # spec §5
TOO_MANY_SNIPPETS = f"At most {MAX_SNIPPET_IDS} snippets at a time."


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SnippetJob:
    request_id: str
    ids: tuple[str, ...]


def _snippets(store: Store, ids: tuple[str, ...]) -> dict[str, str | None]:
    return {rid: views.snippet(store.archive, rid) for rid in ids}


def _check_snippets(raw: object) -> SnippetJob | Refusal:
    if not isinstance(raw, dict):
        return Refusal(None, UNREADABLE)
    request_id, ids = raw.get("request_id"), raw.get("ids")
    if not isinstance(request_id, str) or not _REQUEST_ID.fullmatch(request_id):
        return Refusal(None, UNREADABLE)
    if not _texts(ids):
        return Refusal(request_id, UNREADABLE)
    ids = tuple(dict.fromkeys(ids))
    if len(ids) > MAX_SNIPPET_IDS:
        return Refusal(request_id, TOO_MANY_SNIPPETS)
    if not all(ID_RE.fullmatch(i) for i in ids):
        return Refusal(request_id, BAD_ID)
    return SnippetJob(request_id, ids)
```
and add these two methods to `TagSession`:
```python
    def check_snippets(self, raw: object) -> SnippetJob | Refusal:
        """Never raises (spec §5)."""
        try:
            return _check_snippets(raw)
        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001  a malformed request must never close the session
            log.warning("snippet request refused (%s)", type(exc).__name__)
            return Refusal(None, UNREADABLE)

    async def snippets(self, job: SnippetJob | Refusal, send: Send) -> None:
        """Reads on the snippet reader, never the writer thread. The session's extended task
        runs one request at a time, queueing the next (spec §5)."""
        try:
            if isinstance(job, Refusal):
                await send({"request_id": job.request_id, "snippets": {}, "message": job.message})
                return
            texts = await self.store.read_off_loop(partial(_snippets, self.store, job.ids))
            await send({"request_id": job.request_id, "snippets": texts, "message": None})
        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001  the session must stay up
            log.warning("snippets %s failed (%s)", job.request_id, type(exc).__name__)
```

- [ ] **Step 8: Wire the inputs and messages**

Replace `packages/ui/src/recordings_ui/shiny_app.py` with:
```python
"""Shiny server for the React client. Server holds only reactive computation and returns JSON
(shinyreact-build-app skill). ReactApp discovers www/ui.js + www/ui.css next to THIS file, and
reads the immediate calling frame to do so, so ReactApp(...) must be called here, not in a helper.

Tagging (tagging spec §5): the browser sends `tag_request` and `snippet_request` as event
inputs, mounted once in App with null defaults, and `refresh`. Each request is checked here, on
the event loop, then run as this session's extended task, outside Shiny's process-wide reactive
lock, so one window's batch never freezes another. Replies go back as messages ("tag_result",
"snippets") through shinyreact's send_message. Nothing is ever sent from a URL.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import shinyreact
from shiny import Inputs, Outputs, Session, reactive
from shinyreact import ReactApp, reactive_output

from recordings_ui import runtime, views
from recordings_ui.tagging import Job, Refusal, SnippetJob


def server(input: Inputs, output: Outputs, session: Session) -> None:
    archive = runtime.archive()
    media_base = runtime.media_base()
    tags = runtime.tagging().session()
    refreshed = reactive.value(0)

    async def send_tag_result(reply: dict) -> None:
        await shinyreact.send_message(session, "tag_result", reply)

    async def send_snippets(reply: dict) -> None:
        await shinyreact.send_message(session, "snippets", reply)

    @reactive_output
    async def library():
        refreshed()  # Refresh, and nothing else, rebuilds the view: deltas arrive by message
        return await tags.library()

    @reactive.effect
    @reactive.event(input.refresh)  # ignore_none: the mount default is null (decision 8)
    def _refresh() -> None:
        refreshed.set(refreshed.get() + 1)

    @reactive.extended_task
    async def tag_job(job: Job | Refusal) -> None:
        await tags.run(job, send_tag_result)

    @reactive.effect
    @reactive.event(input.tag_request)  # ignore_none, never ignore_init (spec §5)
    def _tag_request() -> None:
        tag_job.invoke(tags.check(input.tag_request()))  # check, enqueue, return

    @reactive.extended_task
    async def snippet_job(job: SnippetJob | Refusal) -> None:
        await tags.snippets(job, send_snippets)

    @reactive.effect
    @reactive.event(input.snippet_request)
    def _snippet_request() -> None:
        snippet_job.invoke(tags.check_snippets(input.snippet_request()))

    @reactive_output
    def recording():
        # Unset (before the client's first message) is a silent exception, which the client
        # sees as "pending"; an explicit null clears the pane.
        rid = input.selected_id()
        return views.recording_view(archive, rid, media_base=media_base) if rid else None

    @reactive_output
    def status():
        reactive.invalidate_later(60)  # backups finish and disks fill without anyone clicking
        return views.status_view(archive, runtime.status_settings())


app = ReactApp(server)
```

### Task 8: The browser build: `react-dom` bundled, and the new components and libraries

**Checkpoint lens:** forward compatibility of the build (one React; nothing bundled that the shinyreact skill says must stay external).

shinyreact's `ReactDOM` global is `react-dom/client` only (Task 1's upstream entry). Radix (Popover, Alert Dialog, Dialog), Sonner and TanStack Virtual import `createPortal` and `flushSync` from `react-dom`. So the build maps only `react` and `react-dom/client` to the globals, and bundles the `react-dom` index module, which requires `react` and so shares the one React (spec §6).

Checked before writing (CLAUDE.md): the shadcn skill (`.claude/skills/shadcn/SKILL.md`: add with the CLI, review what it adds, items inside their groups, a Dialog needs a Title, toasts are Sonner's `toast()` on a Radix project); `npx shadcn@4.21.4 view command popover alert-dialog sonner` on 2026-10-15 (Command depends on `cmdk`, Dialog and Input Group; Popover and Alert Dialog on `radix-ui`; Sonner's template imports `next-themes`); React's `react-dom/cjs/react-dom.production.js` (`createPortal`, `flushSync`); the shinyreact-build-app skill's Vite tier (its config maps `react-dom` to the global, which is what this task changes).

**Files:**
- Modify: `packages/ui/frontend/vite.config.js`
- Modify: `packages/ui/frontend/tsconfig.json` (`allowJs`, so a test can import the config)
- Modify: `packages/ui/frontend/package.json`, `package-lock.json` (`cmdk` 1.1.1, `sonner` 2.0.8, `@tanstack/react-virtual` 3.14.13; dev: `axe-core` 4.13.0)
- Create (by the shadcn CLI): `packages/ui/frontend/src/components/ui/{command,dialog,input-group,input,textarea,popover,alert-dialog,sonner}.tsx`
- Replace: `packages/ui/frontend/src/components/ui/sonner.tsx` (no `next-themes`)
- Modify: `packages/ui/frontend/src/lib/theme.ts` (`currentTheme`)
- Test: `packages/ui/frontend/src/build.test.ts` (new), `packages/ui/frontend/src/lib/theme.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces:
  - `vite.config.js` exports `EXTERNAL = ["react", "react-dom/client"]` and `GLOBALS` (`react` → `window.shinyreact.React`, `react-dom/client` → `window.shinyreact.ReactDOM`), used by its `rollupOptions`
  - `@/components/ui/command`: `Command, CommandDialog, CommandInput, CommandList, CommandEmpty, CommandGroup, CommandItem, CommandShortcut, CommandSeparator` (`CommandDialog` takes `title`, `description`, `className`, `showCloseButton`, and `Dialog`'s `open`/`onOpenChange`)
  - `@/components/ui/popover`: `Popover, PopoverTrigger, PopoverContent, PopoverAnchor`
  - `@/components/ui/alert-dialog`: `AlertDialog, AlertDialogContent, AlertDialogHeader, AlertDialogTitle, AlertDialogDescription, AlertDialogFooter, AlertDialogCancel, AlertDialogAction`
  - `@/components/ui/sonner`: `Toaster` (themed from `lib/theme.ts`)
  - `lib/theme.ts`: `currentTheme(root: HTMLElement): Resolved`

In `packages/ui/frontend/src/lib/theme.ts`, append:
```ts
/** The theme applyTheme last set on `root`: the `dark` class, or light. */
export function currentTheme(root: HTMLElement): Resolved {
  return root.classList.contains("dark") ? "dark" : "light";
}
```

Replace `packages/ui/frontend/src/components/ui/sonner.tsx` with:
```tsx
import { CircleCheckIcon, InfoIcon, Loader2Icon, OctagonXIcon, TriangleAlertIcon } from "lucide-react";
import { useEffect, useState, type CSSProperties } from "react";
import { Toaster as Sonner, type ToasterProps } from "sonner";

import { currentTheme, type Resolved } from "@/lib/theme";

/** shadcn's Sonner template reads next-themes; this app has its own theme (lib/theme.ts), which
 * sets the `dark` class on <html>. Follow that class instead (tagging spec §6). */
function useResolvedTheme(): Resolved {
  const [theme, setTheme] = useState<Resolved>(() => currentTheme(document.documentElement));
  useEffect(() => {
    const root = document.documentElement;
    const observer = new MutationObserver(() => setTheme(currentTheme(root)));
    observer.observe(root, { attributes: true, attributeFilter: ["class"] });
    return () => observer.disconnect();
  }, []);
  return theme;
}

const Toaster = ({ ...props }: ToasterProps) => {
  const theme = useResolvedTheme();
  return (
    <Sonner
      theme={theme}
      className="toaster group"
      icons={{
        success: <CircleCheckIcon className="size-4" />,
        info: <InfoIcon className="size-4" />,
        warning: <TriangleAlertIcon className="size-4" />,
        error: <OctagonXIcon className="size-4" />,
        loading: <Loader2Icon className="size-4 animate-spin" />,
      }}
      style={
        {
          "--normal-bg": "var(--popover)",
          "--normal-text": "var(--popover-foreground)",
          "--normal-border": "var(--border)",
          "--border-radius": "var(--radius)",
        } as CSSProperties
      }
      toastOptions={{ classNames: { toast: "cn-toast" } }}
      {...props}
    />
  );
};

export { Toaster };
```

### Task 9: The browser's logic, as pure modules with Vitest

**Checkpoint lens:** UX and accessibility (the keyboard model and the selection rules are where a wrong default bites every click).

Everything the components decide lives here, in plain `.ts` modules a test can call without React (spec §9, "Vitest"): the shortcut guard, the Finder selection reducer, the picker's ranking and Create rules, the request queue, merging replies, the toast and dialog wording, the toast timer, the grid's columns and sorts, and the hash routes.

**Files:**
- Modify: `packages/ui/frontend/src/types.ts`
- Modify: `packages/ui/frontend/src/lib/nav.ts`, `lib/nav.test.ts`
- Create: `packages/ui/frontend/src/lib/{keys,selection,picker,queue,library,toasts,dismiss,grid}.ts` and a `.test.ts` beside each

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: nothing new (`filterRows` from `lib/transcript.ts`).
- Produces (each exported exactly as named; later tasks import them):
  - **`types.ts`:** `LibraryRow.duration_ms: number | null`; `TagCount`; `LibraryView.can_tag`, `cannot_tag_reason`, `saves`; `TagAction`; `TagRequest`; `LosesPrivate`; `TagResult`; `SnippetRequest`; `SnippetResult`; `Page = "library" | "tags" | "status"`; `Route`
  - **`lib/nav.ts`:** `routeFromHash(hash): Route`, `pageFromHash(hash): Page`, `hashFor(page): string`, `openHref(id): string`
  - **`lib/keys.ts`:** `KeyLike`, `isTextEntry(target)`, `commandKey(e)`, `plainShortcut(e)`, `commandShortcut(e)`
  - **`lib/selection.ts`:** `Sel`, `NO_SEL`, `Move`, `SelAction`, `reduceSel(sel, action, order)`, `prune(sel, order) -> {sel, hidden}`, `targetsOf(sel, order)`
  - **`lib/picker.ts`:** `PickerTag`, `TagState`, `pickerTags(view, extra?)`, `rankTags(tags, query)`, `createOffer(tags, query)`, `tagState(tag, targets)`, `stateWords(state)`, `alreadyWords(state)`
  - **`lib/queue.ts`:** `OriginKind`, `Origin`, `Entry`, `Queue`, `EMPTY_QUEUE`, `STILL_WORKING_MS`, `enqueue`, `enqueueFront`, `takeNext`, `heard`, `pause`, `resume`, `stillWorking`, `dropAll`, `pendingRequests`, `newRequestId`
  - **`lib/library.ts`:** `mergeReply(view, reply)`, `orderFor(rows, filter)`, `withIds(order, ids, all)`, `ShownTag`, `shownTags(row, pending)`, `Mark`, `Marks`, `markReply(marks, reply)`, `filterIsShown(filter, view)`
  - **`lib/toasts.ts`:** `Text`, `textOf(text)`, `actionText(request, reply, saves)`, `failureLines(failures)`, `confirmTitle(request, lp, isPrivate)`, `confirmBody(lp)`, `progressText(done, total)`, `cellText(changes)`, `droppedText(dropped, inFlight)`, `STILL_WORKING`
  - **`lib/dismiss.ts`:** `Clock`, `TOAST_MS = 8000`, `pausableTimer(ms, onExpire, clock?) -> {pause, resume, cancel}`
  - **`lib/grid.ts`:** `DEFAULT_COLUMNS`, `COLUMN_KEY`, `defaultColumns(view)`, `Columns`, `layoutColumns(opts)`, `columnOrder(columns)`, `RowSort`, `sortRows(rows, sort)`, `gridRows(rows, opts)`, `digitColumn(key, columns)`, `digitFor(index)`, `loadChoices(storage)`, `saveChoices(storage, choices, isPrivate)`, `snippetsToFetch(onScreen, have, asked, max?)`, `foldersOf(tags)`

- [ ] **Step 1: Extend the types**

In `packages/ui/frontend/src/types.ts`, replace `LibraryRow`, `LibraryView` and `Page`, and append the tag types:
```ts
export interface LibraryRow {
  id: string; title: string; recorded_at: string; when: string; duration: string | null;
  duration_ms: number | null; kind: "audio" | "video"; sources: string[]; tags: TagRef[];
  private: boolean; untagged: boolean;
}

/** A tag and how many recordings carry it. `private` comes from the server, the browser's only
 * source for it: the browser never re-implements the privacy rule (spec §5). */
export interface TagCount { tag: string; count: number; private: boolean }

export interface LibraryView {
  recordings: LibraryRow[];
  counts: { all: number; untagged: number };
  tags: TagCount[];
  note_types: { note_type: string; count: number }[];
  problems: { path: string; message: string }[];
  can_tag: boolean; cannot_tag_reason: string | null;
  saves: boolean; // false only on the Pages demo: "Demo: changes aren't saved"
}

export type Page = "library" | "tags" | "status";
export interface Route { page: Page; open: string | null } // open: #/library/<id>, read-only

export type TagAction = "add" | "remove" | "undo";

/** The `tag_request` input (spec §5). Never sent on load, from a URL or from storage. */
export interface TagRequest {
  request_id: string; action: TagAction; ids: string[]; tags: string[];
  undo?: string; confirm_ids?: string[];
}

export interface LosesPrivate { visible: number; untagged: number; ids: string[] }

/** The `tag_result` message (spec §5). */
export interface TagResult {
  request_id: string | null; status: "done" | "confirm" | "failed" | "progress";
  changed: number; unchanged: number; done_so_far: number; total: number;
  loses_private: LosesPrivate | null; now_visible: number;
  failures: { id: string; reason: string }[]; undo: string | null;
  rows: LibraryRow[]; counts: LibraryView["counts"] | null; tags: TagCount[] | null;
  note_types: LibraryView["note_types"] | null; message: string | null;
}

export interface SnippetRequest { request_id: string; ids: string[] }
export interface SnippetResult { request_id: string | null; snippets: Record<string, string | null>; message: string | null }
```

`packages/ui/frontend/src/lib/keys.test.ts`:
```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

import { commandShortcut, isTextEntry, plainShortcut, type KeyLike } from "./keys";

const key = (over: Partial<KeyLike> = {}): KeyLike => ({
  key: "t", metaKey: false, ctrlKey: false, altKey: false, shiftKey: false, isComposing: false,
  target: document.body, ...over,
});

describe("isTextEntry", () => {
  it("is true in inputs, text areas, selects and editable content, and false elsewhere", () => {
    document.body.innerHTML = `<input id="i"><textarea id="t"></textarea><select id="s"></select>
      <div contenteditable="true"><span id="e">x</span></div><div contenteditable="false" id="n"></div>
      <div role="row" id="r"></div>`;
    for (const id of ["i", "t", "s", "e"]) expect(isTextEntry(document.getElementById(id))).toBe(true);
    for (const id of ["n", "r"]) expect(isTextEntry(document.getElementById(id))).toBe(false);
    expect(isTextEntry(null)).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe("the shortcut guard (spec §6)", () => {
  it("lets a bare key through only without Cmd, Ctrl or Alt, outside text, not composing", () => {
    expect(plainShortcut(key())).toBe(true);
    expect(plainShortcut(key({ metaKey: true }))).toBe(false);
    expect(plainShortcut(key({ ctrlKey: true }))).toBe(false);
    expect(plainShortcut(key({ altKey: true }))).toBe(false); // Alt+T is Sonner's
    expect(plainShortcut(key({ isComposing: true }))).toBe(false);
    document.body.innerHTML = `<input id="i">`;
    expect(plainShortcut(key({ target: document.getElementById("i") }))).toBe(false);
  });
  it("lets a Cmd/Ctrl shortcut through with either key, but not with Alt or in text", () => {
    expect(commandShortcut(key({ key: "a", metaKey: true }))).toBe(true);
    expect(commandShortcut(key({ key: "a", ctrlKey: true }))).toBe(true);
    expect(commandShortcut(key({ key: "a" }))).toBe(false);
    expect(commandShortcut(key({ key: "a", metaKey: true, altKey: true }))).toBe(false);
    document.body.innerHTML = `<textarea id="t"></textarea>`;
    expect(commandShortcut(key({ key: "a", metaKey: true, target: document.getElementById("t") }))).toBe(false);
  });
});
```

`packages/ui/frontend/src/lib/selection.test.ts`:
```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

import { NO_SEL, prune, reduceSel, targetsOf, type Sel, type SelAction } from "./selection";

const order = ["a", "b", "c", "d", "e"];
const run = (...actions: SelAction[]): Sel => actions.reduce((s, a) => reduceSel(s, a, order), NO_SEL);
const ids = (s: Sel) => [...s.selected].sort();
const click = (id: string, meta = false, shift = false): SelAction => ({ type: "click", id, meta, shift });

describe("clicks follow Finder (spec §6)", () => {
  it("a plain click selects one row and moves the anchor", () => {
    const s = run(click("b"), click("d"));
    expect(ids(s)).toEqual(["d"]);
    expect(s.anchor).toBe("d");
    expect(s.focus).toBe("d");
  });
  it("Cmd-click adds or removes a row and moves the anchor", () => {
    const s = run(click("b"), click("d", true), click("b", true));
    expect(ids(s)).toEqual(["d"]);
    expect(s.anchor).toBe("b");
  });
  it("Shift-click selects anchor to row, in either direction, keeping the anchor", () => {
    expect(ids(run(click("b"), click("d", false, true)))).toEqual(["b", "c", "d"]);
    const back = run(click("d"), click("b", false, true));
    expect(ids(back)).toEqual(["b", "c", "d"]);
    expect(back.anchor).toBe("d");
  });
  it("Shift-click replaces the selection; Cmd-Shift-click adds the range", () => {
    expect(ids(run(click("a"), click("e", true), click("c", false, true)))).toEqual(["c", "d", "e"]);
    expect(ids(run(click("a"), click("d", true), click("e", true, true)))).toEqual(["a", "d", "e"]);
  });
  it("the checkbox acts as Cmd-click, and as an added range with Shift", () => {
    expect(ids(run(click("a"), { type: "checkbox", id: "c", shift: false }))).toEqual(["a", "c"]);
    expect(ids(run(click("a"), { type: "checkbox", id: "c", shift: true }))).toEqual(["a", "b", "c"]);
  });
  it("Shift-click without an anchor is a plain click", () => {
    expect(ids(run(click("c", false, true)))).toEqual(["c"]);
  });
});

describe("keys follow Finder (spec §6)", () => {
  const move = (to: "next" | "prev" | "pageDown" | "pageUp" | "first" | "last", shift = false, meta = false): SelAction =>
    ({ type: "move", to, shift, meta, page: 2 });
  it("Up and Down move focus and select only the focused row", () => {
    const s = run(click("b"), move("next"), move("next"));
    expect(s.focus).toBe("d");
    expect(ids(s)).toEqual(["d"]);
    expect(run(move("next")).focus).toBe("a"); // from nothing focused: the first row
  });
  it("Shift+Up and Down extend from the anchor", () => {
    expect(ids(run(click("b"), move("next", true), move("next", true)))).toEqual(["b", "c", "d"]);
    expect(ids(run(click("c"), move("prev", true), move("prev", true)))).toEqual(["a", "b", "c"]);
  });
  it("Cmd+Up and Down move focus without changing the selection; Cmd+Space toggles", () => {
    const s = run(click("b"), move("next", false, true), move("next", false, true));
    expect(s.focus).toBe("d");
    expect(ids(s)).toEqual(["b"]);
    expect(ids(reduceSel(s, { type: "toggleFocused" }, order))).toEqual(["b", "d"]);
  });
  it("pages, the first and the last row, clamped, with Shift extending", () => {
    expect(run(click("a"), move("pageDown")).focus).toBe("c");
    expect(run(click("e"), move("pageDown")).focus).toBe("e");
    expect(run(click("b"), move("pageUp")).focus).toBe("a");
    expect(ids(run(click("b"), move("last", true)))).toEqual(["b", "c", "d", "e"]);
    expect(ids(run(click("d"), move("first")))).toEqual(["a"]);
  });
  it("Cmd-A selects every row in the filter, drawn or not; Esc clears", () => {
    const all = run(click("c"), { type: "all" });
    expect(ids(all)).toEqual(order);
    expect(all.focus).toBe("c");
    expect(ids(reduceSel(all, { type: "clear" }, order))).toEqual([]);
  });
});

describe("pruning and targets", () => {
  it("a filter change drops hidden rows, counts them, and focuses the first row", () => {
    const { sel, hidden } = prune(run(click("a"), click("c", true), click("e", true)), ["b", "c", "d"]);
    expect(ids(sel)).toEqual(["c"]);
    expect(hidden).toBe(2);
    expect(sel.anchor).toBe(null); // "e" is hidden now
    expect(sel.focus).toBe("b");
  });
  it("T targets the selection in list order, or the focused row when nothing is selected", () => {
    expect(targetsOf(run(click("d"), click("b", true)), order)).toEqual(["b", "d"]);
    const focusedOnly = reduceSel(run(click("c")), { type: "clear" }, order);
    expect(targetsOf(focusedOnly, order)).toEqual(["c"]);
    expect(targetsOf(NO_SEL, order)).toEqual([]);
  });
});
```

`packages/ui/frontend/src/lib/picker.test.ts`:
```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

import type { LibraryView } from "../types";
import { alreadyWords, createOffer, pickerTags, rankTags, stateWords, tagState, type PickerTag } from "./picker";

const tag = (name: string, count = 1, priv = false): PickerTag => ({ tag: name, count, private: priv });
const tags = [tag("school/course-101", 5), tag("courses", 2), tag("talks", 9), tag("private", 3, true), tag("notes/lecture", 4)];
const names = (q: string) => rankTags(tags, q).map((t) => t.tag);

describe("ranking (spec §6)", () => {
  it("puts an exact match first, then folder parts that start with it, then substrings", () => {
    expect(names("course")).toEqual(["school/course-101", "courses"]);
    expect(names("courses")).toEqual(["courses"]);
    // a folder part's start first, then substrings by use ("notes" holds an s)
    expect(names("S")).toEqual(["school/course-101", "talks", "notes/lecture", "courses"]);
    expect(names("ours")).toEqual(["school/course-101", "courses"]);
  });
  it("matches case-insensitively, and lists every tag by use when the box is empty", () => {
    expect(names("TALKS")).toEqual(["talks"]);
    expect(names("")).toEqual(["talks", "school/course-101", "notes/lecture", "private", "courses"]);
  });
});

describe("Create (spec §6)", () => {
  it("offers the typed text, trimmed, unless a tag matches it case-insensitively", () => {
    expect(createOffer(tags, "  work ")).toBe("work");
    expect(createOffer(tags, "Talks")).toBe(null); // a case-variant: never offered
    expect(createOffer(tags, "course")).toBe("course"); // a partial match still offers Create, last
    expect(createOffer(tags, "   ")).toBe(null);
  });
  it("never makes a notes/ tag", () => {
    expect(createOffer(tags, "notes/summary")).toBe(null);
    expect(createOffer(tags, "Notes/x")).toBe(null);
    expect(createOffer(tags, "notes")).toBe(null);
    expect(createOffer(tags, "notebook")).toBe("notebook");
  });
  it("creates a decomposed name in its composed form (Review Focus 1)", () => {
    const decomposed = "cafe\u0301";
    expect(createOffer(tags, decomposed)).toBe("caf\u00e9");
    expect(rankTags([tag("caf\u00e9")], decomposed).map((t) => t.tag)).toEqual(["caf\u00e9"]);
  });
});

describe("item states, in words as well as glyphs (spec §6)", () => {
  const rows = [{ tags: [{ tag: "talks" }] }, { tags: [{ tag: "talks" }, { tag: "work" }] }, { tags: [] }];
  it("counts how many targets carry the tag", () => {
    expect(tagState("talks", rows)).toEqual({ on: 2, of: 3, state: "some" });
    expect(stateWords(tagState("talks", rows))).toBe("on 2 of 3");
    expect(stateWords(tagState("talks", rows.slice(0, 2)))).toBe("on all 2");
    expect(stateWords(tagState("x", rows))).toBe("");
    expect(alreadyWords(tagState("talks", rows.slice(0, 2)))).toBe("Already on all 2");
    expect(alreadyWords(tagState("talks", rows.slice(0, 1)))).toBe("Already on it");
  });
});

describe("pickerTags", () => {
  it("takes tags with the server's private flags, note types as notes/<type>, and this session's new tags", () => {
    const view = {
      tags: [{ tag: "talks", count: 2, private: false }, { tag: "Private/x", count: 1, private: true }],
      note_types: [{ note_type: "lecture", count: 3 }],
    } as unknown as LibraryView;
    expect(pickerTags(view, ["fresh", "talks"])).toEqual([
      tag("talks", 2), tag("Private/x", 1, true), tag("notes/lecture", 3), tag("fresh", 0),
    ]);
  });
});
```

`packages/ui/frontend/src/lib/queue.test.ts`:
```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

import type { TagRequest, TagResult } from "../types";
import {
  EMPTY_QUEUE, STILL_WORKING_MS, dropAll, enqueue, enqueueFront, heard, newRequestId, pause,
  pendingRequests, resume, stillWorking, takeNext,
} from "./queue";

const request = (id: string): TagRequest => ({ request_id: id, action: "add", ids: ["a"], tags: ["t"] });
const reply = (id: string, status: TagResult["status"]): TagResult => ({
  request_id: id, status, changed: 0, unchanged: 0, done_so_far: 0, total: 0, loses_private: null,
  now_visible: 0, failures: [], undo: null, rows: [], counts: null, tags: null, note_types: null,
  message: null,
});
const origin = { kind: "picker" as const };

describe("the request queue (spec §6)", () => {
  it("sends one request at a time, in order", () => {
    let q = enqueue(enqueue(EMPTY_QUEUE, request("r1"), origin), request("r2"), origin);
    const first = takeNext(q, 0);
    expect(first.send?.request_id).toBe("r1");
    q = first.queue;
    expect(takeNext(q, 1).send).toBe(null); // r1 is in flight
    const done = heard(q, reply("r1", "done"), 2);
    expect(done.finished?.request.request_id).toBe("r1");
    expect(takeNext(done.queue, 3).send?.request_id).toBe("r2");
  });
  it("progress keeps a request in flight and resets the 30-second clock", () => {
    let q = takeNext(enqueue(EMPTY_QUEUE, request("r1"), origin), 0).queue;
    expect(stillWorking(q, STILL_WORKING_MS - 1)).toBe(false);
    q = heard(q, reply("r1", "progress"), 20_000).queue;
    expect(q.inFlight?.request.request_id).toBe("r1");
    expect(stillWorking(q, 20_000 + STILL_WORKING_MS - 1)).toBe(false);
    expect(stillWorking(q, 20_000 + STILL_WORKING_MS)).toBe(true);
  });
  it("ignores a reply to another request", () => {
    const q = takeNext(enqueue(EMPTY_QUEUE, request("r1"), origin), 0).queue;
    expect(heard(q, reply("other", "done"), 1).finished).toBe(null);
  });
  it("pauses while the confirmation is open, and sends the confirmed resend first", () => {
    let q = enqueue(enqueue(EMPTY_QUEUE, request("r1"), origin), request("r2"), origin);
    q = takeNext(q, 0).queue;
    q = pause(heard(q, reply("r1", "confirm"), 1).queue);
    expect(takeNext(q, 2).send).toBe(null);
    q = resume(enqueueFront(q, { ...request("r1b"), confirm_ids: ["a"] }, origin));
    expect(takeNext(q, 3).send?.request_id).toBe("r1b");
  });
  it("drops what is queued when the connection is lost, and says whether one was in flight", () => {
    const q = takeNext(enqueue(enqueue(EMPTY_QUEUE, request("r1"), origin), request("r2"), origin), 0).queue;
    expect(pendingRequests(q).map((r) => r.request_id)).toEqual(["r1", "r2"]);
    const lost = dropAll(q);
    expect(lost).toEqual({ queue: EMPTY_QUEUE, dropped: 1, inFlight: true });
  });
  it("makes request IDs from getRandomValues, never randomUUID", () => {
    const id = newRequestId((a) => a.fill(171));
    expect(id).toBe("ab".repeat(16));
    expect(newRequestId()).toMatch(/^[0-9a-f]{32}$/);
  });
});
```

```ts
import type { LibraryRow, LibraryView, TagResult } from "../types";
import { filterIsShown, markReply, mergeReply, orderFor, shownTags, withIds } from "./library";

const row = (id: string, tags: string[] = []): LibraryRow => ({

  note_types: [], problems: [], can_tag: true, cannot_tag_reason: null, saves: true,

const result = (over: Partial<TagResult>): TagResult => ({
  request_id: "r", status: "done", changed: 0, unchanged: 0, done_so_far: 0, total: 0,
  loses_private: null, now_visible: 0, failures: [], undo: null, rows: [], counts: null, tags: null,

describe("merging replies (spec §5, deltas)", () => {
  it("patches the changed rows in place and takes the recounted counts and tags", () => {
    const merged = mergeReply(view, result({
      rows: [row("b", ["work"])], counts: { all: 3, untagged: 1 },
      tags: [{ tag: "talks", count: 1, private: false }, { tag: "work", count: 1, private: false }],
    }));
    expect(merged.recordings.map((r) => r.id)).toEqual(["a", "b", "c"]);
    expect(merged.recordings[1].tags).toEqual([{ tag: "work", by: "you" }]);
    expect(merged.counts.untagged).toBe(1);
    expect(merged.tags.map((t) => t.tag)).toEqual(["talks", "work"]);
  });
  it("keeps its counts when a reply carries none (a refusal)", () => {
    expect(mergeReply(view, result({ status: "failed" }))).toBe(view);
  });
});

describe("rows stay put (spec §6)", () => {
  it("orders by the filter once; a later tag change keeps the row", () => {
    const untagged = orderFor(view.recordings, { kind: "untagged" });
    expect(untagged).toEqual(["b", "c"]);
    const merged = mergeReply(view, result({ rows: [row("b", ["work"])] }));
    expect(untagged.map((id) => merged.recordings.find((r) => r.id === id)?.id)).toEqual(["b", "c"]);
  });
  it("an Undo puts the rows it touched back, at their place in the view", () => {
    expect(withIds(["c"], ["a"], view.recordings)).toEqual(["a", "c"]);
  });
});

describe("pending changes show at once (spec §6)", () => {
  it("adds and removes optimistically, marked pending, only on the request's rows", () => {
    const pending = [
      { request_id: "1", action: "add" as const, ids: ["a"], tags: ["work"] },
      { request_id: "2", action: "remove" as const, ids: ["a"], tags: ["talks"] },
      { request_id: "3", action: "add" as const, ids: ["b"], tags: ["other"] },
    ];
    expect(shownTags(view.recordings[0], pending)).toEqual([
      { tag: "talks", by: "you", pending: "remove" },
      { tag: "work", by: "you", pending: "add" },
    ]);
  });
});

describe("marks", () => {
  it("marks changed rows done and failed rows with their reason", () => {
    const marks = markReply({}, result({ rows: [row("a")], failures: [{ id: "b", reason: "the recording is gone" }] }));
    expect(marks).toEqual({ a: { done: true }, b: { failed: "the recording is gone" } });
  });
});

describe("filterIsShown (Review Focus 4)", () => {
  it("is false for a tag filter whose tag no longer has a count", () => {
    expect(filterIsShown({ kind: "tag", tag: "talks" }, view)).toBe(true);
    expect(filterIsShown({ kind: "tag", tag: "gone" }, view)).toBe(false);
    expect(filterIsShown({ kind: "untagged" }, view)).toBe(true);
  });
});
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

import type { TagRequest, TagResult } from "../types";
import { actionText, cellText, confirmBody, confirmTitle, droppedText, failureLines, progressText, textOf } from "./toasts";

const req = (over: Partial<TagRequest>): TagRequest => ({ request_id: "r", action: "add", ids: [], tags: [], ...over });
const res = (over: Partial<TagResult>): TagResult => ({
  request_id: "r", status: "done", changed: 0, unchanged: 0, done_so_far: 0, total: 0,
  loses_private: null, now_visible: 0, failures: [], undo: "u", rows: [], counts: null, tags: null,
  note_types: null, message: null, ...over,
});

describe("toast wording (spec §6)", () => {
  it("names the tag and the count", () => {
    const text = actionText(req({ tags: ["school/course-101"], ids: Array(12).fill("x") }), res({ changed: 12 }), true);
    expect(textOf(text)).toBe("Added school/course-101 to 12 recordings");
    expect(text).toContainEqual({ code: "school/course-101" });
    expect(textOf(actionText(req({ action: "remove", tags: ["talks"] }), res({ changed: 1 }), true)))
      .toBe("Removed talks from 1 recording");
    expect(textOf(actionText(req({ tags: ["a", "b"] }), res({ changed: 3 }), true))).toBe("Added 2 tags to 3 recordings");
  });
  it("says when nothing changed", () => {
    expect(textOf(actionText(req({ tags: ["t"], ids: Array(12).fill("x") }), res({ changed: 0, unchanged: 12 }), true)))
      .toBe("Already on all 12");
  });
  it("adds the visibility, auto-pick and demo notes", () => {
    const visible = actionText(req({ tags: ["work"] }), res({ changed: 12, now_visible: 12 }), true);
    expect(textOf(visible)).toBe("Added work to 12 recordings · 12 now visible to external agents");
    const noted = actionText(req({ action: "remove", tags: ["notes/lecture"] }), res({ changed: 1 }), true);
    expect(textOf(noted)).toBe("Removed notes/lecture from 1 recording · auto-pick won't add it back");
    expect(textOf(actionText(req({ tags: ["t"] }), res({ changed: 1 }), false)))
      .toBe("Added t to 1 recording · Demo: changes aren't saved");
  });
  it("says an Undo, with counts grouped by thousands", () => {
    expect(textOf(actionText(req({ action: "undo", undo: "u" }), res({ changed: 1200 }), true))).toBe("Undone on 1,200 recordings");
    expect(progressText(1200, 2000)).toBe("Tagging 1,200 of 2,000…");
    expect(cellText(4)).toBe("4 changes");
    expect(cellText(1)).toBe("1 change");
  });
  it("groups failures by reason, with counts", () => {
    const stale = "edited outside the app: run `recordings reindex`";
    expect(failureLines([{ id: "a", reason: stale }, { id: "b", reason: stale }, { id: "c", reason: "the recording is gone" }]))
      .toEqual([`2 ${stale}`, "1 the recording is gone"]);
  });
  it("says what a lost connection dropped", () => {
    expect(droppedText(2, true)).toBe("Lost the connection: 2 queued changes were dropped. The change already sent was applied once or not at all; reload to see.");
    expect(droppedText(0, false)).toBe("Lost the connection. Reload to keep working.");
  });
});

describe("the confirmation's wording (spec §6)", () => {
  const isPrivate = (t: string) => t === "private" || t.startsWith("private/");
  it("names the private tag and how many recordings", () => {
    const lp = { visible: 2, untagged: 1, ids: ["a", "b", "c"] };
    expect(textOf(confirmTitle(req({ action: "remove", tags: ["private", "talks"] }), lp, isPrivate)))
      .toBe("Remove private from 3 recordings?");
    expect(textOf(confirmTitle(req({ action: "undo", undo: "u" }), lp, isPrivate)))
      .toBe("Undo will remove private from 3 recordings?");
    expect(confirmBody(lp)).toBe("2 become visible to external agents such as Claude Code. 1 will have no tags left, and stays hidden until its next tag.");
  });
  it("uses the singular and plural forms", () => {
    expect(confirmBody({ visible: 1, untagged: 0, ids: ["a"] })).toBe("1 becomes visible to external agents such as Claude Code.");
    expect(confirmBody({ visible: 0, untagged: 2, ids: ["a", "b"] })).toBe("2 will have no tags left, and stay hidden until their next tag.");
    const both = { visible: 0, untagged: 1, ids: ["a"] };
    expect(textOf(confirmTitle(req({ action: "remove", tags: ["private/a", "private/b"] }), both, isPrivate)))
      .toBe("Remove private/a, private/b from 1 recording?");
  });
});
```

```ts
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";

import { pausableTimer } from "./dismiss";

describe("pausableTimer (spec §6: about 8 s, paused while hovered or focused)", () => {
  beforeEach(() => vi.useFakeTimers());
  afterEach(() => vi.useRealTimers());
  it("expires after its time, minus the time it was paused", () => {
    const expired = vi.fn();
    const timer = pausableTimer(8000, expired);
    vi.advanceTimersByTime(5000);
    timer.pause();
    vi.advanceTimersByTime(60_000);
    expect(expired).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    timer.resume();
    vi.advanceTimersByTime(2999);
    expect(expired).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    vi.advanceTimersByTime(1);
    expect(expired).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });
  it("counts nested pauses (hover and focus together) and can be cancelled", () => {
    const expired = vi.fn();
    const timer = pausableTimer(1000, expired);
    timer.pause();
    timer.pause();
    timer.resume();
    vi.advanceTimersByTime(5000);
    expect(expired).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    timer.resume();
    timer.cancel();
    vi.advanceTimersByTime(5000);
    expect(expired).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
});
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

import type { LibraryRow, LibraryView } from "../types";
import {
  columnOrder, defaultColumns, digitColumn, digitFor, foldersOf, gridRows, layoutColumns, loadChoices,
  saveChoices, snippetsToFetch, sortRows,
} from "./grid";

const view = {
  tags: Array.from({ length: 20 }, (_, i) => ({ tag: `t${String(i).padStart(2, "0")}`, count: 20 - i, private: false }))
    .concat([{ tag: "private", count: 5, private: true }, { tag: "Private/health", count: 1, private: true }]),
  note_types: [{ note_type: "lecture", count: 30 }],
} as unknown as LibraryView;
const isPrivate = (t: string) => t === "private" || t === "Private/health";

describe("columns (spec §7)", () => {
  it("defaults to the 15 most-used tags, counted across all tags, notes/* included", () => {
    const cols = defaultColumns(view);
    expect(cols).toHaveLength(15);
    expect(cols[0]).toBe("notes/lecture");
    expect(cols).toContain("t00");
    expect(cols).not.toContain("t19");
  });
  it("pins private, groups other private tags at the right, and adds this session's tags at the end", () => {
    const all = [...view.tags.map((t) => t.tag), "notes/lecture"];
    const columns = layoutColumns({ base: defaultColumns(view), session: ["fresh"], all: [...all, "fresh"], isPrivate, choices: {}, hidden: new Set() });
    expect(columns.pinned).toBe("private");
    expect(columns.privateGroup).toEqual(["Private/health"]);
    expect(columns.normal.at(-1)).toBe("fresh");
    expect(columnOrder(columns)[0]).toBe("private");
  });
  it("shows and hides columns by the stored choices, and hides a private one for the session only", () => {
    const all = view.tags.map((t) => t.tag);
    const columns = layoutColumns({ base: ["t00"], session: [], all, isPrivate, choices: { t19: true, t00: false }, hidden: new Set(["Private/health"]) });
    expect(columns.normal).toEqual(["t19"]);
    expect(columns.privateGroup).toEqual([]);
    expect(columns.pinned).toBe("private");
  });
});

describe("stored choices never name a private tag (spec §7, K-M3)", () => {
  it("saves only non-private choices, and reads back defensively", () => {
    const store = new Map<string, string>();
    const storage = { getItem: (k: string) => store.get(k) ?? null, setItem: (k: string, v: string) => void store.set(k, v) };
    saveChoices(storage, { t01: true, "Private/health": false, private: false }, isPrivate);
    expect(JSON.parse(store.get("recordings.tagColumns") ?? "{}")).toEqual({ t01: true });
    expect(loadChoices(storage)).toEqual({ t01: true });
    store.set("recordings.tagColumns", "not json");
    expect(loadChoices(storage)).toEqual({});
    const throwing = { getItem: () => { throw new Error("blocked"); }, setItem: () => { throw new Error("blocked"); } };
    expect(loadChoices(throwing)).toEqual({});
    expect(() => saveChoices(throwing, { t01: true }, isPrivate)).not.toThrow();
  });
});

const row = (id: string, over: Partial<LibraryRow> = {}): LibraryRow => ({
  id, title: id, recorded_at: "2026-10-06T14:00:00-07:00", when: "", duration: null, duration_ms: null,
  kind: "audio", sources: [], tags: [], private: false, untagged: true, ...over,
});

describe("rows (spec §7)", () => {

    row("b", { title: "Beta", recorded_at: "2026-10-02T10:00:00Z", duration_ms: 50 }),
    row("a", { title: "alpha", recorded_at: "2026-10-03T10:00:00Z", duration_ms: 900, untagged: false, tags: [{ tag: "talks", by: "you" }] }),
    row("c", { title: "Gamma", recorded_at: "2026-10-01T10:00:00Z", duration_ms: null }),
  ];
  it("sorts by newest, oldest, title, duration and has-this-tag", () => {
    expect(sortRows(rows, { by: "newest" }).map((r) => r.id)).toEqual(["a", "b", "c"]);
    expect(sortRows(rows, { by: "oldest" }).map((r) => r.id)).toEqual(["c", "b", "a"]);
    expect(sortRows(rows, { by: "title" }).map((r) => r.id)).toEqual(["a", "b", "c"]);
    expect(sortRows(rows, { by: "duration" }).map((r) => r.id)).toEqual(["a", "b", "c"]);

describe("digit keys (spec §7)", () => {

    expect(digitColumn("1", cols)).toBe("c0");
    expect(digitColumn("0", cols)).toBe("c9");
    expect(digitColumn("a", cols)).toBe(null);
    expect(digitColumn("3", ["x"])).toBe(null);

  });
});

describe("snippets and folders", () => {
  it("asks for on-screen rows it hasn't got or asked for, at most 200", () => {
    const ids = Array.from({ length: 300 }, (_, i) => `r${i}`);

      ["school", ["school/a", "school/b"]], ["", ["talks"]],
    ]);
  });
});
```

`packages/ui/frontend/src/lib/nav.ts` (replace):
```ts
import type { Page, Route } from "../types";

const ID = /^[0-9]{8}T[0-9]{6}[+-][0-9]{4}_[0-9a-f]{8}$/;

/** What a URL's hash shows: `#/status`, `#/tags`, `#/library/<id>`, or the Library. The hash only
 * ever chooses what to show; nothing is written from it (spec §15). */
export function routeFromHash(hash: string): Route {
  const parts = hash.replace(/^#\/?/, "").split("?")[0].split("/");
  if (parts[0] === "status") return { page: "status", open: null };
  if (parts[0] === "tags") return { page: "tags", open: null };
  if (parts[0] === "library" && parts[1]) {
    let id = "";
    try {
      id = decodeURIComponent(parts[1]);
    } catch {
      id = "";
    }
    return { page: "library", open: ID.test(id) ? id : null };
  }
  return { page: "library", open: null };
}

export function pageFromHash(hash: string): Page {
  return routeFromHash(hash).page;
}

export function hashFor(page: Page): string {
  return page === "status" ? "#/status" : page === "tags" ? "#/tags" : "#/";
}

/** The Tags grid's Open: the recording in the Library, in a new tab (spec §7). */
export function openHref(id: string): string {
  return `#/library/${encodeURIComponent(id)}`;
}
```

`packages/ui/frontend/src/lib/keys.ts`:
```ts
/** The shortcut guard (spec §6): shortcuts fire only when focus is in the list or the grid, and
 * never with Cmd, Ctrl or Alt unless the key map lists them, while composing, or in text. */

export interface KeyLike {
  key: string; metaKey: boolean; ctrlKey: boolean; altKey: boolean; shiftKey: boolean;
  isComposing?: boolean; target: EventTarget | null;
}

const TEXT_ENTRY = "input, textarea, select, [contenteditable]:not([contenteditable='false'])";

export function isTextEntry(target: EventTarget | null): boolean {
  return target instanceof Element && target.closest(TEXT_ENTRY) !== null;
}

/** Cmd on a Mac, Ctrl elsewhere: the key map's "Cmd/Ctrl". */
export function commandKey(e: Pick<KeyLike, "metaKey" | "ctrlKey">): boolean {
  return e.metaKey || e.ctrlKey;
}

/** A bare shortcut: `T`, `R`, the digits. */
export function plainShortcut(e: KeyLike): boolean {
  return !e.metaKey && !e.ctrlKey && !e.altKey && !e.isComposing && !isTextEntry(e.target);
}

/** A key-map shortcut with Cmd/Ctrl: Cmd-A, Cmd-Z, Cmd+Space, Cmd+arrows. */
export function commandShortcut(e: KeyLike): boolean {
  return commandKey(e) && !e.altKey && !e.isComposing && !isTextEntry(e.target);
}
```

`packages/ui/frontend/src/lib/selection.ts`:
```ts
/** Finder-style selection (spec §6), shared by the Library list and the Tags grid. `order` is the
 * rows in the current filter, drawn or not. */

export interface Sel { selected: ReadonlySet<string>; anchor: string | null; focus: string | null }
export const NO_SEL: Sel = { selected: new Set(), anchor: null, focus: null };

export type Move = "next" | "prev" | "pageDown" | "pageUp" | "first" | "last";
export type SelAction =
  | { type: "click"; id: string; meta: boolean; shift: boolean }
  | { type: "checkbox"; id: string; shift: boolean }
  | { type: "move"; to: Move; shift: boolean; meta: boolean; page?: number }
  | { type: "toggleFocused" }
  | { type: "focus"; id: string }
  | { type: "all" }
  | { type: "clear" };

function toggled(set: ReadonlySet<string>, id: string): Set<string> {
  const next = new Set(set);
  if (next.has(id)) next.delete(id);
  else next.add(id);
  return next;
}

function span(order: readonly string[], a: string, b: string): string[] {
  const i = order.indexOf(a);
  const j = order.indexOf(b);
  if (i < 0 || j < 0) return [b];
  return order.slice(Math.min(i, j), Math.max(i, j) + 1);
}

export function reduceSel(sel: Sel, action: SelAction, order: readonly string[]): Sel {
  switch (action.type) {
    case "click": {
      const { id } = action;
      if (action.shift && sel.anchor !== null && order.includes(sel.anchor)) {
        const range = span(order, sel.anchor, id);
        const selected = action.meta ? new Set([...sel.selected, ...range]) : new Set(range);
        return { selected, anchor: sel.anchor, focus: id };
      }
      if (action.meta) return { selected: toggled(sel.selected, id), anchor: id, focus: id };
      return { selected: new Set([id]), anchor: id, focus: id };
    }
    case "checkbox": // as Cmd-click, and an added range with Shift
      return reduceSel(sel, { type: "click", id: action.id, meta: true, shift: action.shift }, order);
    case "move": {
      if (order.length === 0) return sel;
      const at = sel.focus === null ? -1 : order.indexOf(sel.focus);
      const page = Math.max(1, action.page ?? 10);
      const wanted = {
        next: at + 1, prev: at < 0 ? 0 : at - 1, pageDown: at + page, pageUp: at - page,
        first: 0, last: order.length - 1,
      }[action.to];
      const focus = order[Math.min(order.length - 1, Math.max(0, wanted))];
      if (action.meta) return { ...sel, focus };
      if (action.shift) {
        const anchor = sel.anchor !== null && order.includes(sel.anchor) ? sel.anchor : (sel.focus ?? focus);
        return { selected: new Set(span(order, anchor, focus)), anchor, focus };
      }
      return { selected: new Set([focus]), anchor: focus, focus };
    }
    case "toggleFocused":
      if (sel.focus === null) return sel;
      return { selected: toggled(sel.selected, sel.focus), anchor: sel.focus, focus: sel.focus };
    case "focus":
      return { ...sel, focus: action.id };
    case "all":
      return { selected: new Set(order), anchor: sel.anchor ?? order[0] ?? null, focus: sel.focus ?? order[0] ?? null };
    case "clear":
      return { ...sel, selected: new Set() };
  }
}

/** A filter change: hidden rows leave the selection (announced), focus goes to the first row. */
export function prune(sel: Sel, order: readonly string[]): { sel: Sel; hidden: number } {
  const keep = new Set(order);
  const selected = new Set([...sel.selected].filter((id) => keep.has(id)));
  return {
    sel: { selected, anchor: sel.anchor !== null && keep.has(sel.anchor) ? sel.anchor : null, focus: order[0] ?? null },
    hidden: sel.selected.size - selected.size,
  };
}

/** What `T`, the selection bar and Cmd-Z's neighbours act on: the selection in list order, or the
 * focused row when nothing is selected (tagging plan, decision 4). */
export function targetsOf(sel: Sel, order: readonly string[]): string[] {
  if (sel.selected.size === 0) return sel.focus !== null && order.includes(sel.focus) ? [sel.focus] : [];
  return order.filter((id) => sel.selected.has(id));
}
```

`packages/ui/frontend/src/lib/picker.ts`:
```ts
import type { LibraryView } from "../types";

/** The tag picker's logic (spec §6). Private flags come from the server, never from a rule here. */
export interface PickerTag { tag: string; private: boolean; count: number }
export interface TagState { on: number; of: number; state: "all" | "some" | "none" }

export function pickerTags(view: Pick<LibraryView, "tags" | "note_types">, extra: readonly string[] = []): PickerTag[] {
  const tags: PickerTag[] = view.tags.map((t) => ({ tag: t.tag, private: t.private, count: t.count }));
  for (const n of view.note_types) tags.push({ tag: `notes/${n.note_type}`, private: false, count: n.count });
  const have = new Set(tags.map((t) => t.tag));
  for (const tag of extra) {
    if (!have.has(tag)) {
      tags.push({ tag, private: false, count: 0 });
      have.add(tag);
    }
  }
  return tags;
}

const fold = (s: string) => s.normalize("NFC").toLowerCase();

function rank(tag: string, q: string): number {
  const t = fold(tag);
  if (t === q) return 0;
  if (t.startsWith(q) || t.split("/").some((part) => part.startsWith(q))) return 1;
  return t.includes(q) ? 2 : -1;
}

const byUse = (a: PickerTag, b: PickerTag) => b.count - a.count || a.tag.localeCompare(b.tag);

/** An exact case-insensitive match, then folder parts that start with the text, then any other
 * substring; within each, the most used first. Create comes after all of them. */
export function rankTags(tags: readonly PickerTag[], query: string): PickerTag[] {
  const q = fold(query.trim());
  if (!q) return [...tags].sort(byUse);
  return tags
    .map((t) => [rank(t.tag, q), t] as const)
    .filter(([r]) => r >= 0)
    .sort(([ra, a], [rb, b]) => ra - rb || byUse(a, b))
    .map(([, t]) => t);
}

/** What Create (and Shift+Enter) would make: the typed text, trimmed and composed (NFC; tagging
 * plan, decision 5). Never a notes/ tag, never a case-variant of an existing tag. */
export function createOffer(tags: readonly PickerTag[], query: string): string | null {
  const text = query.trim().normalize("NFC");
  if (!text || /^notes(\/|$)/i.test(text)) return null;
  const folded = fold(text);
  return tags.some((t) => fold(t.tag) === folded) ? null : text;
}

export function tagState(tag: string, targets: readonly { tags: readonly { tag: string }[] }[]): TagState {
  const on = targets.filter((r) => r.tags.some((t) => t.tag === tag)).length;
  return { on, of: targets.length, state: on === 0 ? "none" : on === targets.length ? "all" : "some" };
}

/** Each item's state in words as well as a glyph: ✓ "on all 12", – "on 5 of 12". */
export function stateWords(s: TagState): string {
  if (s.state === "all") return s.of === 1 ? "on it" : `on all ${s.of}`;
  return s.state === "some" ? `on ${s.on} of ${s.of}` : "";
}

/** Enter on a ✓ item adds nothing, and says so. */
export function alreadyWords(s: TagState): string {
  return s.of === 1 ? "Already on it" : `Already on all ${s.of}`;
}
```

`packages/ui/frontend/src/lib/queue.ts`:
```ts
import type { TagRequest, TagResult } from "../types";

/** The browser's request queue (spec §6): one request in flight at a time, because shinyreact
 * merges input values set in the same tick; paused while the confirmation is open; never re-sent. */

export type OriginKind = "picker" | "drop" | "header" | "bar" | "cell" | "chip" | "column" | "digit" | "undo";
export interface Origin { kind: OriginKind }
export interface Entry { request: TagRequest; origin: Origin; heardAt: number }
export interface Queue { inFlight: Entry | null; waiting: Entry[]; paused: boolean }

export const EMPTY_QUEUE: Queue = { inFlight: null, waiting: [], paused: false };
export const STILL_WORKING_MS = 30_000;

export function enqueue(q: Queue, request: TagRequest, origin: Origin): Queue {
  return { ...q, waiting: [...q.waiting, { request, origin, heardAt: 0 }] };
}

/** The confirmed resend goes first: it answers the request the dialog interrupted. */
export function enqueueFront(q: Queue, request: TagRequest, origin: Origin): Queue {
  return { ...q, waiting: [{ request, origin, heardAt: 0 }, ...q.waiting] };
}

export function takeNext(q: Queue, now: number): { queue: Queue; send: TagRequest | null } {
  if (q.inFlight || q.paused || q.waiting.length === 0) return { queue: q, send: null };
  const [next, ...rest] = q.waiting;
  return { queue: { ...q, inFlight: { ...next, heardAt: now }, waiting: rest }, send: next.request };
}

/** Progress keeps the request in flight and restarts the 30-second clock; any other status ends
 * it. A reply to another request changes nothing. */
export function heard(q: Queue, reply: TagResult, now: number): { queue: Queue; finished: Entry | null } {
  if (!q.inFlight || reply.request_id !== q.inFlight.request.request_id) return { queue: q, finished: null };
  if (reply.status === "progress") return { queue: { ...q, inFlight: { ...q.inFlight, heardAt: now } }, finished: null };
  return { queue: { ...q, inFlight: null }, finished: q.inFlight };
}

export const pause = (q: Queue): Queue => ({ ...q, paused: true });
export const resume = (q: Queue): Queue => ({ ...q, paused: false });

/** "Still working…": the request is never re-sent, because it may still apply. */
export function stillWorking(q: Queue, now: number): boolean {
  return q.inFlight !== null && now - q.inFlight.heardAt >= STILL_WORKING_MS;
}

/** On shiny:disconnected: the queue is dropped. A request already sent was applied once or not
 * at all (spec §8). */
export function dropAll(q: Queue): { queue: Queue; dropped: number; inFlight: boolean } {
  return { queue: EMPTY_QUEUE, dropped: q.waiting.length, inFlight: q.inFlight !== null };
}

export function pendingRequests(q: Queue): TagRequest[] {
  return [...(q.inFlight ? [q.inFlight.request] : []), ...q.waiting.map((e) => e.request)];
}

/** 128 random bits from crypto.getRandomValues: crypto.randomUUID needs a secure context, and the
 * app may be served over plain HTTP on the tailnet (spec §5). */
export function newRequestId(fill: (a: Uint8Array) => Uint8Array = (a) => crypto.getRandomValues(a)): string {
  return Array.from(fill(new Uint8Array(16)), (b) => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")).join("");
}
```

`packages/ui/frontend/src/lib/library.ts`:
```ts
import type { Filter, LibraryRow, LibraryView, TagRef, TagRequest, TagResult } from "../types";
import { filterRows } from "./transcript";

/** Merge a reply's delta into the library the browser holds (spec §5): the changed rows in
 * place, and the recounted counts, tags and note types. The full view comes only on load and
 * Refresh. */
export function mergeReply(view: LibraryView, reply: TagResult): LibraryView {
  if (reply.rows.length === 0 && reply.counts === null) return view;
  const byId = new Map(reply.rows.map((r) => [r.id, r]));
  const recordings = view.recordings.map((r) => byId.get(r.id) ?? r);
  const known = new Set(view.recordings.map((r) => r.id));
  for (const r of reply.rows) if (!known.has(r.id)) recordings.push(r);
  return {
    ...view,
    recordings,
    counts: reply.counts ?? view.counts,
    tags: reply.tags ?? view.tags,
    note_types: reply.note_types ?? view.note_types,
  };
}

/** The rows a filter shows, in the view's order. Worked out on load, on Refresh and on a filter
 * change, never on a tag change: rows stay put, marked done, until then (spec §6). */
export function orderFor(rows: readonly LibraryRow[], filter: Filter): string[] {
  return filterRows([...rows], filter).map((r) => r.id);
}

/** An Undo puts the rows it touched back into the current view, at their place in `all`. */
export function withIds(order: readonly string[], ids: readonly string[], all: readonly LibraryRow[]): string[] {
  const want = new Set([...order, ...ids]);
  return all.map((r) => r.id).filter((id) => want.has(id));
}

export type ShownTag = TagRef & { pending?: "add" | "remove" };
const idSets = new WeakMap<TagRequest, Set<string>>();

function idsOf(request: TagRequest): Set<string> {
  let ids = idSets.get(request);
  if (!ids) {
    ids = new Set(request.ids);
    idSets.set(request, ids);
  }
  return ids;
}

/** A row's tags as they will be: a pending add shows at once, a pending removal shows struck
 * through, both marked pending, until the reply (spec §6). */
export function shownTags(row: LibraryRow, pending: readonly TagRequest[]): ShownTag[] {
  const tags: ShownTag[] = row.tags.map((t) => ({ ...t }));
  for (const request of pending) {
    if (request.action === "undo" || !idsOf(request).has(row.id)) continue;
    for (const tag of request.tags) {
      const at = tags.findIndex((t) => t.tag === tag);
      if (request.action === "add" && at < 0) tags.push({ tag, by: "you", pending: "add" });
      if (request.action === "remove" && at >= 0) tags[at] = { ...tags[at], pending: "remove" };
    }
  }
  return tags;
}

export interface Mark { done?: boolean; failed?: string }
export type Marks = Readonly<Record<string, Mark>>;

/** Done rows keep a "✓ Tagged" mark, failed rows their reason, until Refresh (spec §6). */
export function markReply(marks: Marks, reply: TagResult): Marks {
  const next: Record<string, Mark> = { ...marks };
  for (const r of reply.rows) next[r.id] = { done: true };
  for (const f of reply.failures) next[f.id] = { ...next[f.id], failed: f.reason };
  return next;
}

/** Whether the sidebar lists the filter's item. A tag filter whose tag lost its last recording
 * would vanish while still applied (Review Focus 4): the sidebar keeps it, at 0. */
export function filterIsShown(filter: Filter, view: Pick<LibraryView, "tags" | "note_types">): boolean {
  if (filter.kind !== "tag") return true;
  if (filter.tag.startsWith("notes/")) return view.note_types.some((n) => `notes/${n.note_type}` === filter.tag);
  return view.tags.some((t) => t.tag === filter.tag);
}
```

`packages/ui/frontend/src/lib/toasts.ts`:
```ts
import type { LosesPrivate, TagRequest, TagResult } from "../types";

/** What toasts and the confirmation say (spec §6). A tag name is a `{ code }` part, drawn as
 * <code>, always as text, never as HTML. */
export type Text = (string | { code: string })[];

export const STILL_WORKING = "Still working…";
const n = (count: number) => count.toLocaleString("en");
const recordings = (count: number) => `${n(count)} recording${count === 1 ? "" : "s"}`;

export function textOf(text: Text): string {
  return text.map((part) => (typeof part === "string" ? part : part.code)).join("");
}

function listed(tags: readonly string[]): Text {
  return tags.flatMap((tag, i) => (i === 0 ? [{ code: tag }] : [", ", { code: tag }]));
}

export function actionText(request: TagRequest, reply: TagResult, saves: boolean): Text {
  let text: Text;
  const tags = request.tags;
  if (request.action === "undo") text = [`Undone on ${recordings(reply.changed)}`];
  else if (reply.changed === 0 && request.action === "add") {
    const of = reply.unchanged || request.ids.length;
    text = [of === 1 ? "Already on it" : `Already on all ${n(of)}`];
  } else if (tags.length === 1) {
    text = request.action === "add"
      ? ["Added ", { code: tags[0] }, ` to ${recordings(reply.changed)}`]
      : ["Removed ", { code: tags[0] }, ` from ${recordings(reply.changed)}`];
  } else {
    text = [`${request.action === "add" ? "Added" : "Removed"} ${tags.length} tags ${request.action === "add" ? "to" : "from"} ${recordings(reply.changed)}`];
  }
  if (reply.now_visible > 0) text.push(` · ${n(reply.now_visible)} now visible to external agents`);
  if (request.action === "remove" && tags.some((t) => t.startsWith("notes/"))) text.push(" · auto-pick won't add it back");
  if (!saves) text.push(" · Demo: changes aren't saved");
  return text;
}

/** Partial failures, grouped by reason, with counts: "3 edited outside the app: run …". */
export function failureLines(failures: readonly { id: string; reason: string }[]): string[] {
  const counts = new Map<string, number>();
  for (const f of failures) counts.set(f.reason, (counts.get(f.reason) ?? 0) + 1);
  return [...counts].map(([reason, count]) => `${n(count)} ${reason}`);
}

export function confirmTitle(request: TagRequest, lp: LosesPrivate, isPrivate: (tag: string) => boolean): Text {
  const count = recordings(lp.ids.length);
  if (request.action === "undo") return ["Undo will remove ", { code: "private" }, ` from ${count}?`];
  const named = request.tags.filter(isPrivate);
  return ["Remove ", ...listed(named.length ? named : ["private"]), ` from ${count}?`];
}

export function confirmBody(lp: LosesPrivate): string {
  const parts: string[] = [];
  if (lp.visible > 0) {
    parts.push(`${n(lp.visible)} ${lp.visible === 1 ? "becomes" : "become"} visible to external agents such as Claude Code.`);
  }
  if (lp.untagged > 0) {
    const one = lp.untagged === 1;
    parts.push(`${n(lp.untagged)} will have no tags left, and ${one ? "stays" : "stay"} hidden until ${one ? "its" : "their"} next tag.`);
  }
  return parts.join(" ");
}

export function progressText(done: number, total: number): string {
  return `Tagging ${n(done)} of ${n(total)}…`;
}

/** The Tags grid's merged toast for single-cell toggles: "4 changes · Undo last". */
export function cellText(changes: number): string {
  return `${n(changes)} change${changes === 1 ? "" : "s"}`;
}

export function droppedText(dropped: number, inFlight: boolean): string {
  if (dropped === 0 && !inFlight) return "Lost the connection. Reload to keep working.";
  const queued = dropped > 0 ? `: ${n(dropped)} queued change${dropped === 1 ? " was" : "s were"} dropped.` : ".";
  const sent = inFlight ? " The change already sent was applied once or not at all; reload to see." : " Reload to keep working.";
  return `Lost the connection${queued}${sent}`;
}
```

`packages/ui/frontend/src/lib/dismiss.ts`:
```ts
/** A toast's own timer (tagging plan, decision 9): about 8 s, paused while the toast is hovered
 * or focused. Pauses nest, so hover and focus together need both to end. */

export interface Clock { set(fn: () => void, ms: number): unknown; clear(handle: unknown): void; now(): number }

export const TOAST_MS = 8000;

const realClock: Clock = {
  set: (fn, ms) => setTimeout(fn, ms),
  clear: (handle) => clearTimeout(handle as ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>),
  now: () => Date.now(),
};

export function pausableTimer(ms: number, onExpire: () => void, clock: Clock = realClock) {
  let left = ms;
  let startedAt = clock.now();
  let handle: unknown = clock.set(onExpire, left);
  let pauses = 0;
  let cancelled = false;
  return {
    pause() {
      if (cancelled) return;
      pauses += 1;
      if (pauses === 1 && handle !== null) {
        clock.clear(handle);
        handle = null;
        left -= clock.now() - startedAt;
      }
    },
    resume() {
      if (cancelled || pauses === 0) return;
      pauses -= 1;
      if (pauses === 0) {
        startedAt = clock.now();
        handle = clock.set(onExpire, Math.max(0, left));
      }
    },
    cancel() {
      cancelled = true;
      if (handle !== null) clock.clear(handle);
      handle = null;
    },
  };
}
```
Vitest's fake timers replace `setTimeout`, `clearTimeout` and `Date.now`, so the default clock works under them; resolve `realClock`'s functions at call time, as above, not at import.

`packages/ui/frontend/src/lib/grid.ts`:
```ts
import type { LibraryRow, LibraryView } from "../types";

/** The Tags page grid's logic (spec §7). */

export const DEFAULT_COLUMNS = 15;
export const COLUMN_KEY = "recordings.tagColumns";

type Storage = { getItem(k: string): string | null; setItem(k: string, v: string): void };

/** The 15 most-used tags, counted across all tags, notes/* included. Worked out on load and on
 * Refresh, never live, so columns don't move under the cursor. */
export function defaultColumns(view: Pick<LibraryView, "tags" | "note_types">, limit = DEFAULT_COLUMNS): string[] {
  const all = [
    ...view.tags.map((t) => ({ tag: t.tag, count: t.count })),
    ...view.note_types.map((n) => ({ tag: `notes/${n.note_type}`, count: n.count })),
  ];
  return all.sort((a, b) => b.count - a.count || a.tag.localeCompare(b.tag)).slice(0, limit).map((t) => t.tag);
}

export interface Columns { pinned: string | null; normal: string[]; privateGroup: string[] }

/** `private` pinned next to the first column; other private tags grouped at the right; this
 * session's tags at the end of their group. Private columns are shown unless hidden for this
 * session, so no private tag name is ever stored (spec §7). */
export function layoutColumns(opts: {
  base: readonly string[]; session: readonly string[]; all: readonly string[];
  isPrivate: (tag: string) => boolean; choices: Readonly<Record<string, boolean>>; hidden: ReadonlySet<string>;
}): Columns {
  const { base, session, all, isPrivate, choices, hidden } = opts;
  const order = [...new Set([...base, ...session, ...[...all].sort((a, b) => a.localeCompare(b))])];
  const shown = (tag: string) =>
    !hidden.has(tag) && (isPrivate(tag) || (choices[tag] ?? (base.includes(tag) || session.includes(tag))));
  const visible = order.filter((tag) => tag !== "private" && shown(tag));
  return {
    pinned: hidden.has("private") ? null : "private",
    normal: visible.filter((tag) => !isPrivate(tag)),
    privateGroup: visible.filter((tag) => isPrivate(tag)),
  };
}

export function columnOrder(columns: Columns): string[] {
  return [...(columns.pinned ? [columns.pinned] : []), ...columns.normal, ...columns.privateGroup];
}

export type RowSort = { by: "newest" | "oldest" | "title" | "duration" } | { by: "has"; tag: string };

export function sortRows(rows: readonly LibraryRow[], sort: RowSort): LibraryRow[] {
  const time = (r: LibraryRow) => Date.parse(r.recorded_at);
  const copy = [...rows];
  switch (sort.by) {
    case "newest": return copy.sort((a, b) => time(b) - time(a));
    case "oldest": return copy.sort((a, b) => time(a) - time(b));
    case "title": return copy.sort((a, b) => a.title.localeCompare(b.title, undefined, { sensitivity: "base" }));
    case "duration": return copy.sort((a, b) => (b.duration_ms ?? -1) - (a.duration_ms ?? -1));
    case "has": {
      const has = (r: LibraryRow) => (r.tags.some((t) => t.tag === sort.tag) ? 0 : 1);
      return copy.sort((a, b) => has(a) - has(b) || time(b) - time(a));
    }
  }
}

export function gridRows(rows: readonly LibraryRow[], opts: { untaggedOnly: boolean; title: string }): LibraryRow[] {
  const q = opts.title.trim().toLowerCase();
  return rows.filter((r) => (!opts.untaggedOnly || r.untagged) && (!q || r.title.toLowerCase().includes(q)));
}

/** `1`–`9` and `0`: the first ten visible tag columns. */
export function digitColumn(key: string, columns: readonly string[]): string | null {
  if (!/^[0-9]$/.test(key)) return null;
  return columns[key === "0" ? 9 : Number(key) - 1] ?? null;
}

export function digitFor(index: number): string | null {
  return index < 9 ? String(index + 1) : index === 9 ? "0" : null;
}

export function loadChoices(storage: Storage | null): Record<string, boolean> {
  try {
    const parsed: unknown = JSON.parse(storage?.getItem(COLUMN_KEY) ?? "{}");
    if (!parsed || typeof parsed !== "object" || Array.isArray(parsed)) return {};
    return Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(parsed).filter(([, v]) => typeof v === "boolean")) as Record<string, boolean>;
  } catch {
    return {};
  }
}

export function saveChoices(storage: Storage | null, choices: Readonly<Record<string, boolean>>, isPrivate: (tag: string) => boolean): void {
  const kept = Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(choices).filter(([tag]) => !isPrivate(tag)));
  try {
    storage?.setItem(COLUMN_KEY, JSON.stringify(kept));
  } catch {
    /* a per-browser convenience only */
  }
}

/** Snippets for rows on screen (spec §5): ones not yet held or asked for, at most 200. */
export function snippetsToFetch(onScreen: readonly string[], have: ReadonlyMap<string, unknown>, asked: ReadonlySet<string>, max = 200): string[] {
  return onScreen.filter((id) => !have.has(id) && !asked.has(id)).slice(0, max);
}

/** The column picker's groups: by first folder; "" for tags with no folder. */
export function foldersOf(tags: readonly string[]): Map<string, string[]> {
  const groups = new Map<string, string[]>();
  for (const tag of tags) {
    const folder = tag.includes("/") ? tag.split("/")[0] : "";
    groups.set(folder, [...(groups.get(folder) ?? []), tag]);
  }
  return groups;
}
```

### Task 10: App plumbing: the inputs, the message handlers, the queue, toasts, the confirmation, and the header's chips

**Checkpoint lens:** UX and accessibility (toasts, the dialog's focus, the reply path in `App`).

Everything that must outlive a page lives in `App`, which never unmounts (spec §6): the three event inputs, the `tag_result` and `snippets` handlers, the request queue, the toasts, the confirmation, the merged library. A hook, `useTagging`, holds it, and `App` calls it; components reach it through `TaggingContext`. The recording header's chips move from the `recording` output to the open recording's row, and gain an ×. The first e2e writing tests arrive, in a module of their own with a server per test.

Checked before writing (CLAUDE.md): the installed shinyreact types (`node_modules/@posit-dev/shinyreact/dist-npm/types/shiny-react/use-shiny.d.ts`: `useSetShinyInput(id, default, { debounceMs, priority })`, `useShinyMessageHandler(id, handler)`, handlers kept in a ref); the shinyreact-build-app skill ("Action button": `debounceMs: 0`, `priority: "event"`); Sonner's docs through context7 (`toast(node, { id, duration })` updates a toast with the same id; `toast.dismiss(id)`); shadcn's Alert Dialog (`npx shadcn@4.21.4 docs alert-dialog`: Cancel and Action inside the Footer, Title and Description required). Radix's Alert Dialog focuses Cancel when it opens and returns focus when it closes.

```ts
import { createElement } from "react";
import { renderToStaticMarkup } from "react-dom/server";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

import { AlertToast, ProgressToast, Rich } from "./ActionToast";

describe("toast bodies (spec §6)", () => {
  it("draws a tag as code, as text and never as HTML", () => {
    const html = renderToStaticMarkup(createElement(Rich, { text: ["Added ", { code: "<b>x</b>" }, " to 1 recording"] }));
    expect(html).toBe("<span>Added </span><code>&lt;b&gt;x&lt;/b&gt;</code><span> to 1 recording</span>");
  });
  it("announces progress once, and keeps the moving count out of the live region", () => {
    const html = renderToStaticMarkup(createElement(ProgressToast, { announced: "Tagging 2,000 recordings…", shown: "Tagging 1,200 of 2,000…" }));
    expect(html).toContain('<span class="sr-only">Tagging 2,000 recordings…</span>');
    expect(html).toContain('<span aria-hidden="true">Tagging 1,200 of 2,000…</span>');
  });
  it("puts partial failures in an alert that stays until dismissed", () => {
    const html = renderToStaticMarkup(createElement(AlertToast, { id: "x", lines: ["3 the recording is gone"] }));
    expect(html).toContain('role="alert"');
    expect(html).toContain(">Dismiss</button>");
  });
});
```

```tsx
import { useEffect, useRef, type FocusEvent } from "react";
import { toast } from "sonner";

import { TOAST_MS, pausableTimer } from "@/lib/dismiss";
import type { Text } from "@/lib/toasts";

/** Text with tag names as <code>, always as text nodes. */
export function Rich({ text }: { text: Text }) {
  return <>{text.map((part, i) => (typeof part === "string" ? <span key={i}>{part}</span> : <code key={i}>{part.code}</code>))}</>;
}

const leaving = (e: FocusEvent<HTMLElement>) => !e.currentTarget.contains(e.relatedTarget as Node | null);

/** An action's toast: about 8 s, paused while hovered or focused (spec §6; plan decision 9). */
export function ActionToast({ id, text, undo }: { id: string; text: Text; undo: { label: string; run: () => void } | null }) {
  const timer = useRef<ReturnType<typeof pausableTimer> | null>(null);
  useEffect(() => {
    timer.current = pausableTimer(TOAST_MS, () => toast.dismiss(id));
    return () => timer.current?.cancel();
  }, [id, text]);
  return (
    <div
      className="action-toast"
      data-testid="action-toast"
      onMouseEnter={() => timer.current?.pause()}
      onMouseLeave={() => timer.current?.resume()}
      onFocus={(e) => leaving(e) && timer.current?.pause()}
      onBlur={(e) => leaving(e) && timer.current?.resume()}
    >
      <span className="action-toast-text"><Rich text={text} /></span>
      {undo && (
        <button type="button" className="toast-undo" onClick={() => { toast.dismiss(id); undo.run(); }}>
          {undo.label}
        </button>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

/** Partial failures and stops: role="alert", and they stay until dismissed (spec §6). */
export function AlertToast({ id, lines }: { id: string; lines: string[] }) {
  return (
    <div role="alert" className="alert-toast" data-testid="alert-toast">
      {lines.map((line) => <p key={line}>{line}</p>)}
      <button type="button" className="toast-undo" onClick={() => toast.dismiss(id)}>Dismiss</button>
    </div>
  );
}

/** A batch's progress: announced at the start (and, by the result, at the end) only; the moving
 * count is hidden from the live region (spec §6). */
export function ProgressToast({ announced, shown }: { announced: string; shown: string }) {
  return (
    <div className="progress-toast" data-testid="progress-toast">
      <span className="sr-only">{announced}</span>
      <span aria-hidden="true">{shown}</span>
    </div>
  );
}
```
`onFocus` and `onBlur` act only when focus enters from outside or leaves altogether, so moving between the text and Undo doesn't stack pauses.

`packages/ui/frontend/src/tagging/toasts.tsx`:
```tsx
import { toast } from "sonner";

import { STILL_WORKING, actionText, cellText, droppedText, failureLines, progressText, type Text } from "@/lib/toasts";
import type { TagRequest, TagResult } from "@/types";

import { ActionToast, AlertToast, ProgressToast } from "./ActionToast";

const forever = { duration: Infinity }; // our own timers dismiss them (plan decision 9)
const toastId = (request: TagRequest) => `tag-${request.request_id}`;

export function showProgress(request: TagRequest, done: number, total: number): void {
  const announced = `Tagging ${total.toLocaleString("en")} recordings…`;
  toast(<ProgressToast announced={announced} shown={progressText(done, total)} />, { ...forever, id: toastId(request) });
}

export function showStillWorking(request: TagRequest): void {
  toast(<ProgressToast announced={STILL_WORKING} shown={STILL_WORKING} />, { ...forever, id: toastId(request) });
}

function showAction(id: string, text: Text, undo: { label: string; run: () => void } | null): void {
  toast(<ActionToast id={id} text={text} undo={undo} />, { ...forever, id });
}

/** The toast an ending reply shows: the action with its Undo (or Redo, for an Undo; plan
 * decision 7), and, when the batch stopped or was refused, an alert with the fixed phrase. */
export function showResult(request: TagRequest, reply: TagResult, saves: boolean, onUndo: (token: string) => void): void {
  const id = toastId(request);
  if (reply.status === "done" || reply.changed > 0) {
    const token = reply.undo;
    const undo = token ? { label: request.action === "undo" ? "Redo" : "Undo", run: () => onUndo(token) } : null;
    showAction(id, actionText(request, reply, saves), undo);
  } else {
    toast.dismiss(id);
  }
  if (reply.status === "failed" && reply.message) showAlert(`stop-${request.request_id}`, [reply.message]);
  if (reply.failures.length > 0) showAlert(`failed-${request.request_id}`, failureLines(reply.failures));
}

/** Single-cell toggles in the grid share one running toast: "4 changes · Undo last". */
export function showCells(changes: number, onUndoLast: () => void): void {
  showAction("grid-cells", [cellText(changes)], { label: "Undo last", run: onUndoLast });
}

export function showAlert(id: string, lines: string[]): void {
  toast(<AlertToast id={id} lines={lines} />, { ...forever, id });
}

export function showNote(id: string, text: Text): void {
  showAction(id, text, null);
}

export function showDropped(dropped: number, inFlight: boolean): void {
  showAlert("connection-lost", [droppedText(dropped, inFlight)]);
}
```
