`packages/ui/frontend/src/tagging/useTagging.tsx`:
```tsx
import { createContext, useCallback, useContext, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from "react";

import { snippetsToFetch } from "@/lib/grid";
import { markReply, mergeReply, type Marks } from "@/lib/library";
import {
  EMPTY_QUEUE, dropAll, enqueue, enqueueFront, heard, newRequestId, pause, pendingRequests, resume,
  stillWorking, takeNext, type Origin, type Queue,
} from "@/lib/queue";
import { useSetShinyInput, useShinyMessageHandler } from "@/sr";
import type { LibraryView, SnippetRequest, SnippetResult, TagRequest, TagResult } from "@/types";

import { showAlert, showCells, showDropped, showProgress, showResult, showStillWorking } from "./toasts";

export interface PickerTarget { ids: string[]; title: string } // the dialog form: T, the selection bar, a drop
export interface ConfirmState { request: TagRequest; origin: Origin; reply: TagResult }

export interface Tagging {
  view: LibraryView | null;
  loadKey: number; // bumps on each full library: load and Refresh
  marks: Marks;
  pending: TagRequest[];
  confirm: ConfirmState | null;
  revealed: { key: number; ids: string[] }; // rows an Undo put back into the view
  touched: number;
  lastTagged: string | null;
  sessionTags: string[];
  drag: { current: string[] | null }; // set at dragstart, never read from dataTransfer
  canTag: boolean;
  reason: string | null;
  saves: boolean;
  isPrivate: (tag: string) => boolean;
  tag: (action: "add" | "remove", ids: string[], tags: string[], origin: Origin) => void;
  undo: (token: string) => void;
  undoLast: () => void;
  refresh: () => void;
  answerConfirm: (remove: boolean) => void;
  picker: PickerTarget | null;
  openPicker: (target: PickerTarget) => void;
  closePicker: () => void;
  snippet: (id: string) => string | null | undefined;
  wantSnippets: (ids: string[]) => void;
}

export const TaggingContext = createContext<Tagging | null>(null);

export function useTaggingContext(): Tagging {
  const tagging = useContext(TaggingContext);
  if (!tagging) throw new Error("useTaggingContext is used outside App");
  return tagging;
}

const UNDO_STACK = 20;
const PROGRESS_FROM = 50; // a request this big shows a progress toast at once
const event = { priority: "event" as const, debounceMs: 0 };

/** Every input, message handler, queue and toast for tagging. Called by App, which never
 * unmounts: shinyreact drops a message that has no handler (spec §6). */
export function useTagging(library: LibraryView | undefined): Tagging {
  // Mounted once, here, with null defaults; nothing is ever sent on load (spec §5).
  const sendTag = useSetShinyInput<TagRequest | null>("tag_request", null, event);
  const sendRefresh = useSetShinyInput<number | null>("refresh", null, event);
  const sendSnippets = useSetShinyInput<SnippetRequest | null>("snippet_request", null, event);

  const [view, setView] = useState<LibraryView | null>(null);
  const [loadKey, setLoadKey] = useState(0);
  const [marks, setMarks] = useState<Marks>({});
  const [pending, setPending] = useState<TagRequest[]>([]);
  const [confirm, setConfirm] = useState<ConfirmState | null>(null);
  const [revealed, setRevealed] = useState({ key: 0, ids: [] as string[] });
  const [lastTagged, setLastTagged] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [sessionTags, setSessionTags] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [picker, setPicker] = useState<PickerTarget | null>(null);
  const [snippetVersion, setSnippetVersion] = useState(0);

  const queue = useRef<Queue>(EMPTY_QUEUE);
  const confirmRef = useRef<ConfirmState | null>(null);
  const viewRef = useRef<LibraryView | null>(null);
  const undoStack = useRef<string[]>([]);
  const refreshes = useRef(0);
  const drag = useRef<string[] | null>(null);
  const cells = useRef({ changes: 0, tokens: [] as string[] });
  const stillShown = useRef<string | null>(null);
  const snippets = useRef(new Map<string, string | null>());
  const snippetAsked = useRef(new Set<string>());
  const snippetInFlight = useRef<string | null>(null);
  const snippetWanted = useRef<string[]>([]);
  viewRef.current = view;

  // The full library arrives on load and on Refresh: the merged copy and its marks are dropped.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!library) return;
    setView(library);
    setMarks({});
    setLoadKey((k) => k + 1);
    cells.current = { changes: 0, tokens: [] };
  }, [library]);

  const pump = useCallback(() => {
    const { queue: next, send } = takeNext(queue.current, Date.now());
    queue.current = next;
    if (send) sendTag(send);
    setPending(pendingRequests(next));
  }, [sendTag]);

  const submit = useCallback((request: TagRequest, origin: Origin) => {
    if (!viewRef.current?.can_tag) {
      showAlert("cannot-tag", [viewRef.current?.cannot_tag_reason ?? "Tagging is off."]);
      return;
    }
    queue.current = enqueue(queue.current, request, origin);
    if (request.ids.length >= PROGRESS_FROM) showProgress(request, 0, request.ids.length);
    pump();
  }, [pump]);

  const tag = useCallback((action: "add" | "remove", ids: string[], tags: string[], origin: Origin) => {
    if (ids.length === 0 || tags.length === 0) return;
    if (action === "add") setSessionTags((s) => [...s, ...tags.filter((t) => !s.includes(t))]);
    submit({ request_id: newRequestId(), action, ids, tags, confirm_ids: [] }, origin);
  }, [submit]);

  const undo = useCallback((token: string) => {
    submit({ request_id: newRequestId(), action: "undo", ids: [], tags: [], undo: token, confirm_ids: [] }, { kind: "undo" });
  }, [submit]);

  const undoLast = useCallback(() => {
    const token = undoStack.current.pop();
    if (token) undo(token);
  }, [undo]);

  const refresh = useCallback(() => {
    refreshes.current += 1;
    sendRefresh(refreshes.current);
  }, [sendRefresh]);

  const answerConfirm = useCallback((remove: boolean) => {
    const c = confirmRef.current;
    if (!c) return; // Radix also reports the close after Remove: only the first answer counts
    confirmRef.current = null;
    setConfirm(null);
    if (remove) {
      // A new request_id, the same undo token for an Undo, and the IDs the server named.
      const resend = { ...c.request, request_id: newRequestId(), confirm_ids: c.reply.loses_private?.ids ?? [] };
      queue.current = enqueueFront(queue.current, resend, c.origin);
    }
    queue.current = resume(queue.current);
    pump();
  }, [pump]);

  useShinyMessageHandler<TagResult>("tag_result", (reply) => {
    const { queue: next, finished } = heard(queue.current, reply, Date.now());
    queue.current = next;
    if (reply.status === "progress") {
      if (next.inFlight && next.inFlight.request.ids.length >= PROGRESS_FROM) {
        showProgress(next.inFlight.request, reply.done_so_far, reply.total);
      }
      return;
    }
    if (!finished) return; // another window's, or a request already ended
    const { request, origin } = finished;
    if (reply.status === "confirm") {
      queue.current = pause(queue.current);
      confirmRef.current = { request, origin, reply };
      setConfirm(confirmRef.current);
      setPending(pendingRequests(queue.current));
      return;
    }
    setView((v) => (v ? mergeReply(v, reply) : v));
    setMarks((m) => markReply(m, reply));
    if (request.action === "undo") {
      undoStack.current = undoStack.current.filter((t) => t !== request.undo);
      setRevealed((r) => ({ key: r.key + 1, ids: reply.rows.map((row) => row.id) }));
    } else if (reply.undo) {
      undoStack.current = [...undoStack.current, reply.undo].slice(-UNDO_STACK);
    }
    if (request.action === "add" && reply.changed > 0) {
      setLastTagged(request.tags.length === 1 ? request.tags[0] : `${request.tags.length} tags`);
    }
    const saves = viewRef.current?.saves ?? true;
    if (origin.kind === "cell" && reply.status === "done" && reply.failures.length === 0 && reply.undo) {
      cells.current = { changes: cells.current.changes + 1, tokens: [...cells.current.tokens, reply.undo] };
      showCells(cells.current.changes, () => {
        const token = cells.current.tokens.pop();
        if (token) {
          cells.current = { ...cells.current, changes: Math.max(0, cells.current.changes - 1) };
          undo(token);
        }
      });
    } else {
      showResult(request, reply, saves, undo);
    }
    pump();
  });

  // "Still working…" after 30 s with no reply and no progress; never a re-send (spec §6).
  useEffect(() => {
    const timer = window.setInterval(() => {
      const inFlight = queue.current.inFlight;
      if (inFlight && stillShown.current !== inFlight.request.request_id && stillWorking(queue.current, Date.now())) {
        stillShown.current = inFlight.request.request_id;
        showStillWorking(inFlight.request);
      }
    }, 5000);
    return () => window.clearInterval(timer);
  }, []);

  // Shiny's reconnect stays off: on a lost connection the queue is dropped, and the toast says so.
  useEffect(() => {
    const $ = (window as unknown as { jQuery?: (el: Document) => { on(e: string, f: () => void): void; off(e: string, f: () => void): void } }).jQuery;
    if (!$) return;
    const lost = () => {
      const { queue: next, dropped, inFlight } = dropAll(queue.current);
      queue.current = next;
      confirmRef.current = null;
      setConfirm(null);
      setPending([]);
      showDropped(dropped, inFlight);
    };
    $(document).on("shiny:disconnected", lost);
    return () => $(document).off("shiny:disconnected", lost);
  }, []);

  // Snippets: one request at a time, at most 200 IDs, kept in memory only (spec §5).
  const pumpSnippets = useCallback(() => {
    if (snippetInFlight.current) return;
    const ids = snippetsToFetch(snippetWanted.current, snippets.current, snippetAsked.current);
    if (ids.length === 0) return;
    const request_id = newRequestId();
    snippetInFlight.current = request_id;
    for (const id of ids) snippetAsked.current.add(id);
    sendSnippets({ request_id, ids });
  }, [sendSnippets]);

  const wantSnippets = useCallback((ids: string[]) => {
    snippetWanted.current = ids;
    pumpSnippets();
  }, [pumpSnippets]);

  useShinyMessageHandler<SnippetResult>("snippets", (reply) => {
    if (reply.request_id !== snippetInFlight.current) return;
    snippetInFlight.current = null;
    for (const [id, text] of Object.entries(reply.snippets)) snippets.current.set(id, text);
    setSnippetVersion((v) => v + 1);
    pumpSnippets();
  });

  const privateTags = useMemo(() => new Set((view?.tags ?? []).filter((t) => t.private).map((t) => t.tag)), [view]);
  const isPrivate = useCallback((t: string) => privateTags.has(t), [privateTags]);
  // snippetVersion re-creates `snippet`, so components that read it draw new snippets.
  const snippet = useCallback((id: string) => snippets.current.get(id), [snippetVersion]); // a new function per reply
  const touched = useMemo(() => Object.values(marks).filter((m) => m.done).length, [marks]);
  const openPicker = useCallback((target: PickerTarget) => setPicker(target), []);
  const closePicker = useCallback(() => setPicker(null), []);

  return useMemo(() => ({
    view, loadKey, marks, pending, confirm, revealed, touched, lastTagged, sessionTags, drag,
    canTag: view?.can_tag ?? false, reason: view?.cannot_tag_reason ?? null, saves: view?.saves ?? true,
    isPrivate, tag, undo, undoLast, refresh, answerConfirm, picker, openPicker, closePicker, snippet, wantSnippets,
  }), [view, loadKey, marks, pending, confirm, revealed, touched, lastTagged, sessionTags, isPrivate, tag, undo, undoLast, refresh, answerConfirm, picker, openPicker, closePicker, snippet, wantSnippets]);
}
```

`packages/ui/frontend/src/tagging/ConfirmDialog.tsx`:
```tsx
import {
  AlertDialog, AlertDialogAction, AlertDialogCancel, AlertDialogContent, AlertDialogDescription,
  AlertDialogFooter, AlertDialogHeader, AlertDialogTitle,
} from "@/components/ui/alert-dialog";
import { confirmBody, confirmTitle } from "@/lib/toasts";

import { Rich } from "./ActionToast";
import { useTaggingContext } from "./useTagging";

/** The privacy confirmation (spec §6). It opens only when the server replies `confirm`, over the
 * picker if one is open, without closing it. Cancel has focus and Esc means Cancel; Remove
 * resends with confirm_ids set to the IDs the server named. Radix returns focus on close. */
export function ConfirmDialog() {
  const tagging = useTaggingContext();
  const c = tagging.confirm;
  const lp = c?.reply.loses_private ?? null;
  return (
    <AlertDialog open={c !== null} onOpenChange={(open) => { if (!open) tagging.answerConfirm(false); }}>
      {c && lp && (
        <AlertDialogContent data-testid="confirm-dialog">
          <AlertDialogHeader>
            <AlertDialogTitle><Rich text={confirmTitle(c.request, lp, tagging.isPrivate)} /></AlertDialogTitle>
            <AlertDialogDescription>{confirmBody(lp)}</AlertDialogDescription>
          </AlertDialogHeader>
          <AlertDialogFooter>
            <AlertDialogCancel>Cancel</AlertDialogCancel>
            <AlertDialogAction onClick={() => tagging.answerConfirm(true)}>Remove</AlertDialogAction>
          </AlertDialogFooter>
        </AlertDialogContent>
      )}
    </AlertDialog>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Write the chips**

`packages/ui/frontend/src/components/TagChips.tsx`:
```tsx
import { X } from "lucide-react";
import type { ReactNode } from "react";

import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
import type { ShownTag } from "@/lib/library";

/** After × removes a chip, focus moves to the next control in the row: the next ×, or the + (spec
 * §6). Controls carry data-control; their container carries data-controls. */
export function nextControl(button: HTMLElement): HTMLElement | null {
  const controls = [...(button.closest("[data-controls]")?.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>("[data-control]") ?? [])];
  const at = controls.indexOf(button);
  return controls[at + 1] ?? controls[at - 1] ?? null;
}

/** A recording's chips, each with an × that removes that tag from that recording (spec §6). In a
 * row, the controls are reached with Left and Right (tabindex -1); in the header, with Tab. A
 * pending chip shows its new state at once, marked pending. The full tag is in the chip's title
 * and in the ×'s name, so a long tag can be truncated (Review Focus 5). */
export function TagChips({ tags, title, canTag, place, onRemove, children }: {
  tags: ShownTag[]; title: string; canTag: boolean; place: "row" | "header";
  onRemove: (tag: string, next: HTMLElement | null) => void; children?: ReactNode;
}) {
  return (
    <div className="chips" data-testid="chips" data-controls="">
      {tags.map((t) => (
        <span
          key={t.tag}
          className={cn("chip", t.by === "auto" && "auto", t.tag.startsWith("notes/") && "nt", t.pending && "pending", t.pending === "remove" && "pending-remove")}
          data-testid="chip"
          data-tag={t.tag}
          title={t.tag}
        >
          <span className="chip-label">
            {t.tag}
            {t.by === "auto" ? " · auto" : ""}
          </span>
          {t.pending && <span className="sr-only">{t.pending === "add" ? " (adding)" : " (removing)"}</span>}
          {canTag && !t.pending && (
            <button
              type="button"
              className="chip-x"
              data-control="remove"
              tabIndex={place === "row" ? -1 : 0}
              aria-label={`Remove tag ${t.tag} from ${title}`}
              onClick={(e) => {
                e.stopPropagation(); // never a row click: no selection change, nothing opens
                onRemove(t.tag, nextControl(e.currentTarget));
              }}
            >
              <X aria-hidden="true" />
            </button>
          )}
        </span>
      ))}
      {children}
    </div>
  );
}
```

- Add, above `RecordingDetail`:
```tsx
/** The header's chips read the open recording's row in the merged library, never the `recording`
 * output, which isn't re-sent for a tag change because it carries the whole transcript (spec §5). */
function HeaderChips({ rec, row }: { rec: RecordingView; row: LibraryRow | null }) {
  const tagging = useTaggingContext();
  const tags = row ? shownTags(row, tagging.pending) : rec.tags;
  return (
    <TagChips
      tags={tags}
      title={row?.title ?? rec.title}
      canTag={tagging.canTag && row !== null}
      place="header"
      onRemove={(tag, next) => {
        next?.focus();
        tagging.tag("remove", [rec.id], [tag], { kind: "header" });
      }}
    />
  );
}
```
with the imports `import { shownTags } from "../lib/library";`, `import { useTaggingContext } from "../tagging/useTagging";` and `import { TagChips } from "./TagChips";`.

Replace `packages/ui/frontend/src/App.tsx` with:
```tsx
import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";

import { Toaster } from "@/components/ui/sonner";

import { RecordingList } from "./components/RecordingList";
import { RecordingPane } from "./components/RecordingPane";
import { Sidebar } from "./components/Sidebar";
import { StatusPage } from "./components/StatusPage";
import { TopBar } from "./components/TopBar";
import { hashFor, routeFromHash } from "./lib/nav";
import { filterRows } from "./lib/transcript";
import { useShinyInitialized, useShinyInput, useShinyOutputStatus, useShinyOutputValue } from "./sr";
import { ConfirmDialog } from "./tagging/ConfirmDialog";
import { TaggingContext, useTagging } from "./tagging/useTagging";
import type { Filter, LibraryView, Page, Route } from "./types";

export default function App() {
  const initialized = useShinyInitialized();
  const library = useShinyOutputValue<LibraryView>("library");
  // Every page's output lives as long as the app. shinyreact 0.1.0 removes an unmounted output's
  // element before Shiny unbinds it, so Shiny never does, and each later value for that output
  // logs "Output <id> not found". Held here, no output is ever unmounted. A new output gets a
  // line here too (docs/upstream-issues.md). Message handlers live here too, in useTagging.
  useShinyOutputStatus("recording");
  useShinyOutputStatus("status");
  const tagging = useTagging(library);
  const [selectedId, setSelectedId] = useShinyInput<string | null>("selected_id", null);
  const [filter, setFilter] = useState<Filter>({ kind: "all" });
  const [route, setRoute] = useState<Route>(() => routeFromHash(window.location.hash));
  const view = tagging.view;
  const rows = useMemo(() => (view ? filterRows(view.recordings, filter) : []), [view, filter]);
  const openRow = useMemo(() => view?.recordings.find((r) => r.id === selectedId) ?? null, [view, selectedId]);

  // Hash routes: Back returns to the page you came from, and the server never logs them. The hash
  // only chooses what to show: `#/library/<id>` opens that recording, read-only (spec §7, §15).
  useEffect(() => {
    const onHash = () => setRoute(routeFromHash(window.location.hash));
    window.addEventListener("hashchange", onHash);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("hashchange", onHash);
  }, []);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (initialized && route.open) setSelectedId(route.open);
  }, [initialized, route.open, setSelectedId]);
  const go = (next: Page) => {
    window.location.hash = hashFor(next);
    setRoute({ page: next, open: null });
  };

  if (!initialized) return null; // no flash of empty defaults during connection
  return (
    <TaggingContext.Provider value={tagging}>
      <div className="app">
        <TopBar page={route.page} onPage={go} />
        {route.page === "status" ? <StatusPage /> : view ? (
          <main className="panes">
            <Sidebar library={view} filter={filter} onFilter={setFilter} />
            <RecordingList rows={rows} selectedId={selectedId} onSelect={setSelectedId} />
            <RecordingPane selectedId={selectedId} row={openRow} />
          </main>
        ) : <p className="empty">Loading…</p>}
        <ConfirmDialog />
        <Toaster position="bottom-right" />
      </div>
    </TaggingContext.Provider>
  );
}
```

```css
/* Chips with × (tagging spec §6). The × has a hit area of at least 24×24 px, padded beyond the
   glyph, and shows on hover or focus. A long tag is truncated; its full name is in the chip's
   title and the ×'s accessible name. */
.chips { display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; align-items: center; gap: 4px; min-width: 0; }
.chips .chip { display: inline-flex; align-items: center; gap: 2px; max-width: 100%; margin: 0; }
.chip-label { overflow: hidden; text-overflow: ellipsis; white-space: nowrap; max-width: 28ch; }
.chip-x { display: inline-grid; place-items: center; min-width: 24px; min-height: 24px; margin: -6px -8px -6px -2px; padding: 0; border: 0; border-radius: 999px; background: none; color: inherit; cursor: pointer; opacity: 0; }
.chip-x svg { width: 12px; height: 12px; }
.chip:hover .chip-x, .chip-x:focus-visible, .row:focus-within .chip-x { opacity: 1; }
/* A pending change shows at once, with a dashed outline in --muted-foreground (spec §6). */
.chip.pending { outline: 1px dashed var(--muted-foreground); outline-offset: 1px; }
.chip.pending-remove .chip-label { text-decoration: line-through; }
.action-toast { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 12px; width: 100%; }
.alert-toast { display: grid; gap: 4px; width: 100%; }
.alert-toast p { margin: 0; }
.toast-undo { margin-left: auto; min-height: 24px; padding: 4px 6px; border: 0; background: none; color: var(--primary); font: inherit; font-weight: 700; cursor: pointer; }
```

```python
"""Servers for the browser tests. A test that writes gets a server of its own (tagging spec §9),
started with TMPDIR=<its tmp_path>, so it finds the demo's temporary copy and reads what the app
wrote there, through Archive."""

import os
import socket
import subprocess
import sys
import time
import urllib.request
from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path

import pytest
from recordings.archive import Archive


def _free_port() -> int:
    with socket.socket() as s:
        s.bind(("127.0.0.1", 0))
        return s.getsockname()[1]


@dataclass
class Server:
    url: str
    tmp: Path
    proc: subprocess.Popen
    log: Path

    @property
    def copy(self) -> Path:
        (found,) = self.tmp.glob("recordings-demo-*/archive")
        return found

    def tags(self, rid: str) -> list[tuple[str, str]]:
        """The tags on disk, read through Archive: tag and `by` only (spec §9)."""
        return [(t.tag, t.by) for t in Archive(self.copy).load(rid).tags]

    def stop(self) -> None:
        self.proc.terminate()
        try:
            self.proc.wait(timeout=10)
        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
            self.proc.kill()
            self.proc.wait()


def start_server(tmp: Path, archive: Path | None = None) -> Server:
    tmp.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    port = _free_port()
    env = {k: v for k, v in os.environ.items() if not k.startswith("RECORDINGS_")}
    env["TMPDIR"] = str(tmp)
    if archive is not None:
        env["RECORDINGS_DEMO_ARCHIVE"] = str(archive)
    log = tmp / "server.log"
    with log.open("wb") as out:
        proc = subprocess.Popen(
            [sys.executable, "-m", "recordings_ui", "--demo", "--port", str(port)],
            env=env, stdout=out, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
        )
    url = f"http://127.0.0.1:{port}"
    deadline = time.monotonic() + 30
    while True:
        if proc.poll() is not None:
            pytest.fail(f"demo server exited with {proc.returncode}:\n{log.read_text(errors='replace')[-2000:]}")
        try:
            with urllib.request.urlopen(f"{url}/healthz", timeout=1):
                break
        except OSError:
            if time.monotonic() > deadline:
                proc.kill()
                pytest.fail(f"demo server did not start:\n{log.read_text(errors='replace')[-2000:]}")
            time.sleep(0.2)
    return Server(url, tmp, proc, log)


@pytest.fixture
def server(tmp_path):
    started = start_server(tmp_path / "server")
    yield started
    started.stop()


@pytest.fixture
def console(page):
    """Fails the test on any console error or uncaught exception (spec §9: a clean console)."""
    errors: list[str] = []
    page.on("console", lambda m: errors.append(m.text) if m.type == "error" else None)
    page.on("pageerror", lambda e: errors.append(f"uncaught: {e}"))
    yield errors
    assert errors == [], f"console errors: {errors}"
```

```python
"""Browser tests that tag (tagging spec §9). Each gets a server of its own, so what one test
writes never shows in another; each reads the files the app wrote, through Archive."""

import re
import time

import pytest
from playwright.sync_api import Page, expect

pytestmark = pytest.mark.e2e


def wait_until(check, timeout: float = 10.0) -> None:
    deadline = time.monotonic() + timeout
    while not check():
        assert time.monotonic() < deadline, "timed out"
        time.sleep(0.05)


def open_library(page: Page, url: str) -> None:
    page.goto(url)
    expect(page.get_by_test_id("recording-row")).to_have_count(4)


def open_row(page: Page, text: str) -> None:
    page.get_by_test_id("recording-row").filter(has_text=text).click()
    expect(page.get_by_test_id("recording-header")).to_contain_text(text)


def test_the_headers_x_removes_a_tag_and_undo_puts_it_back(page: Page, server, console, demo_ids):
    jfk = demo_ids["jfk-rice"]
    open_library(page, server.url)
    open_row(page, "JFK")
    header = page.get_by_test_id("recording-header")
    header.locator("[data-testid=chip][data-tag=talks]").hover()
    header.get_by_role("button", name=re.compile(r"^Remove tag talks from JFK")).click()
    toast = page.get_by_test_id("action-toast")
    expect(toast).to_contain_text("Removed talks from 1 recording")
    expect(header.locator("[data-testid=chip][data-tag=talks]")).to_have_count(0)
    assert server.tags(jfk) == [("notes/conference-talk", "auto")]
    toast.get_by_role("button", name="Undo").click()
    expect(header.locator("[data-testid=chip][data-tag=talks]")).to_have_count(1)
    wait_until(lambda: server.tags(jfk) == [("talks", "you"), ("notes/conference-talk", "auto")])


def test_removing_a_last_private_tag_asks_first(page: Page, server, console, demo_ids):
    fdr = demo_ids["fdr-fireside-1"]
    open_library(page, server.url)
    open_row(page, "Fireside")
    header = page.get_by_test_id("recording-header")
    remove = header.get_by_role("button", name=re.compile(r"^Remove tag private from Fireside"))
    remove.click()
    dialog = page.get_by_role("alertdialog")
    expect(dialog).to_contain_text("Remove private from 1 recording?")
    expect(dialog).to_contain_text("1 will have no tags left, and stays hidden until its next tag.")
    expect(dialog.get_by_role("button", name="Cancel")).to_be_focused()
    page.keyboard.press("Escape")  # Esc means Cancel
    expect(dialog).to_have_count(0)
    expect(header.locator("[data-testid=chip][data-tag=private]")).to_have_count(1)
    assert server.tags(fdr) == [("private", "you")]
    remove.click()
    page.get_by_role("alertdialog").get_by_role("button", name="Remove").click()
    expect(page.get_by_test_id("action-toast")).to_contain_text("Removed private from 1 recording")
    wait_until(lambda: server.tags(fdr) == [])
```

---
### Task 11: The tag picker: the dialog for `T`, and the + popover

**Checkpoint lens:** UX and accessibility (Enter adds only; Esc layering; focus returning to the input after the confirmation).

The picker has two forms (spec §6): a centred Command dialog (opened by `T`, the selection bar or a drop; Task 12 and 13 open it) and a Popover on a **+** (rows and the header). Both draw the same body. This task adds the body, both forms, and the header's **+**.

Checked before writing (CLAUDE.md): shadcn's Command (`npx shadcn@4.21.4 docs command` and the `view` output: `CommandDialog` takes `title` and puts it in an sr-only `DialogTitle`; items sit inside `CommandGroup`s); cmdk through context7 (`/dip/cmdk`: `shouldFilter={false}` renders items as given; the root `onKeyDown` runs first and cmdk skips its own Enter when the event is `defaultPrevented`; `value`/`onValueChange` control the highlighted item); shadcn's Popover (`PopoverContent` takes Radix's `onFocusOutside` and `onInteractOutside`); Radix's dismissable layer (a focus move into a layer above, such as the Alert Dialog, counts as "outside" for a Popover, so the Popover must refuse it while the confirmation is open).

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/ui/frontend/src/components/TagPicker.tsx`
- Modify: `packages/ui/frontend/src/components/RecordingPane.tsx` (the header's +), `src/App.tsx` (the dialog form), `src/app.css`
- Test: `packages/ui/frontend/src/components/TagPicker.test.ts`, `packages/ui/tests/e2e/test_tagging.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `lib/picker.ts` (Task 9); `Tagging` (Task 10: `view`, `sessionTags`, `tag`, `confirm`, `picker`, `closePicker`); `@/components/ui/command`, `@/components/ui/popover` (Task 8).
- Produces:
  - `pickerTitle(ids: readonly string[], titleOf: (id: string) => string): string` ("Tag Week 4", "Tag 12 recordings")
  - `PickerBody({ ids, title })`: the shared body (`data-testid="picker-input"`, `"picker-item"` with `data-tag` and `data-state`, `"picker-create"`, `"picker-note"`)
  - `TagPickerDialog()`: the dialog form, driven by `tagging.picker`; mounted once in `App`
  - `AddTagsButton({ id, title, place })`: the + and its Popover; `data-control="add"`, `data-testid="add-tag"`, named "Add tags to <title>"

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the picker**

`packages/ui/frontend/src/components/TagPicker.tsx`:
```tsx
import { Lock, Plus, X } from "lucide-react";
import { useMemo, useState, type KeyboardEvent } from "react";

import { Command, CommandDialog, CommandEmpty, CommandGroup, CommandInput, CommandItem, CommandList } from "@/components/ui/command";
import { Popover, PopoverContent, PopoverTrigger } from "@/components/ui/popover";
import { alreadyWords, createOffer, pickerTags, rankTags, stateWords, tagState, type PickerTag } from "@/lib/picker";
import { useTaggingContext } from "@/tagging/useTagging";

export function pickerTitle(ids: readonly string[], titleOf: (id: string) => string): string {
  return ids.length === 1 ? `Tag ${titleOf(ids[0])}` : `Tag ${ids.length.toLocaleString("en")} recordings`;
}

const TAG = "tag:";
const CREATE = "create:";

/** The picker's body, shared by both forms (spec §6).
 * - Enter only ever adds: the highlighted tag, then the box clears and the picker stays open. On
 *   a ✓ item it says "Already on all 12".
 * - Shift+Enter creates exactly the typed text (trimmed, NFC), when Create is offered.
 * - Shift+Delete, or Cmd/Ctrl+Backspace, removes the highlighted ✓ or – tag from the targets;
 *   the × on the item does the same with the mouse.
 * - Esc closes (Radix). States are in words as well as glyphs; private tags are grouped under a
 *   lock, by the server's flags. */
export function PickerBody({ ids, title }: { ids: string[]; title: string }) {
  const tagging = useTaggingContext();
  const [query, setQuery] = useState("");
  const [value, setValue] = useState("");
  const [note, setNote] = useState("");
  const view = tagging.view;
  const all = useMemo(() => (view ? pickerTags(view, tagging.sessionTags) : []), [view, tagging.sessionTags]);
  const targets = useMemo(() => {
    const wanted = new Set(ids);
    return view ? view.recordings.filter((r) => wanted.has(r.id)) : [];
  }, [view, ids]);
  const ranked = rankTags(all, query);
  const create = createOffer(all, query);

  const add = (tag: string) => {
    const state = tagState(tag, targets);
    if (state.state === "all") {
      setNote(alreadyWords(state));
      return;
    }
    tagging.tag("add", ids, [tag], { kind: "picker" });
    setQuery("");
    setNote("");
  };
  const remove = (tag: string) => {
    if (tagState(tag, targets).state === "none") return;
    tagging.tag("remove", ids, [tag], { kind: "picker" });
    setNote("");
  };
  const highlighted = value.startsWith(TAG) ? value.slice(TAG.length) : null;

  const onKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {
    if (e.key === "Enter" && e.shiftKey) {
      e.preventDefault(); // cmdk then skips its own Enter
      if (create) add(create);
      else if (query.trim()) setNote("That tag exists: Enter adds it.");
      return;
    }
    const removing = (e.key === "Delete" && e.shiftKey) || (e.key === "Backspace" && (e.metaKey || e.ctrlKey));
    if (removing && highlighted && tagState(highlighted, targets).state !== "none") {
      e.preventDefault();
      remove(highlighted);
    }
  };

  const item = (t: PickerTag) => {
    const state = tagState(t.tag, targets);
    const words = stateWords(state);
    return (
      <CommandItem key={t.tag} value={`${TAG}${t.tag}`} onSelect={() => add(t.tag)}
        data-testid="picker-item" data-tag={t.tag} data-state={state.state}>
        <span className="picker-glyph" aria-hidden="true">{state.state === "all" ? "✓" : state.state === "some" ? "–" : ""}</span>
        <span className="picker-tag" title={t.tag}>{t.tag}</span>
        {words && <span className="picker-state">{words}</span>}
        {state.state !== "none" && (
          // The mouse's way to remove; the keyboard's is Shift+Delete. Not a button inside the
          // option (axe: nested-interactive), so it is hidden from assistive technology.
          <span className="picker-x" aria-hidden="true" onPointerDown={(e) => e.preventDefault()}
            onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); remove(t.tag); }}>
            <X />
          </span>
        )}
      </CommandItem>
    );
  };

  const open = ranked.filter((t) => !t.private);
  const locked = ranked.filter((t) => t.private);
  return (
    <Command shouldFilter={false} value={value} onValueChange={setValue} onKeyDown={onKeyDown} label={title} className="picker">
      <div className="picker-title" aria-hidden="true">{title}</div>
      <CommandInput value={query} onValueChange={(q) => { setQuery(q); setNote(""); }} placeholder="Find or create a tag" data-testid="picker-input" />
      <CommandList>
        <CommandEmpty>No tag matches.</CommandEmpty>
        {open.length > 0 && <CommandGroup heading="Tags">{open.map(item)}</CommandGroup>}
        {locked.length > 0 && (
          <CommandGroup heading={<span className="picker-lock"><Lock aria-hidden="true" /> Private</span>}>{locked.map(item)}</CommandGroup>
        )}
        {create && (
          <CommandGroup heading="New">
            <CommandItem value={`${CREATE}${create}`} onSelect={() => add(create)} data-testid="picker-create">
              Create “{create}”
            </CommandItem>
          </CommandGroup>
        )}
      </CommandList>
      <p className="picker-note" data-testid="picker-note" role="status">{note}</p>
      <p className="picker-keys">Enter add · Shift+Enter create · Shift+Delete remove · Esc close</p>
    </Command>
  );
}

/** The dialog form: `T`, the selection bar's Tag…, a drop (spec §6). Radix returns focus to
 * where it came from when it closes. */
export function TagPickerDialog() {
  const tagging = useTaggingContext();
  const target = tagging.picker;
  return (
    <CommandDialog open={target !== null} onOpenChange={(open) => { if (!open) tagging.closePicker(); }}
      title={target?.title ?? "Tag"} description="Find, add, create or remove tags" className="picker-dialog">
      {target && <PickerBody ids={target.ids} title={target.title} />}
    </CommandDialog>
  );
}

/** The + after a row's chips, and in the header: the picker for that one recording, as a Popover
 * on the +, whose row stays rendered (spec §6). Clicking it never changes the selection or opens
 * the row. */
export function AddTagsButton({ id, title, place }: { id: string; title: string; place: "row" | "header" }) {
  const tagging = useTaggingContext();
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  // The confirmation opens over the picker without closing it (spec §6): a focus move into the
  // Alert Dialog is "outside" this Popover, so it is refused while the confirmation is open.
  const keepOpen = (e: Event) => { if (tagging.confirm) e.preventDefault(); };
  return (
    <Popover open={open} onOpenChange={setOpen}>
      <PopoverTrigger asChild>
        <button type="button" className="add-tag" data-control="add" data-testid="add-tag"
          tabIndex={place === "row" ? -1 : 0} aria-label={`Add tags to ${title}`}
          onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}>
          <Plus aria-hidden="true" />
        </button>
      </PopoverTrigger>
      <PopoverContent className="picker-popover p-0" align="start" aria-label={`Tag ${title}`}
        onFocusOutside={keepOpen} onInteractOutside={keepOpen}>
        <PickerBody ids={[id]} title={`Tag ${title}`} />
      </PopoverContent>
    </Popover>
  );
}
```

```css
/* The tag picker (tagging spec §6). */
.picker { width: 100%; }
.picker-title { padding: 10px 12px 2px; font-weight: 700; }
.picker-glyph { width: 1em; text-align: center; }
.picker-tag { min-width: 0; overflow: hidden; text-overflow: ellipsis; white-space: nowrap; }
.picker-state { margin-left: auto; color: var(--muted-foreground); font-size: 12px; white-space: nowrap; }
.picker-x { display: inline-grid; place-items: center; min-width: 24px; min-height: 24px; border-radius: 999px; }

.picker-x svg { width: 12px; height: 12px; }
.picker-lock { display: inline-flex; align-items: center; gap: 4px; }
.picker-lock svg { width: 12px; height: 12px; }
.picker-note { min-height: 1.4em; margin: 0; padding: 2px 12px; color: var(--muted-foreground); font-size: 12px; }
.picker-keys { margin: 0; padding: 6px 12px 10px; border-top: 1px solid var(--border); color: var(--muted-foreground); font-size: 11.5px; }
.picker-popover { width: min(340px, calc(100vw - 32px)); }
.add-tag { display: inline-grid; place-items: center; min-width: 24px; min-height: 24px; padding: 0; border: 1px dashed var(--border); border-radius: 999px; background: none; color: var(--muted-foreground); cursor: pointer; }
```

```python
def test_the_plus_adds_several_tags_creates_removes_and_stays_open(page: Page, server, console, demo_ids):
    apollo13 = demo_ids["apollo13-problem"]
    open_library(page, server.url)
    open_row(page, "Apollo 13")
    page.get_by_test_id("recording-header").get_by_role("button", name=re.compile(r"^Add tags to Apollo 13")).click()
    box = page.get_by_test_id("picker-input")
    expect(box).to_be_focused()
    box.fill("lectures")
    box.press("Shift+Enter")  # creates exactly the typed text
    expect(box).to_have_value("")
    box.fill("apol")
    box.press("Enter")  # Enter on a partial name picks the existing tag, never Create
    expect(box).to_be_visible()  # the picker stays open for the next tag
    wait_until(lambda: server.tags(apollo13) == [("lectures", "you"), ("space/apollo", "you")])
    box.fill("apollo")
    item = page.locator("[data-testid=picker-item][data-tag='space/apollo']")
    expect(item).to_have_attribute("data-state", "all")
    expect(item).to_contain_text("on it")
    box.press("Enter")
    expect(page.get_by_test_id("picker-note")).to_have_text("Already on it")
    box.press("Shift+Delete")  # removes the highlighted tag
    wait_until(lambda: server.tags(apollo13) == [("lectures", "you")])
    box.fill("Lectures")
    expect(page.get_by_test_id("picker-create")).to_have_count(0)  # never a case-variant
    box.fill("notes/x")
    expect(page.get_by_test_id("picker-create")).to_have_count(0)  # never a notes/ tag
    box.press("Escape")
    expect(box).to_have_count(0)
    header = page.get_by_test_id("recording-header")
    expect(header.locator("[data-testid=chip]")).to_have_count(1)
```

### Task 12: The Library list rebuilt: a virtual ARIA grid with Finder selection, row controls, `T`, Refresh

**Checkpoint lens:** UX and accessibility (keyboard model, focus through virtualisation, row states without colour alone).

The list's rows stop being `<button>`s, so each can hold its own controls (the `RecordingList` half of the main spec's `<div>`-inside-`<button>` fix). The list becomes an ARIA grid drawn by TanStack Virtual, with Finder selection, the row's × and +, `T`, the selection bar, Refresh (`R` and a button) and the one-line key hint. Rows stay put, marked done, until Refresh or a filter change (spec §6).

Checked before writing (CLAUDE.md): TanStack Virtual through context7 (`/websites/tanstack_virtual`: `useVirtualizer({ count, getScrollElement, estimateSize, overscan, getItemKey, rangeExtractor, useFlushSync })`, `defaultRangeExtractor(range)`, `measureElement` as a ref with `data-index`, `scrollToIndex(i, { align })`); WAI-ARIA's grid pattern (`role="grid"`, `aria-multiselectable`, `aria-rowcount` counting the header row, `aria-rowindex` from 1, `aria-selected` on rows); Playwright's `ControlOrMeta` modifier.

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/ui/frontend/src/lib/useRowOrder.ts`, `src/lib/useMediaQuery.ts`, `src/components/SelectionBar.tsx`
- Replace: `packages/ui/frontend/src/components/RecordingList.tsx`
- Modify: `packages/ui/frontend/src/App.tsx`, `src/app.css`
- Modify: `packages/ui/tests/e2e/conftest.py` (the 300-recording archive), `packages/ui/tests/e2e/test_tagging.py`
- Test: `packages/ui/frontend/src/components/SelectionBar.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `lib/selection.ts`, `lib/keys.ts`, `lib/library.ts` (Task 9); `Tagging` (Task 10); `TagChips` (Task 10); `AddTagsButton`, `pickerTitle` (Task 11); `showAlert` (Task 10).
- Produces:
  - `useRowOrder(view, filter, loadKey, revealed) -> RowOrder { ids: string[]; key: number; reason: "load" | "filter" | "reveal" }`
  - `useMediaQuery(query: string): boolean`; `PHONE = "(max-width: 760px)"`; `NO_HOVER = "(hover: none)"`
  - `SelectionBar({ count, lastTagged, onTag, onClear })` (`data-testid="selection-bar"`)
  - `RecordingList({ rows, sel, act, openId, onOpen, announce })`: `data-testid="recording-list"` (`role="grid"`), rows `data-testid="recording-row"` with `data-id`, `aria-rowindex`, `aria-selected`, `aria-current`; `data-testid="refresh"`, `"cannot-tag"`; exports `KEY_HINT`, `openPickerFor(ids, rows, tagging)`

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the hooks and the selection bar**

`packages/ui/frontend/src/lib/useMediaQuery.ts`:
```ts
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

export const PHONE = "(max-width: 760px)";
export const NO_HOVER = "(hover: none)";

export function useMediaQuery(query: string): boolean {
  const [matches, setMatches] = useState(() => window.matchMedia(query).matches);
  useEffect(() => {
    const media = window.matchMedia(query);
    const sync = () => setMatches(media.matches);
    sync();
    media.addEventListener("change", sync);
    return () => media.removeEventListener("change", sync);
  }, [query]);
  return matches;
}
```

`packages/ui/frontend/src/lib/useRowOrder.ts`:
```ts
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";

import type { Filter, LibraryView } from "../types";
import { orderFor, withIds } from "./library";

export interface RowOrder { ids: string[]; key: number; reason: "load" | "filter" | "reveal" }

/** The rows a filter shows, worked out on load, on Refresh and on a filter change only, so a row
 * whose tags change stays put, marked done (spec §6). An Undo puts the rows it touched back. */
export function useRowOrder(view: LibraryView | null, filter: Filter, loadKey: number, revealed: { key: number; ids: string[] }): RowOrder {
  const [order, setOrder] = useState<RowOrder>({ ids: [], key: 0, reason: "load" });
  const viewRef = useRef(view);
  viewRef.current = view;
  const lastLoad = useRef(-1);
  const filterKey = JSON.stringify(filter);

  useEffect(() => {
    const v = viewRef.current;
    if (!v) return;
    const reason = lastLoad.current !== loadKey ? "load" : "filter";
    lastLoad.current = loadKey;
    const ids = orderFor(v.recordings, JSON.parse(filterKey) as Filter);
    setOrder((o) => ({ ids, key: o.key + 1, reason }));
  }, [loadKey, filterKey]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const v = viewRef.current;
    if (!v || revealed.ids.length === 0) return;
    setOrder((o) => ({ ids: withIds(o.ids, revealed.ids, v.recordings), key: o.key + 1, reason: "reveal" }));
  }, [revealed]);

  return order;
}
```

`packages/ui/frontend/src/components/SelectionBar.tsx`:
```tsx
/** "12 selected · Tag… (T) · Clear (Esc)". The selection stays after an action, so a second tag
 * can follow, and the bar adds "just tagged `work`" (spec §6). On phones it sits above the
 * bottom tab bar (app.css). */
export function SelectionBar({ count, lastTagged, onTag, onClear }: {
  count: number; lastTagged: string | null; onTag: () => void; onClear: () => void;
}) {
  if (count < 2) return null;
  return (
    <div className="selection-bar" role="region" aria-label="Selection" data-testid="selection-bar">
      <span>{count.toLocaleString("en")} selected</span>
      <button type="button" onClick={onTag}>Tag{"…"} <kbd>T</kbd></button>
      <button type="button" onClick={onClear}>Clear <kbd>Esc</kbd></button>
      {lastTagged && <span className="muted">just tagged <code>{lastTagged}</code></span>}
    </div>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Rebuild the list**

Replace `packages/ui/frontend/src/components/RecordingList.tsx` with:
```tsx
import { defaultRangeExtractor, useVirtualizer, type Range, type VirtualItem } from "@tanstack/react-virtual";
import { Check, Lock, TriangleAlert } from "lucide-react";
import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, type KeyboardEvent, type MouseEvent } from "react";

import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

import { commandShortcut, isTextEntry, plainShortcut } from "../lib/keys";
import { shownTags } from "../lib/library";
import { targetsOf, type Move, type Sel, type SelAction } from "../lib/selection";
import { showAlert } from "../tagging/toasts";
import { useTaggingContext, type Tagging } from "../tagging/useTagging";
import type { LibraryRow } from "../types";
import { SelectionBar } from "./SelectionBar";
import { TagChips } from "./TagChips";
import { AddTagsButton, pickerTitle } from "./TagPicker";

const ROW_ESTIMATE = 84;
const FOLLOW_MS = 300; // the pane follows focus after a short pause, as Mail's does (spec §6)
export const KEY_HINT = "T tag · Cmd-A all · Esc clear · Cmd-Z undo · R refresh";

/** `T`, the selection bar and a drop open the picker's dialog form; when tagging is off, the
 * reason shows in a toast instead (spec §6). */
export function openPickerFor(ids: string[], rows: readonly LibraryRow[], tagging: Tagging): void {
  if (ids.length === 0) return;
  if (!tagging.canTag) {
    showAlert("cannot-tag", [tagging.reason ?? "Tagging is off."]);
    return;
  }
  const titles = new Map(rows.map((r) => [r.id, r.title]));
  tagging.openPicker({ ids, title: pickerTitle(ids, (id) => titles.get(id) ?? id) });
}

type Props = {
  rows: LibraryRow[]; sel: Sel; act: (action: SelAction) => void;
  openId: string | null; onOpen: (id: string) => void; announce: string;
};

/** The Library list (spec §6): an ARIA grid, one tab stop, drawn virtually. Up and Down move
 * focus and select; Shift extends from the anchor; Cmd/Ctrl moves focus only; Cmd/Ctrl+Space
 * toggles; Page Up and Down, Home and End; Enter opens; Left and Right move across the focused
 * row's controls. Space is left to the player. Selection changes on click, never on mousedown. */
export function RecordingList({ rows, sel, act, openId, onOpen, announce }: Props) {
  const tagging = useTaggingContext();
  const scrollRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const rowEls = useRef(new Map<string, HTMLDivElement>());
  const wantFocus = useRef(false);
  const follow = useRef<number | null>(null);
  const selRef = useRef(sel);
  selRef.current = sel;
  const order = useMemo(() => rows.map((r) => r.id), [rows]);
  const index = useMemo(() => new Map(order.map((id, i) => [id, i])), [order]);
  const focusIdx = sel.focus === null ? -1 : (index.get(sel.focus) ?? -1);
  const anchorIdx = sel.anchor === null ? -1 : (index.get(sel.anchor) ?? -1);

  // The focused row, the anchor (and, in Task 13, a dragged row) always stay rendered.
  const rangeExtractor = useCallback((range: Range) => {
    const keep = [focusIdx, anchorIdx].filter((i) => i >= 0 && i < range.count);
    return [...new Set([...defaultRangeExtractor(range), ...keep])].sort((a, b) => a - b);
  }, [focusIdx, anchorIdx]);

  const virtualizer = useVirtualizer({
    count: rows.length,
    getScrollElement: () => scrollRef.current,
    estimateSize: () => ROW_ESTIMATE,
    overscan: 8,
    getItemKey: (i) => order[i],
    rangeExtractor,
    useFlushSync: false, // the bundled react-dom's flushSync can't flush the global React (spec §6)
  });

  // Moving focus by key scrolls the row into view, then focuses it once it is drawn.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!wantFocus.current || focusIdx < 0) return;
    wantFocus.current = false;
    virtualizer.scrollToIndex(focusIdx, { align: "auto" });
    const id = sel.focus;
    requestAnimationFrame(() => { if (id) rowEls.current.get(id)?.focus({ preventScroll: true }); });
  }, [focusIdx, sel.focus, virtualizer]);

  const page = () => Math.max(1, Math.floor((scrollRef.current?.clientHeight ?? ROW_ESTIMATE * 10) / ROW_ESTIMATE) - 1);
  const openPicker = () => openPickerFor(targetsOf(selRef.current, order), rows, tagging);

  const move = (e: KeyboardEvent, to: Move) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    wantFocus.current = true;
    const meta = e.metaKey || e.ctrlKey;
    act({ type: "move", to, shift: e.shiftKey, meta, page: page() });
    if (!meta && !e.shiftKey) {
      if (follow.current) window.clearTimeout(follow.current);
      follow.current = window.setTimeout(() => { const id = selRef.current.focus; if (id) onOpen(id); }, FOLLOW_MS);
    }
  };

  const across = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
    const row = sel.focus ? rowEls.current.get(sel.focus) : undefined;
    if (!row) return;
    e.preventDefault();
    const controls = [...row.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>("[data-control]:not([data-control=checkbox])")];
    const at = controls.indexOf(document.activeElement as HTMLElement);
    if (e.key === "ArrowRight") (controls[at + 1] ?? controls[at])?.focus();
    else if (at <= 0) row.focus();
    else controls[at - 1].focus();
  };

  const onKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {
    // Keys typed in a portal (the + picker) bubble here through React: not the list's.
    if (!e.currentTarget.contains(e.target as Node) || isTextEntry(e.target) || e.nativeEvent.isComposing) return;
    switch (e.key) {
      case "ArrowDown": return move(e, "next");
      case "ArrowUp": return move(e, "prev");
      case "PageDown": return move(e, "pageDown");
      case "PageUp": return move(e, "pageUp");
      case "Home": return move(e, "first");
      case "End": return move(e, "last");
      case "ArrowLeft":
      case "ArrowRight": return across(e);
      case "Enter":
        if (sel.focus && e.target === rowEls.current.get(sel.focus)) {
          e.preventDefault();
          onOpen(sel.focus);
        }
        return;
      case "Escape":
        if (!e.defaultPrevented) act({ type: "clear" }); // only an Esc nothing else handled
        return;
      case " ":
        if (commandShortcut(e)) {
          e.preventDefault();
          act({ type: "toggleFocused" });
        }
        return; // Space alone is the player's (main spec §12)
    }
    const key = e.key.toLowerCase();
    if (commandShortcut(e) && key === "a") {
      e.preventDefault(); // only here, with focus in the list
      act({ type: "all" });
    } else if (commandShortcut(e) && key === "z" && !e.shiftKey) {
      e.preventDefault();
      tagging.undoLast();
    } else if (plainShortcut(e) && key === "t") {
      e.preventDefault();
      openPicker();
    } else if (plainShortcut(e) && key === "r") {
      e.preventDefault();
      tagging.refresh();
    }
  };

  const onRowClick = (e: MouseEvent<HTMLDivElement>, id: string) => {
    const target = e.target as Element;
    if (!e.currentTarget.contains(target)) return; // a click in a portal (the picker)
    if (target.closest("[data-control]:not([data-control=checkbox])")) return; // × and + never select
    if (target.closest("[data-control=checkbox]")) {
      act({ type: "checkbox", id, shift: e.shiftKey });
      return;
    }
    const meta = e.metaKey || e.ctrlKey;
    act({ type: "click", id, meta, shift: e.shiftKey });
    if (!meta && !e.shiftKey) onOpen(id);
  };

  const drawRow = (v: VirtualItem) => {
    const r = rows[v.index];
    const selected = sel.selected.has(r.id);
    const mark = tagging.marks[r.id];
    const tabStop = r.id === sel.focus || (sel.focus === null && v.index === 0);
    return (
      <div
        key={v.key}
        ref={(el) => {
          if (el) {
            rowEls.current.set(r.id, el);
            virtualizer.measureElement(el);
          } else rowEls.current.delete(r.id);
        }}
        data-index={v.index}
        role="row"
        aria-rowindex={v.index + 2}
        aria-selected={selected}
        aria-current={r.id === openId ? "true" : undefined}
        tabIndex={tabStop ? 0 : -1}
        data-testid="recording-row"
        data-id={r.id}
        className={cn("row", selected && "sel", r.id === openId && "on", mark?.done && "done", mark?.failed && "failed")}
        style={{ transform: `translateY(${v.start}px)` }}
        onClick={(e) => onRowClick(e, r.id)}
        onFocus={(e) => { if (e.currentTarget.contains(e.target as Node) && selRef.current.focus !== r.id) act({ type: "focus", id: r.id }); }}
      >
        <div role="gridcell" className="row-cell" data-controls="">
          <span className="check" aria-hidden="true" data-control="checkbox">{selected ? <Check /> : null}</span>
          <div className="row-body">
            <div className="t"><span className="row-title">{r.title}</span>{r.private && <Lock size={13} className="lock" aria-label="Private" />}</div>
            <div className="m">{[r.when, r.duration, r.sources.join(", "), r.kind === "video" ? "video" : null].filter(Boolean).join(" · ")}</div>
            <TagChips
              tags={shownTags(r, tagging.pending)}
              title={r.title}
              canTag={tagging.canTag}
              place="row"
              onRemove={(tag, next) => {
                (next ?? rowEls.current.get(r.id))?.focus();
                tagging.tag("remove", [r.id], [tag], { kind: "chip" });
              }}
            >
              {tagging.canTag && <AddTagsButton id={r.id} title={r.title} place="row" />}
            </TagChips>
            {mark?.done && <span className="done-label">{"✓"} Tagged</span>}
            {mark?.failed && <span className="failed-label"><TriangleAlert aria-hidden="true" size={13} /> {mark.failed}</span>}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  };

  return (
    <section className="list" aria-label="Recordings">
      <div className="list-head">
        <span>{rows.length.toLocaleString("en")} recording{rows.length === 1 ? "" : "s"} {"·"} newest first</span>
        <button type="button" className="refresh" data-testid="refresh" onClick={tagging.refresh}>
          {tagging.touched > 0 ? `${tagging.touched} tagged · Refresh` : "Refresh"}
        </button>
        {!tagging.saves && <span className="demo-note">Demo: changes aren't saved</span>}
        {!tagging.canTag && tagging.reason && <span className="cannot-tag" data-testid="cannot-tag">Tagging is off: {tagging.reason}</span>}
        <span className="key-hint" data-testid="key-hint">{KEY_HINT}</span>
        <span className="sr-only" role="status">{announce}</span>
      </div>
      <div
        ref={scrollRef}
        className={cn("vlist", sel.selected.size > 0 && "has-selection")}
        role="grid"
        aria-multiselectable="true"
        aria-rowcount={rows.length + 1}
        aria-label="Recordings"
        data-testid="recording-list"
        onKeyDown={onKeyDown}
      >
        <div role="rowgroup" className="sr-only">
          <div role="row" aria-rowindex={1}><div role="columnheader">Recording</div></div>
        </div>
        <div role="rowgroup" className="vrows" style={{ height: virtualizer.getTotalSize() }}>
          {virtualizer.getVirtualItems().map(drawRow)}
        </div>
      </div>
      <SelectionBar count={sel.selected.size} lastTagged={tagging.lastTagged} onTag={openPicker} onClear={() => act({ type: "clear" })} />
    </section>
  );
}
```

In `packages/ui/frontend/src/App.tsx`:
- Add imports: `useCallback`, `useRef` from `react`; `NO_SEL, prune, reduceSel, type Sel, type SelAction` from `./lib/selection`; `useRowOrder` from `./lib/useRowOrder`; `type LibraryRow` from `./types`. Remove the `filterRows` import.
- Replace the `rows` line with:
```tsx
  const order = useRowOrder(view, filter, tagging.loadKey, tagging.revealed);
  const orderIds = useRef<string[]>([]);
  orderIds.current = order.ids;
  const [sel, setSel] = useState<Sel>(NO_SEL);
  const act = useCallback((a: SelAction) => setSel((s) => reduceSel(s, a, orderIds.current)), []);
  const [announce, setAnnounce] = useState("");
  const byId = useMemo(() => new Map((view?.recordings ?? []).map((r) => [r.id, r])), [view]);
  const rows = useMemo(() => order.ids.map((id) => byId.get(id)).filter((r): r is LibraryRow => r !== undefined), [order.ids, byId]);

  const selNow = useRef(sel);
  selNow.current = sel;

  // A filter change drops hidden rows from the selection, says so, and focuses the first row; a
  // Refresh drops vanished rows quietly; an Undo's rows coming back change nothing (spec §6).
  useEffect(() => {
    if (order.reason === "reveal") return;
    const before = selNow.current;
    const { sel: next, hidden } = prune(before, order.ids);
    if (order.reason === "filter") {
      setSel(next);
      setAnnounce(hidden > 0 ? `${hidden} hidden row${hidden === 1 ? "" : "s"} deselected` : "");
    } else {
      const kept = before.focus !== null && order.ids.includes(before.focus);
      setSel({ ...next, focus: kept ? before.focus : next.focus });
    }
  }, [order.key]); // order.ids and order.reason change with order.key
```

In `packages/ui/frontend/src/app.css`, replace the line `.row.on { background: var(--sel); box-shadow: inset 3px 0 0 var(--primary); }` with `.row.on { box-shadow: inset 3px 0 0 var(--primary); }`, replace `.list { border-right: 1px solid var(--border); overflow: auto; background: var(--card); }` with `.list { border-right: 1px solid var(--border); background: var(--card); display: flex; flex-direction: column; min-height: 0; overflow: hidden; }`, and append:
```css
/* The Library list as an ARIA grid, drawn virtually (tagging spec §6). Each state is distinct
   without colour: focused has the ring, selected the tint and a visible check, open the 3px bar,
   done the muted background and "✓ Tagged" at full-strength text. Never opacity. */
.list-head { display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; align-items: center; gap: 4px 10px; }
.refresh { margin-left: auto; min-height: 24px; padding: 2px 8px; border: 1px solid var(--border); border-radius: 6px; background: var(--card); color: var(--foreground); font: inherit; cursor: pointer; }
.key-hint, .demo-note { width: 100%; font-size: 11.5px; color: var(--muted-foreground); }
.cannot-tag { width: 100%; color: var(--destructive); font-size: 12.5px; }
.vlist { flex: 1; min-height: 0; overflow: auto; }
.vrows { position: relative; width: 100%; }
.vrows > .row { position: absolute; top: 0; left: 0; }
.row { user-select: none; -webkit-user-select: none; }
.row:focus-visible { outline: 2px solid var(--ring); outline-offset: -2px; }
.row.sel { background: var(--sel); }
.row.done { background: var(--muted); }
.row.sel.done { background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--primary) 12%, var(--muted)); }
.row-cell { display: grid; grid-template-columns: 18px minmax(0, 1fr); gap: 6px; }
.row-body { min-width: 0; }
.row-title { min-width: 0; overflow: hidden; text-overflow: ellipsis; }
.check { display: inline-grid; place-items: center; width: 16px; height: 16px; margin-top: 2px; border: 1px solid var(--muted-foreground); border-radius: 4px; cursor: pointer; visibility: hidden; }
.check svg { width: 12px; height: 12px; }
.row:hover .check, .vlist.has-selection .check { visibility: visible; }
.done-label { display: inline-block; margin-top: 2px; font-size: 12px; color: var(--muted-foreground); }
.failed-label { display: inline-flex; align-items: center; gap: 4px; margin-top: 2px; font-size: 12px; color: var(--destructive); }
.selection-bar { display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; align-items: center; gap: 6px 12px; padding: 8px 12px; border-top: 1px solid var(--border); background: var(--card); }
```

```python
import io
```

```python
def _wav(seed: int) -> bytes:
    """A tenth of a second of quiet, different for each seed: real audio, so a row that opens
    plays without a decode error."""
    buffer = io.BytesIO()
    with wave.open(buffer, "wb") as out:
        out.setnchannels(1)
        out.setsampwidth(2)
        out.setframerate(8000)
        out.writeframes(seed.to_bytes(2, "little") * 800)
    return buffer.getvalue()


@pytest.fixture(scope="module")
def big_archive(tmp_path_factory) -> Path:
    """300 synthetic recordings with the demo's UUID, so demo mode serves them through
    RECORDINGS_DEMO_ARCHIVE (spec §9). 200 untagged; 100 tagged from 20 topics, every tenth of
    those private too. Built through init_archive and Writer.add; invented titles only."""
    base = tmp_path_factory.mktemp("big")
    root = base / "archive"
    init_archive(root, base / "state", writer_id="e2e", archive_uuid=DEMO_UUID)
    writer = Writer(root, State.open(base / "state"), Index(base / "index.db"), writer_id="e2e")
    media = base / "media"
    media.mkdir()
    t0 = datetime.fromisoformat("2026-01-05T09:00:00-08:00")
    for i in range(300):
        path = media / f"{i:03d}.wav"
        path.write_bytes(_wav(i))
        tags: tuple[TagRef, ...] = ()
        if i >= 200:
            tags = (TagRef(tag=f"topic/t{i % 20:02d}", by="you"),)
            if i % 10 == 0:
                tags += (TagRef(tag="private", by="you"),)
        writer.add(
            Incoming(
                media=path, recorded_at=t0 + timedelta(hours=i), timezone_name="America/Vancouver",
                time_source="metadata", title=f"Synthetic {i:03d}", kind="audio", sources=(),
                tags=tags, duration_ms=60_000 + i * 1000,
            )
        )
    return root


@pytest.fixture
def big_server(tmp_path, big_archive):
    started = start_server(tmp_path / "server", archive=big_archive)
    yield started
    started.stop()
```

---
### Task 13: Drag and drop onto the sidebar, and + New tag…

**Checkpoint lens:** security and privacy (a drop acts only on a drag that started in this page).

Drag a row, or the selection when the row is in it, onto a tag or note type in the sidebar to add it; drop onto **+ New tag…** to open the picker for those recordings (spec §6). The dragged IDs come from in-page state set at `dragstart`, never from `dataTransfer`, so a foreign page's drag, a file or text can never tag anything. The sidebar also keeps the active tag filter's item when its tag has lost its last recording (Review Focus 4).

Checked before writing (CLAUDE.md): MDN's HTML Drag and Drop API (`dragstart`'s target is the draggable element, so where the drag began comes from `pointerdown`; `dropEffect`, `setDragImage` needs an element in the document; a drop target must cancel `dragover`); Playwright's `locator.drag_to` (drives native drag and drop in Chromium; spec §11 asks for this to be checked).

**Files:**
- Modify: `packages/ui/frontend/src/components/RecordingList.tsx` (draggable rows; the dragged row stays rendered)
- Modify: `packages/ui/frontend/src/components/Sidebar.tsx`
- Modify: `packages/ui/frontend/src/App.tsx` (`onNewTag`), `src/app.css`
- Test: `packages/ui/frontend/src/components/Sidebar.test.ts` (new), `packages/ui/tests/e2e/test_tagging.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `Tagging.drag`, `Tagging.tag`, `Tagging.canTag` (Task 10); `openPickerFor` (Task 12); `targetsOf` (Task 9); `filterIsShown` (Task 9).
- Produces:
  - `Sidebar({ library, filter, onFilter, onNewTag })` where `onNewTag(ids: string[] | null)`: null means "the selection"; tag items stay `data-testid="filter-tag"`; `data-testid="new-tag"`; a drop target being hovered has the class `drop`
  - `dragImage(text): HTMLElement` in `RecordingList.tsx`

- [ ] **Step 3: Make the sidebar a drop target**

Replace `packages/ui/frontend/src/components/Sidebar.tsx` with:
```tsx
import { Lock } from "lucide-react";
import { useState, type DragEvent, type ReactNode } from "react";

import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

import { filterIsShown } from "../lib/library";
import { useTaggingContext } from "../tagging/useTagging";
import type { Filter, LibraryView } from "../types";

type Props = {
  library: LibraryView; filter: Filter; onFilter: (f: Filter) => void;
  onNewTag: (ids: string[] | null) => void; // null: the selection
};

const keyOf = (f: Filter) => (f.kind === "tag" ? `tag:${f.tag}` : f.kind);

/** The filters, and the drop targets for tagging by drag and drop (spec §6). A drop acts only on
 * a drag that started in this page: the IDs come from in-page state set at dragstart, never from
 * dataTransfer, so files, text and other windows' drags are ignored. */
export function Sidebar({ library, filter, onFilter, onNewTag }: Props) {
  const tagging = useTaggingContext();
  const [over, setOver] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const drop = (target: string, tag: string | null) => {
    if (!tagging.canTag) return {};
    return {
      onDragOver: (e: DragEvent<HTMLElement>) => {
        if (!tagging.drag.current) return; // not ours: no drop allowed here
        e.preventDefault();
        e.dataTransfer.dropEffect = "copy";
        setOver(target);
      },
      onDragLeave: () => setOver((o) => (o === target ? null : o)),
      onDrop: (e: DragEvent<HTMLElement>) => {
        setOver(null);
        const ids = tagging.drag.current;
        if (!ids) return; // a foreign drop: ignored
        e.preventDefault();
        tagging.drag.current = null;
        if (tag === null) onNewTag(ids);
        else tagging.tag("add", ids, [tag], { kind: "drop" });
      },
    };
  };

  const item = (f: Filter, label: ReactNode, n: number, testid: string) => {
    const key = keyOf(f);
    return (
      <button key={key} type="button" className={cn("si", key === keyOf(filter) && "on", over === key && "drop")}
        data-testid={testid} onClick={() => onFilter(f)} {...(f.kind === "tag" ? drop(key, f.tag) : {})}>
        <span>{label}</span><span className="n">{n}</span>
      </button>
    );
  };
  const open = library.tags.filter((t) => !t.private);
  const hidden = library.tags.filter((t) => t.private);
  // The active filter's tag lost its last recording: keep its item, at 0, while it applies.
  const gone = filter.kind === "tag" && !filterIsShown(filter, library) ? filter.tag : null;
  return (
    <nav className="sidebar" aria-label="Filters">
      {item({ kind: "all" }, "All recordings", library.counts.all, "filter-all")}
      {item({ kind: "untagged" }, "Untagged", library.counts.untagged, "filter-untagged")}
      {library.problems.length > 0 && (
        <div className="problems" role="status">
          {library.problems.length} recording file{library.problems.length > 1 ? "s" : ""} couldn't be read. Run <code>recordings validate</code>.
        </div>
      )}
      <div className="grp">Tags</div>
      {open.map((t) => item({ kind: "tag", tag: t.tag }, t.tag, t.count, "filter-tag"))}
      {gone !== null && item({ kind: "tag", tag: gone }, gone, 0, "filter-tag")}
      {tagging.canTag && (
        <button type="button" className={cn("si", "new-tag", over === "new" && "drop")} data-testid="new-tag"
          onClick={() => onNewTag(null)} {...drop("new", null)}>
          <span>+ New tag{"…"}</span>
        </button>
      )}
      {hidden.length > 0 && <div className="grp">Private</div>}
      {hidden.map((t) => item({ kind: "tag", tag: t.tag }, <><Lock size={12} className="lock" aria-hidden /> {t.tag}</>, t.count, "filter-tag"))}
      {library.note_types.length > 0 && <div className="grp">Note types</div>}
      {library.note_types.map((n) => item({ kind: "tag", tag: `notes/${n.note_type}` }, n.note_type, n.count, "filter-tag"))}
    </nav>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Make rows draggable**

In `packages/ui/frontend/src/components/RecordingList.tsx`:
- Add `useState` and `type DragEvent` to the `react` import.
- Add, above `RecordingList`:
```tsx
/** The drag image: "12 recordings" (spec §6). setDragImage needs an element in the document. */
export function dragImage(text: string): HTMLElement {
  let el = document.getElementById("drag-image");
  if (!el) {
    el = document.createElement("div");
    el.id = "drag-image";
    el.className = "drag-image";
    el.setAttribute("aria-hidden", "true");
    document.body.appendChild(el);
  }
  el.textContent = text;
  return el;
}
```
- Inside `RecordingList`, after the `anchorIdx` line, add:
```tsx
  const [dragIdx, setDragIdx] = useState(-1);
  const pressedOn = useRef<Element | null>(null);
```
- In `rangeExtractor`, keep the dragged row too: `const keep = [focusIdx, anchorIdx, dragIdx].filter(…)` and add `dragIdx` to its dependency list.
- Add these handlers before `drawRow`:
```tsx
  // A drag starts from in-page state: the selection when the row is in it, else that row alone,
  // leaving the selection as it is. A drag that starts on the checkbox, × or + is cancelled.
  const onDragStart = (e: DragEvent<HTMLDivElement>, id: string, at: number) => {
    if (pressedOn.current?.closest("[data-control]")) {
      e.preventDefault();
      return;
    }
    const ids = sel.selected.has(id) ? targetsOf(sel, order) : [id];
    tagging.drag.current = ids;
    setDragIdx(at);
    const label = `${ids.length.toLocaleString("en")} recording${ids.length === 1 ? "" : "s"}`;
    e.dataTransfer.effectAllowed = "copy";
    e.dataTransfer.setData("text/plain", label); // a drag needs data; it is never read back
    e.dataTransfer.setDragImage(dragImage(label), 12, 12);
  };
  const onDragEnd = () => {
    tagging.drag.current = null;
    setDragIdx(-1);
  };
```

```tsx
        onPointerDown={(e) => { pressedOn.current = e.target as Element; }}
        onDragStart={(e) => onDragStart(e, r.id, v.index)}
        onDragEnd={onDragEnd}
```

```python
FOREIGN_DROP = """([id]) => {
    // A drop that didn't start in this page: text naming a recording, dropped on `private`.
    const target = [...document.querySelectorAll('[data-testid=filter-tag]')]
        .find((el) => el.textContent.includes('private'));
    const data = new DataTransfer();
    data.setData('text/plain', id);
    for (const type of ['dragenter', 'dragover', 'drop']) {
        const init = { bubbles: true, cancelable: true, dataTransfer: data };
        target.dispatchEvent(new DragEvent(type, init));
    }
}"""


def test_dragging_a_selection_onto_a_sidebar_tag_and_a_foreign_drop(page: Page, server, console, demo_ids):
    apollo13, jfk = demo_ids["apollo13-problem"], demo_ids["jfk-rice"]
    open_library(page, server.url)

    rows.filter(has_text="Apollo 13").click()
    rows.filter(has_text="JFK").click(modifiers=["ControlOrMeta"])
    target = page.get_by_test_id("filter-tag").filter(has_text="space/apollo")
    rows.filter(has_text="JFK").drag_to(target)
    expect(page.get_by_test_id("action-toast")).to_contain_text("Added space/apollo to 2 recordings")
    wait_until(lambda: ("space/apollo", "you") in server.tags(apollo13) and ("space/apollo", "you") in server.tags(jfk))
    rows.filter(has_text="JFK").drag_to(target)  # nothing changes, and it says so
    expect(page.get_by_test_id("action-toast").filter(has_text="Already on all 2")).to_be_visible()
    page.evaluate(FOREIGN_DROP, [jfk])
    page.wait_for_timeout(500)
    assert ("private", "you") not in server.tags(jfk)
    # outside the selection
    rows.filter(has_text="Apollo 11").drag_to(page.get_by_test_id("new-tag"))
    expect(page.get_by_role("dialog", name=re.compile(r"^Tag Apollo 11"))).to_be_visible()
    page.keyboard.press("Escape")
    expect(page.get_by_test_id("selection-bar")).to_contain_text("2 selected")  # left alone
```

```bash
git add packages/ui/frontend/src packages/ui/tests/e2e/test_tagging.py
git commit -m "feat(ui): drag recordings onto sidebar tags, note types and + New tag…

The dragged IDs come from in-page state set at dragstart, never from dataTransfer, so files,
text and other windows' drags are ignored. Dragging a selected row drags the selection;
dragging another row drags it alone. Drop targets show a 2px orange outline. The sidebar keeps
the active filter's tag, at 0, after its last removal. Checked: MDN HTML Drag and Drop
(dragstart target, setDragImage, dragover), Playwright locator.drag_to.

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>"
```

---
### Task 14: The Tags page grid (`#/tags`)

**Checkpoint lens:** UX and accessibility (triage speed by keyboard and mouse; grid semantics; columns that don't move).

**Tags** joins the top bar between Library and Status, at `#/tags` (spec §7). The grid reads the merged `library` that `App` holds. A pinned first column holds the selection check, the title, the date, **Open** and, with Untagged only, a snippet; the pinned `private` column follows; every other tag gets a checkbox column, private ones grouped at the right under a lock. Untagged only is on by default, with a live "N left". Columns are fixed per load; digits toggle the first ten.

Checked before writing (CLAUDE.md): the WAI-ARIA grid pattern (`aria-colcount`, `aria-colindex`, `rowheader`, `columnheader` with `aria-sort`, a `gridcell` holding one `role="checkbox"` with `aria-checked`); TanStack Virtual (as Task 12); shadcn's Popover (the column menu and the column picker).

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/ui/frontend/src/components/TagsPage.tsx`
- Modify: `packages/ui/frontend/src/components/TopBar.tsx`, `src/App.tsx`, `src/app.css`
- Test: `packages/ui/frontend/src/components/TopBar.test.ts` (new), `packages/ui/tests/e2e/test_tagging.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `lib/grid.ts`, `lib/selection.ts`, `lib/keys.ts`, `lib/library.ts`, `lib/nav.ts` (`openHref`), `lib/theme.ts` (`browserStorage`) (Task 9); `Tagging` (Task 10: `view`, `loadKey`, `revealed`, `sessionTags`, `pending`, `marks`, `isPrivate`, `tag`, `undoLast`, `refresh`, `touched`, `snippet`, `wantSnippets`, `canTag`, `reason`, `saves`); `openPickerFor` (Task 12); `useMediaQuery` (Task 12).
- Produces:
  - `TagsPage()`: `data-testid="tags-page"`; the grid `data-testid="tags-grid"` (`role="grid"`); rows `data-testid="grid-row"` with `data-id`; tag cells' checkboxes `data-testid="grid-cell"` with `data-tag`; headers `data-testid="grid-header"` with `data-tag`; `data-testid="untagged-only"`, `"left-count"`, `"title-filter"`, `"grid-refresh"`, `"columns"`
  - `TopBar` lists Library, Tags, Status
