- [ ] **Step 4: Write the grid**

`packages/ui/frontend/src/components/TagsPage.tsx`:
```tsx
import { defaultRangeExtractor, useVirtualizer, type Range } from "@tanstack/react-virtual";
import { Check, Columns3, Lock, MoreHorizontal } from "lucide-react";
import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState, type FocusEvent, type KeyboardEvent, type MouseEvent } from "react";

import { Popover, PopoverContent, PopoverTrigger } from "@/components/ui/popover";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

import {
  columnOrder, defaultColumns, digitColumn, digitFor, foldersOf, gridRows, layoutColumns, loadChoices,
  saveChoices, sortRows, type RowSort,
} from "../lib/grid";
import { commandShortcut, isTextEntry, plainShortcut } from "../lib/keys";
import { shownTags, withIds } from "../lib/library";
import { openHref } from "../lib/nav";
import { NO_SEL, prune, reduceSel, targetsOf, type Move, type Sel, type SelAction } from "../lib/selection";
import { browserStorage } from "../lib/theme";
import { useTaggingContext } from "../tagging/useTagging";
import type { LibraryRow, LibraryView } from "../types";
import { KEY_HINT, openPickerFor } from "./RecordingList";

const ROW_HEIGHT = 64;
const PIN = "300px"; // fixed, so the pinned `private` column can sit right beside it (app.css)
const COL = "84px";

function allTags(view: LibraryView): string[] {
  return [...view.tags.map((t) => t.tag), ...view.note_types.map((n) => `notes/${n.note_type}`)];
}

function noTranscript(r: LibraryRow): string {
  return ["No transcript yet", r.duration ?? "length unknown", r.sources.join(", ") || "no source"].join(" · ");
}

const cellKey = (id: string, col: number) => `${id}|${col}`;

/** The Tags page grid (spec §7). */
export function TagsPage() {
  const tagging = useTaggingContext();
  const view = tagging.view as LibraryView; // App draws this page only once the library is in
  const scrollRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const wantFocus = useRef(false);
  const [untaggedOnly, setUntaggedOnly] = useState(true);
  const [title, setTitle] = useState("");
  const [sort, setSort] = useState<RowSort>({ by: "newest" });
  const [choices, setChoices] = useState(() => loadChoices(browserStorage()));
  const [hiddenPrivate, setHiddenPrivate] = useState<ReadonlySet<string>>(new Set());
  const [sel, setSel] = useState<Sel>(NO_SEL);
  const [col, setCol] = useState(0); // 0: the pinned cell; 1…: the tag columns
  const [hint, setHint] = useState("");
  const [base, setBase] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [order, setOrder] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const selNow = useRef(sel);
  selNow.current = sel;
  const sortKey = JSON.stringify(sort);

  // The default columns are worked out on load and on Refresh, never live (spec §7).
  useEffect(() => setBase(defaultColumns(view)), [tagging.loadKey]); // on load and Refresh only
  // Rows stay put until Refresh or a filter change (spec §6, §7).
  useEffect(() => {
    const ids = sortRows(gridRows(view.recordings, { untaggedOnly, title }), sort).map((r) => r.id);
    setOrder(ids);
    const { sel: next, hidden } = prune(selNow.current, ids);
    setSel(next);
    setHint(hidden > 0 ? `${hidden} hidden row${hidden === 1 ? "" : "s"} deselected` : "");
  }, [tagging.loadKey, untaggedOnly, title, sortKey]); // never on a tag change
  // An Undo puts the rows it touched back.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (tagging.revealed.ids.length === 0) return;
    setOrder((o) => withIds(o, tagging.revealed.ids, sortRows(view.recordings, sort)));
  }, [tagging.revealed]);

  const columns = useMemo(() => layoutColumns({
    base, session: tagging.sessionTags, all: allTags(view), isPrivate: tagging.isPrivate, choices, hidden: hiddenPrivate,
  }), [base, tagging.sessionTags, view, tagging.isPrivate, choices, hiddenPrivate]);
  const cols = columnOrder(columns);
  const byId = useMemo(() => new Map(view.recordings.map((r) => [r.id, r])), [view]);
  const rows = useMemo(() => order.map((id) => byId.get(id)).filter((r): r is LibraryRow => r !== undefined), [order, byId]);
  const index = useMemo(() => new Map(order.map((id, i) => [id, i])), [order]);
  const focusIdx = sel.focus === null ? -1 : (index.get(sel.focus) ?? -1);
  const anchorIdx = sel.anchor === null ? -1 : (index.get(sel.anchor) ?? -1);
  const act = useCallback((a: SelAction) => setSel((s) => reduceSel(s, a, order)), [order]);

  const rangeExtractor = useCallback((range: Range) => {
    const keep = [focusIdx, anchorIdx].filter((i) => i >= 0 && i < range.count);
    return [...new Set([...defaultRangeExtractor(range), ...keep])].sort((a, b) => a - b);
  }, [focusIdx, anchorIdx]);
  const virtualizer = useVirtualizer({
    count: rows.length, getScrollElement: () => scrollRef.current, estimateSize: () => ROW_HEIGHT,
    overscan: 6, getItemKey: (i) => order[i], rangeExtractor, useFlushSync: false,
    scrollPaddingStart: ROW_HEIGHT, // the pinned header row
  });

  // Snippets for the rows on screen, with Untagged only (spec §7).
  const onScreen = virtualizer.getVirtualItems().map((v) => order[v.index]).join(",");
  useEffect(() => {
    if (untaggedOnly && onScreen) tagging.wantSnippets(onScreen.split(","));
  }, [untaggedOnly, onScreen]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!wantFocus.current || sel.focus === null) return;
    wantFocus.current = false;
    if (focusIdx >= 0) virtualizer.scrollToIndex(focusIdx, { align: "auto" });
    const key = cellKey(sel.focus, col);
    requestAnimationFrame(() => {
      scrollRef.current?.querySelector<HTMLElement>(`[data-cell="${CSS.escape(key)}"]`)?.focus({ preventScroll: false });
    });
  }, [sel.focus, col, focusIdx, virtualizer]);

  const toggle = (r: LibraryRow, tag: string) => {
    const shown = shownTags(r, tagging.pending).find((t) => t.tag === tag);
    if (shown?.pending) return; // a change is already on its way
    tagging.tag(shown ? "remove" : "add", [r.id], [tag], { kind: "cell" });
  };
  const addToSelection = (tag: string, kind: "column" | "digit") => {
    const ids = sel.selected.size > 0 ? targetsOf(sel, order) : [];
    if (ids.length === 0) {
      setHint("Select rows first: click a title, or Shift-click for a range.");
      return;
    }
    tagging.tag("add", ids, [tag], { kind });
  };
  const openInNewTab = (id: string) => {
    window.open(new URL(openHref(id), window.location.href).href, "_blank", "noopener");
  };
  const focusCell = (c: number) => {
    wantFocus.current = true;
    setCol(Math.max(0, Math.min(cols.length, c)));
  };

  const page = () => Math.max(1, Math.floor((scrollRef.current?.clientHeight ?? ROW_HEIGHT * 10) / ROW_HEIGHT) - 2);
  const move = (e: KeyboardEvent, to: Move, meta = e.metaKey || e.ctrlKey) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    wantFocus.current = true;
    act({ type: "move", to, shift: e.shiftKey, meta, page: page() });
  };

  const onKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {
    if (!e.currentTarget.contains(e.target as Node) || isTextEntry(e.target) || e.nativeEvent.isComposing) return;
    const row = sel.focus ? byId.get(sel.focus) : undefined;
    switch (e.key) {
      case "ArrowDown": return move(e, "next");
      case "ArrowUp": return move(e, "prev");
      case "PageDown": return move(e, "pageDown");
      case "PageUp": return move(e, "pageUp");
      case "Home":
        if (e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) return move(e, "first", false);
        e.preventDefault();
        return focusCell(0); // Home and End stay in the row
      case "End":
        if (e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) return move(e, "last", false);
        e.preventDefault();
        return focusCell(cols.length);
      case "ArrowRight":
        e.preventDefault();
        return focusCell(col + 1);
      case "ArrowLeft":
        e.preventDefault();
        return focusCell(col - 1);
      case " ":
        e.preventDefault();
        if (commandShortcut(e)) act({ type: "toggleFocused" });
        else if (row && col > 0) toggle(row, cols[col - 1]); // the grid has no player (spec §7)
        return;
      case "Enter":
        e.preventDefault();
        if (row && col === 0) openInNewTab(row.id);
        else if (row) toggle(row, cols[col - 1]);
        return;
      case "Escape":
        if (!e.defaultPrevented) act({ type: "clear" });
        return;
    }
    const key = e.key.toLowerCase();
    if (commandShortcut(e) && key === "a") {
      e.preventDefault();
      act({ type: "all" });
    } else if (commandShortcut(e) && key === "z" && !e.shiftKey) {
      e.preventDefault();
      tagging.undoLast();
    } else if (plainShortcut(e) && key === "t") {
      e.preventDefault();
      openPickerFor(targetsOf(sel, order), rows, tagging);
    } else if (plainShortcut(e) && key === "r") {
      e.preventDefault();
      tagging.refresh();
    } else if (plainShortcut(e) && digitColumn(e.key, cols)) {
      e.preventDefault();
      const tag = digitColumn(e.key, cols) as string;
      if (sel.selected.size >= 2) addToSelection(tag, "digit");
      else if (row) toggle(row, tag);
    }
  };

  // Focus moving into a cell (by click or Tab) makes it the focused cell.
  const onFocus = (e: FocusEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {
    const key = (e.target as HTMLElement).dataset?.cell;
    if (!key) return;
    const [id, c] = key.split("|");
    if (id !== selNow.current.focus) act({ type: "focus", id });
    setCol(Number(c));
  };

  const onPinClick = (e: MouseEvent<HTMLDivElement>, id: string) => {
    const target = e.target as Element;
    if (target.closest("[data-control=open]")) return;
    if (target.closest("[data-control=checkbox]")) return act({ type: "checkbox", id, shift: e.shiftKey });
    act({ type: "click", id, meta: e.metaKey || e.ctrlKey, shift: e.shiftKey }); // opens nothing
  };

  const template = `${PIN} repeat(${cols.length}, ${COL})`;
  const left = view.counts.untagged;
  const allChoices = foldersOf(allTags(view).filter((t) => t !== "private").sort((a, b) => a.localeCompare(b)));
  const visible = new Set(cols);
  const toggleColumn = (tag: string) => {
    if (tagging.isPrivate(tag)) {
      // Private columns: for this session only, so no private tag name is stored (spec §7).
      setHiddenPrivate((h) => { const next = new Set(h); if (next.has(tag)) next.delete(tag); else next.add(tag); return next; });
      return;
    }
    const next = { ...choices, [tag]: !visible.has(tag) };
    setChoices(next);
    saveChoices(browserStorage(), next, tagging.isPrivate);
  };

  return (
    <main className="tags-page" data-testid="tags-page">
      <div className="grid-head">
        <h1>Tags</h1>
        <label className="grid-opt">
          <input type="checkbox" checked={untaggedOnly} onChange={(e) => setUntaggedOnly(e.target.checked)} data-testid="untagged-only" />
          Untagged only <span className="muted" data-testid="left-count">{left.toLocaleString("en")} left</span>
        </label>
        <input type="search" className="title-filter" placeholder="Filter by title" aria-label="Filter by title"
          value={title} onChange={(e) => setTitle(e.target.value)} data-testid="title-filter" />
        <Popover>
          <PopoverTrigger asChild>
            <button type="button" className="refresh" data-testid="columns"><Columns3 aria-hidden="true" size={14} /> Columns</button>
          </PopoverTrigger>
          <PopoverContent align="start" className="columns-picker" aria-label="Columns">
            {[...allChoices].map(([folder, tags]) => (
              <fieldset key={folder || "-"}>
                <legend>{folder || "No folder"}</legend>
                {tags.map((tag) => (
                  <label key={tag} className="column-choice">
                    <input type="checkbox" checked={visible.has(tag)} onChange={() => toggleColumn(tag)} />
                    {tagging.isPrivate(tag) && <Lock aria-hidden="true" size={12} />} {tag}
                  </label>
                ))}
              </fieldset>
            ))}
          </PopoverContent>
        </Popover>
        <button type="button" className="refresh" data-testid="grid-refresh" onClick={tagging.refresh}>
          {tagging.touched > 0 ? `${tagging.touched} tagged · Refresh` : "Refresh"}
        </button>
        {!tagging.saves && <span className="demo-note">Demo: changes aren't saved</span>}
        {!tagging.canTag && tagging.reason && <span className="cannot-tag" data-testid="cannot-tag">Tagging is off: {tagging.reason}</span>}
        <span className="key-hint">{KEY_HINT} {"·"} 1{"–"}9, 0 columns</span>
        <span className="sr-only" role="status">{hint}</span>
        {hint && <span className="grid-hint" aria-hidden="true">{hint}</span>}
      </div>
      <div ref={scrollRef} className={cn("tgrid", sel.selected.size > 0 && "has-selection")} role="grid" aria-multiselectable="true" aria-label="Tags"
        aria-rowcount={rows.length + 1} aria-colcount={cols.length + 1} data-testid="tags-grid"
        onKeyDown={onKeyDown} onFocus={onFocus}>
        <div role="rowgroup" className="tg-headgroup">
          <div role="row" aria-rowindex={1} className="tg-row tg-head" style={{ gridTemplateColumns: template }}>
            <div role="columnheader" aria-colindex={1} className="tg-pin"
              aria-sort={sort.by === "has" ? undefined : sort.by === "oldest" || sort.by === "title" ? "ascending" : "descending"}>
              <span>Recording</span>
              <select aria-label="Sort rows" value={sort.by === "has" ? "has" : sort.by}
                onChange={(e) => setSort({ by: e.target.value as "newest" | "oldest" | "title" | "duration" })}>
                <option value="newest">Newest</option>
                <option value="oldest">Oldest</option>
                <option value="title">Title</option>
                <option value="duration">Duration</option>
                {sort.by === "has" && <option value="has" disabled>Has {sort.tag} first</option>}
              </select>
            </div>
            {cols.map((tag, j) => {
              const digit = digitFor(j);
              const priv = tagging.isPrivate(tag) || tag === "private";
              return (
                <div key={tag} role="columnheader" aria-colindex={j + 2} aria-label={tag}
                  aria-sort={sort.by === "has" && sort.tag === tag ? "descending" : undefined}
                  className={cn("tg-h", priv && "priv", tag === "private" && "pinned")} data-testid="grid-header" data-tag={tag}>
                  <button type="button" className="tg-add"
                    title={sel.selected.size > 0 ? `Add to ${sel.selected.size.toLocaleString("en")} selected` : "Select rows first"}
                    aria-label={`Add ${tag} to the selected rows`} onClick={() => addToSelection(tag, "column")}>
                    {digit && <kbd aria-hidden="true">{digit}</kbd>}
                    {priv && <Lock aria-hidden="true" size={11} />}
                    <span className="tg-hname">{tag}</span>
                  </button>
                  <Popover>
                    <PopoverTrigger asChild>
                      <button type="button" className="tg-menu" aria-label={`Sort by ${tag}`}><MoreHorizontal aria-hidden="true" size={14} /></button>
                    </PopoverTrigger>
                    <PopoverContent align="end" className="tg-menu-list" aria-label={`Sort by ${tag}`}>
                      <button type="button" onClick={() => setSort({ by: "has", tag })}>Has this tag first</button>
                    </PopoverContent>
                  </Popover>
                </div>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        </div>
        <div role="rowgroup" className="vrows tg-body" style={{ height: virtualizer.getTotalSize() }}>
          {virtualizer.getVirtualItems().map((v) => {
            const r = rows[v.index];
            const selected = sel.selected.has(r.id);
            const shown = shownTags(r, tagging.pending);
            const mark = tagging.marks[r.id];
            const snippet = tagging.snippet(r.id);
            const isFocus = (c: number) => r.id === sel.focus && col === c;
            const tabStop = (c: number) => isFocus(c) || (sel.focus === null && v.index === 0 && c === 0);
            return (
              <div key={v.key} role="row" aria-rowindex={v.index + 2} aria-selected={selected}
                data-testid="grid-row" data-id={r.id}
                className={cn("tg-row", selected && "sel", mark?.done && "done")}
                style={{ gridTemplateColumns: template, transform: `translateY(${v.start}px)` }}>
                <div role="rowheader" aria-colindex={1} className="tg-pin" data-cell={cellKey(r.id, 0)}
                  tabIndex={tabStop(0) ? 0 : -1} onClick={(e) => onPinClick(e, r.id)}>
                  <span className="check" aria-hidden="true" data-control="checkbox">{selected ? <Check /> : null}</span>
                  <span className="tg-meta">
                    <span className="tg-title">{r.title}</span>
                    {r.private && <Lock aria-label="Private" size={12} className="lock" />}
                    <span className="tg-date">{r.when}{mark?.done ? " · ✓ Tagged" : ""}{mark?.failed ? ` · ⚠ ${mark.failed}` : ""}</span>
                    {untaggedOnly && <span className="tg-snippet">{snippet === undefined ? "…" : snippet ?? noTranscript(r)}</span>}
                  </span>
                  <button type="button" className="tg-open" data-control="open" tabIndex={-1}
                    aria-label={`Open ${r.title} in a new tab`} onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); openInNewTab(r.id); }}>
                    Open
                  </button>
                </div>
                {cols.map((tag, j) => {
                  const t = shown.find((s) => s.tag === tag);
                  const on = t !== undefined && t.pending !== "remove";
                  return (
                    <div key={tag} role="gridcell" aria-colindex={j + 2} className={cn("tg-cell", tag === "private" && "pinned")}
                      onClick={() => tagging.canTag && toggle(r, tag)}>
                      <span role="checkbox" aria-checked={on} aria-label={tag} aria-disabled={!tagging.canTag || undefined}
                        tabIndex={tabStop(j + 1) ? 0 : -1} data-cell={cellKey(r.id, j + 1)}
                        data-testid="grid-cell" data-tag={tag} className={cn("tg-box", t?.pending && "pending")}>
                        {on ? <Check aria-hidden="true" /> : null}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  );
                })}
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}
```

In `packages/ui/frontend/src/App.tsx`, import `TagsPage` from `./components/TagsPage`, and replace the page switch with:

```tsx
          <main className="panes">
```

Append to `packages/ui/frontend/src/app.css`:
```css
/* The Tags page grid (tagging spec §7). The header row and the first column are pinned. */
.tags-page { flex: 1; min-height: 0; display: flex; flex-direction: column; background: var(--card); }
.grid-head { display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; align-items: center; gap: 6px 12px; padding: 10px 16px; border-bottom: 1px solid var(--border); }
.grid-head h1 { margin: 0; font-size: 19px; font-weight: 700; }
.grid-opt { display: inline-flex; align-items: center; gap: 6px; }
.title-filter { min-width: 200px; padding: 2px 8px; border: 1px solid var(--input); border-radius: 6px; background: var(--card); color: var(--foreground); font: inherit; }
.grid-hint { width: 100%; font-size: 12px; color: var(--muted-foreground); }
.tgrid { flex: 1; min-height: 0; overflow: auto; position: relative; }
.tg-headgroup { position: sticky; top: 0; z-index: 2; width: max-content; min-width: 100%; }
.tg-body > .tg-row { position: absolute; top: 0; left: 0; }
.tg-row { display: grid; width: max-content; min-width: 100%; min-height: 64px; border-bottom: 1px solid var(--border); background: var(--card); }
.tg-row.sel { background: var(--sel); }
.tg-row.done { background: var(--muted); }
.tg-head { min-height: 40px; background: var(--card); font-size: 12px; }
.tg-pin { position: sticky; left: 0; z-index: 1; display: grid; grid-template-columns: 18px minmax(0, 1fr) auto; align-items: start; gap: 6px; padding: 6px 10px; background: inherit; border-right: 1px solid var(--border); user-select: none; }
.tg-head .tg-pin { grid-template-columns: 1fr auto; align-items: center; }
.tg-pin:focus-visible, .tg-box:focus-visible { outline: 2px solid var(--ring); outline-offset: -2px; }
.tgrid.has-selection .check, .tg-row:hover .check { visibility: visible; }
.tg-meta { display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: 0 6px; min-width: 0; }
.tg-title { font-weight: 700; min-width: 0; overflow: hidden; text-overflow: ellipsis; white-space: nowrap; max-width: 100%; }
.tg-date { color: var(--muted-foreground); font-size: 12px; }
.tg-snippet { width: 100%; color: var(--muted-foreground); font-size: 12px; display: -webkit-box; -webkit-line-clamp: 2; -webkit-box-orient: vertical; overflow: hidden; }
.tg-open { min-height: 24px; padding: 2px 8px; border: 1px solid var(--border); border-radius: 6px; background: var(--card); color: var(--foreground); font: inherit; font-size: 12px; cursor: pointer; }
.tg-h { display: flex; align-items: center; min-width: 0; border-right: 1px solid var(--border); }
/* The pinned `private` column sits right after the 300px first column. */
.tg-h.pinned, .tg-cell.pinned { position: sticky; left: 300px; z-index: 1; background: inherit; box-shadow: 1px 0 0 var(--border); }
.tg-h.pinned { background: var(--card); }
.tg-add { flex: 1; min-width: 0; display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 3px; min-height: 40px; padding: 2px 4px; border: 0; background: none; color: var(--foreground); font: inherit; cursor: pointer; text-align: left; }
.tg-hname { overflow: hidden; text-overflow: ellipsis; white-space: nowrap; }
.tg-menu { min-width: 24px; min-height: 24px; border: 0; background: none; color: var(--muted-foreground); cursor: pointer; }
.tg-menu-list { display: grid; width: auto; padding: 4px; }
.tg-menu-list button { padding: 6px 10px; border: 0; background: none; color: var(--foreground); font: inherit; text-align: left; cursor: pointer; }
.tg-cell { display: grid; place-items: center; border-right: 1px solid var(--border); cursor: pointer; }
.tg-box { display: inline-grid; place-items: center; width: 20px; height: 20px; border: 1.5px solid var(--muted-foreground); border-radius: 4px; }
.tg-box[aria-checked=true] { background: var(--primary); border-color: var(--primary); color: var(--primary-foreground); }
.tg-box svg { width: 14px; height: 14px; }
.tg-box.pending { outline: 1px dashed var(--muted-foreground); outline-offset: 2px; }
.columns-picker { max-height: 60vh; overflow: auto; }
.columns-picker fieldset { border: 0; margin: 0 0 8px; padding: 0; }
.columns-picker legend { font-size: 11px; text-transform: uppercase; letter-spacing: .06em; color: var(--muted-foreground); }
.column-choice { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 6px; padding: 2px 0; }
```

```python
    long_tag = "folder/" + "x" * 60 + "/" + "y" * 60 + "/" + "z" * 60  # 190 characters

    open_row(page, "JFK")

    overflow = page.evaluate(
        """() => {
            const list = document.querySelector('[data-testid=recording-list]');
            return list.scrollWidth - list.clientWidth;
        }"""
    )
    assert overflow <= 0
    page.goto(f"{server.url}/#/tags")
    page.get_by_test_id("untagged-only").uncheck()
    header = page.locator(f"[data-testid=grid-header][data-tag='{long_tag}']")
    expect(header).to_have_attribute("aria-label", long_tag)
    assert header.bounding_box()["width"] <= 90
```
The last test reloads the page with `goto`; the new tag is in the archive, so the grid's columns include it only if it is among the 15 most used. The demo has seven tags, so it is.

---
### Task 15: Phones, accessibility, theming and reduced motion

**Checkpoint lens:** UX and accessibility.

The rules that depend on the device and the person (spec §6, "Phones", and §9, "Accessibility", "Phones", "Motion"):
- On devices that can't hover, checkboxes always show, list rows show no ×, and the header's × has a 44 px target.
- On phones, the picker is a full-width bottom sheet (the + opens the dialog form there), the selection bar stays above the bottom of the screen, and the Tags page shows the Untagged list with the picker instead of the grid.
- `prefers-reduced-motion` turns off the toast, chip and overlay animations.
- axe-core scans the list and the grid in both themes; keyboard-only runs go through both; focus returns after the picker, the dialog and ×.

Checked before writing (CLAUDE.md): axe-core's API (`axe.run(context, { runOnly: { type: "tag", values } })`, `result.violations`), Playwright's `page.emulate_media(reduced_motion=…)` and `browser_context_args` (`viewport`, `is_mobile`, `has_touch`), MDN's `(hover: none)` and `prefers-reduced-motion` media queries, Tailwind v4's `translate-*` utilities (they set the `translate` property, which the bottom sheet resets).

**Files:**
- Modify: `packages/ui/frontend/src/components/TagPicker.tsx` (the + opens the dialog on phones)
- Modify: `packages/ui/frontend/src/components/TagsPage.tsx` (the phone's Untagged list)
- Modify: `packages/ui/frontend/src/app.css`
- Test: `packages/ui/tests/e2e/test_tagging.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `useMediaQuery`, `PHONE`, `NO_HOVER`, `useRowOrder` (Task 12); `RecordingList` (Task 12); `TagsPage` (Task 14).
- Produces: `TagsPage` renders `TagsGrid` (Task 14's grid, renamed) or `PhoneUntagged`; no new exports used elsewhere.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing e2e tests**

Append to `packages/ui/tests/e2e/test_tagging.py` (add `from pathlib import Path` to its imports):
```python
AXE = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2] / "frontend" / "node_modules" / "axe-core" / "axe.min.js"
WCAG = ["wcag2a", "wcag2aa", "wcag21a", "wcag21aa"]


def axe(page: Page, selector: str) -> list[str]:
    """axe-core's WCAG A and AA violations inside `selector` (spec §9: a dev dependency)."""
    page.add_script_tag(path=str(AXE))
    return page.evaluate(
        """async ([selector, tags]) => {
            const options = { runOnly: { type: "tag", values: tags } };
            const result = await axe.run(document.querySelector(selector), options);
            return result.violations.map((v) => `${v.id} (${v.nodes.length})`);
        }""",
        [selector, WCAG],
    )


@pytest.mark.parametrize("theme", ["light", "dark"])
def test_the_list_and_the_grid_pass_axe_in_both_themes(page: Page, server, console, theme):
    open_library(page, server.url)
    page.get_by_test_id(f"theme-{theme}").click()
    rows = page.get_by_test_id("recording-row")
    rows.first.click()
    rows.nth(2).click(modifiers=["ControlOrMeta"])  # selected, open, focused and the selection bar
    assert axe(page, ".list") == []
    rows.nth(2).hover()
    rows.nth(2).get_by_role("button", name=re.compile(r"^Add tags to")).click()
    assert axe(page, "[data-slot=popover-content]") == []
    page.keyboard.press("Escape")
    page.goto(f"{server.url}/#/tags")
    expect(page.get_by_test_id("tags-grid")).to_be_visible()
    assert axe(page, ".tags-page") == []


def test_the_toasts_follow_the_apps_theme(page: Page, server, console, demo_ids):
    open_library(page, server.url)
    page.get_by_test_id("theme-dark").click()
    open_row(page, "JFK")
    page.get_by_test_id("recording-header").get_by_role("button", name=re.compile(r"^Remove tag talks")).click()
    expect(page.get_by_test_id("action-toast")).to_be_visible()
    expect(page.locator("[data-sonner-toaster]")).to_have_attribute("data-sonner-theme", "dark")


def test_the_grids_roles_and_attributes(page: Page, server, console):
    page.goto(f"{server.url}/#/tags")
    page.get_by_test_id("untagged-only").uncheck()
    grid = page.get_by_role("grid", name="Tags")
    expect(grid).to_have_attribute("aria-multiselectable", "true")
    expect(grid).to_have_attribute("aria-rowcount", "5")
    cols = int(grid.get_attribute("aria-colcount"))
    expect(page.get_by_role("columnheader")).to_have_count(cols)
    expect(page.get_by_role("rowheader")).to_have_count(4)
    # Newest
    expect(page.get_by_role("columnheader").first).to_have_attribute("aria-sort", "descending")
    box = page.get_by_test_id("grid-row").first.get_by_role("checkbox", name="talks")
    expect(box).to_have_attribute("aria-checked", re.compile("true|false"))


def test_by_keyboard_alone_with_focus_returning(page: Page, server, console, demo_ids):
    jfk, fdr = demo_ids["jfk-rice"], demo_ids["fdr-fireside-1"]
    open_library(page, server.url)
    for _ in range(40):  # Tab from the top until the list's one tab stop
        page.keyboard.press("Tab")
        if page.evaluate("document.activeElement?.dataset?.testid") == "recording-row":
            break
    active = lambda: page.evaluate("document.activeElement?.closest('[data-testid=recording-row]')?.dataset.id")  # noqa: E731
    page.keyboard.press("ArrowDown")
    page.keyboard.press("ArrowDown")
    assert active() == jfk
    page.keyboard.press("t")
    box = page.get_by_test_id("picker-input")
    expect(box).to_be_focused()
    box.fill("by-keys")
    box.press("Shift+Enter")
    wait_until(lambda: ("by-keys", "you") in server.tags(jfk))
    box.press("Escape")
    wait_until(lambda: active() == jfk)  # focus returns to the row that had it
    page.keyboard.press("ArrowRight")  # onto the row's first ×
    expect(page.locator(":focus")).to_have_attribute("data-control", "remove")
    page.keyboard.press("Enter")  # removes `talks`; focus moves to the next control
    wait_until(lambda: ("talks", "you") not in server.tags(jfk))
    expect(page.locator(":focus")).to_have_attribute("data-control", re.compile("remove|add"))
    page.keyboard.press("ArrowDown")  # from a control, Down moves to the next row itself
    assert active() == fdr
    page.keyboard.press("ArrowRight")
    page.keyboard.press("Enter")  # FDR's only tag is `private`: the confirmation
    dialog = page.get_by_role("alertdialog")
    expect(dialog.get_by_role("button", name="Cancel")).to_be_focused()
    page.keyboard.press("Escape")
    expect(dialog).to_have_count(0)
    wait_until(lambda: active() == fdr)  # focus returns into the row
    # The grid, by keys: the first row's pinned cell, then a tag cell, then Space.
    page.goto(f"{server.url}/#/tags")
    page.get_by_test_id("grid-row").first.locator(".tg-pin").focus()
    page.keyboard.press("ArrowRight")
    page.keyboard.press(" ")
    expect(page.get_by_test_id("grid-row").first.locator("[data-testid=grid-cell]").first).to_have_attribute("aria-checked", "true")


def test_an_x_has_a_24px_target(page: Page, server, console):
    open_library(page, server.url)
    row = page.get_by_test_id("recording-row").filter(has_text="JFK")
    row.hover()
    box = row.locator(".chip-x").first.bounding_box()
    assert box["width"] >= 24 and box["height"] >= 24


@pytest.mark.browser_context_args(viewport={"width": 375, "height": 740}, is_mobile=True, has_touch=True)
def test_a_phone_layout(page: Page, server, console):
    page.goto(server.url)
    rows = page.get_by_test_id("recording-row")
    expect(rows).to_have_count(4)
    expect(rows.first.locator(".check")).to_be_visible()  # always, where nothing hovers
    expect(rows.filter(has_text="JFK").locator(".chip-x").first).to_be_hidden()  # no × on rows
    rows.filter(has_text="JFK").click()
    x = page.get_by_test_id("recording-header").locator(".chip-x").first
    box = x.bounding_box()
    assert box["width"] >= 44 and box["height"] >= 44
    overflow = page.evaluate("document.documentElement.scrollWidth - document.documentElement.clientWidth")
    assert overflow <= 0
    page.get_by_test_id("recording-header").get_by_role("button", name=re.compile(r"^Add tags to")).click()
    sheet = page.get_by_role("dialog")  # the dialog form, as a bottom sheet
    sheet_box = sheet.bounding_box()
    assert sheet_box["width"] >= 370 and sheet_box["y"] + sheet_box["height"] >= 735
    page.keyboard.press("Escape")
    page.goto(f"{server.url}/#/tags")
    expect(page.get_by_test_id("tags-grid")).to_have_count(0)
    expect(page.get_by_test_id("recording-row")).to_have_count(1)  # the Untagged list


def test_reduced_motion_turns_off_toast_and_chip_animations(page: Page, server, console):
    page.emulate_media(reduced_motion="reduce")
    open_library(page, server.url)
    open_row(page, "JFK")
    page.get_by_test_id("recording-header").get_by_role("button", name=re.compile(r"^Remove tag talks")).click()
    expect(page.get_by_test_id("action-toast")).to_be_visible()
    styles = page.evaluate(
        """() => [...document.querySelectorAll('[data-sonner-toast], .chip')].map((el) => {
            const s = getComputedStyle(el);
            return [s.transitionDuration, s.animationName];
        })"""
    )
    assert styles and all(set(d.replace(" ", "").split(",")) == {"0s"} for d, _ in styles)
    assert all(a == "none" for _, a in styles)
```

Expected: the phone, reduced-motion and keyboard tests fail (the header × is 24 px, the + opens a popover, the grid shows on a phone, toasts animate). Fix every axe violation the scans report in the code or the CSS, never by narrowing the scan.

- [ ] **Step 3: The + opens the bottom sheet on phones**

In `packages/ui/frontend/src/components/TagPicker.tsx`, import `{ PHONE, useMediaQuery }` from `@/lib/useMediaQuery`, and at the top of `AddTagsButton`'s body add:
```tsx
  const phone = useMediaQuery(PHONE);
  if (phone) {
    // On a phone the picker is a full-width bottom sheet: the dialog form (spec §6).
    return (
      <button type="button" className="add-tag" data-control="add" data-testid="add-tag"
        tabIndex={place === "row" ? -1 : 0} aria-label={`Add tags to ${title}`}
        onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); tagging.openPicker({ ids: [id], title: `Tag ${title}` }); }}>
        <Plus aria-hidden="true" />
      </button>
    );
  }
```
Move `const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);` above this block, so the hooks run in the same order on every render.

- [ ] **Step 4: The phone's Tags page is the Untagged list**

In `packages/ui/frontend/src/components/TagsPage.tsx`, rename `export function TagsPage()` to `function TagsGrid()`, add the imports `{ PHONE, useMediaQuery } from "../lib/useMediaQuery"`, `{ useRowOrder } from "../lib/useRowOrder"`, `type Filter` from `../types`, and `RecordingList` from `./RecordingList`, and add:
```tsx
const UNTAGGED: Filter = { kind: "untagged" };

/** On phones the Tags page shows the Untagged list with the picker, not the grid (spec §6). A
 * tap selects; + and the selection bar's Tag… tag. */
function PhoneUntagged() {
  const tagging = useTaggingContext();
  const order = useRowOrder(tagging.view, UNTAGGED, tagging.loadKey, tagging.revealed);
  const ids = useRef<string[]>([]);
  ids.current = order.ids;
  const [sel, setSel] = useState<Sel>(NO_SEL);
  const act = useCallback((a: SelAction) => setSel((s) => reduceSel(s, a, ids.current)), []);
  const byId = useMemo(() => new Map((tagging.view?.recordings ?? []).map((r) => [r.id, r])), [tagging.view]);
  const rows = order.ids.map((id) => byId.get(id)).filter((r): r is LibraryRow => r !== undefined);
  return (
    <main className="tags-page phone" data-testid="tags-page">
      <div className="grid-head">
        <h1>Tags</h1>
        <span className="muted" data-testid="left-count">{(tagging.view?.counts.untagged ?? 0).toLocaleString("en")} left</span>
      </div>
      <RecordingList rows={rows} sel={sel} act={act} openId={null} onOpen={() => {}} announce="" />
    </main>
  );
}

export function TagsPage() {
  const phone = useMediaQuery(PHONE);
  return phone ? <PhoneUntagged /> : <TagsGrid />;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: The device and motion rules**

Append to `packages/ui/frontend/src/app.css`:
```css
/* Devices that can't hover (tagging spec §6): checkboxes always show; list rows show no ×, so
   tags are removed from the header, with 44 px targets, or from the picker. */
@media (hover: none) {
  .check { visibility: visible; }
  .row .chip-x { display: none; }
  .head .chip-x { opacity: 1; min-width: 44px; min-height: 44px; margin: -12px -14px -12px -4px; }
  .head .add-tag { min-width: 44px; min-height: 44px; }
}
/* Phones: the picker is a full-width bottom sheet; the selection bar stays above the bottom. */
@media (max-width: 760px) {
  .picker-dialog {
    top: auto !important; bottom: 0 !important; left: 0 !important; right: 0 !important;
    width: 100% !important; max-width: none !important; translate: none !important;
    transform: none !important; border-radius: 12px 12px 0 0 !important;
  }
  .selection-bar { position: sticky; bottom: 0; z-index: 3; }
  .tags-page.phone .list { max-height: none; flex: 1; }
}
/* Reduced motion: no toast, chip or overlay animations (spec §9). */
@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
  [data-sonner-toast], [data-sonner-toast] *, .chip, .chip *,
  [data-slot=popover-content], [data-slot=dialog-content], [data-slot=alert-dialog-content],
  [data-slot=dialog-overlay], [data-slot=alert-dialog-overlay] {
    animation: none !important;
    transition: none !important;
  }
}
```

### Task 16: The Pages demo tags in memory

**Checkpoint lens:** forward compatibility (Pyodide 3.12, its older pydantic and markdown-it, Shinylive's delivery of messages).

The Pages entry already configures `TagService(pages_store(archive))` (Task 6). This task proves it in the browser: tagging works there, nothing is saved (a reload brings the demo back), `recordings_ui.tagging` and `recordings.tagops` import on Pyodide, Shinylive delivers `send_message` (spec §11), and the console stays clean.

Checked before writing (CLAUDE.md): `scripts/build_pages.py` (it vendors all of `recordings` and of `recordings_ui` except `app.py` and `__main__.py`, so `tagging.py`, `tagops.py` and `writing.py` ship; only `ArchiveStore` would import `writing.py`, and Pages never builds one); Shinylive 0.8.12's assets (Pyodide 0.27.7, Python 3.12, pydantic 2.10.5); `CLAUDE.md`, "Upgrading Shinylive".

**Files:**
- Test: `packages/ui/tests/test_build_pages.py`, `packages/ui/tests/pages/test_pages_site.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `pages/app.py` (Task 6), the browser UI (Tasks 10–15).
- Produces: nothing new.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

In `packages/ui/tests/test_build_pages.py`, in `test_the_app_holds_the_browser_parts_and_the_demo_without_media`, add to the set that must be `<= files`:

Append to `packages/ui/tests/pages/test_pages_site.py`:
```python
def test_tagging_works_in_the_browser_and_a_reload_brings_the_demo_back(page: Page, site_url, watch):
    # why: tagging spec §1 and §5. Pages tags in memory through the same operations, says so, and
    # saves nothing: a reload shows the demo as it was. It also proves recordings_ui.tagging and
    # recordings.tagops import on Pyodide, and that Shinylive delivers send_message (spec §11).
    app, _ = open_demo(page, site_url)
    expect(app.locator(".demo-note")).to_have_text("Demo: changes aren't saved", timeout=RENDER_TIMEOUT)
    row = app.get_by_test_id("recording-row").filter(has_text="Apollo 13")
    row.hover()
    row.get_by_role("button", name=re.compile(r"^Add tags to Apollo 13")).click()
    box = app.get_by_test_id("picker-input")
    box.fill("in-the-browser")
    box.press("Shift+Enter")
    chip = row.locator("[data-testid=chip][data-tag='in-the-browser']")
    expect(chip).to_have_count(1, timeout=RENDER_TIMEOUT)
    # the reply came
    expect(chip).not_to_have_class(re.compile(r"\bpending\b"), timeout=RENDER_TIMEOUT)
    expect(app.get_by_test_id("action-toast")).to_contain_text("Demo: changes aren't saved", timeout=RENDER_TIMEOUT)
    box.press("Escape")
    app, _ = open_demo(page, site_url)  # a reload
    again = app.get_by_test_id("recording-row").filter(has_text="Apollo 13")
    expect(again.locator("[data-testid=chip]")).to_have_count(0, timeout=RENDER_TIMEOUT)
    watch.assert_clean()


def test_removing_a_last_private_tag_asks_first_on_pages_too(page: Page, site_url, watch):
    app, _ = open_demo(page, site_url)
    app.get_by_test_id("recording-row").filter(has_text="Fireside").click()
    header = app.get_by_test_id("recording-header")
    expect(header).to_contain_text("Fireside", timeout=RENDER_TIMEOUT)
    header.get_by_role("button", name=re.compile(r"^Remove tag private from Fireside")).click()
    dialog = app.get_by_role("alertdialog")
    expect(dialog).to_contain_text("Remove private from 1 recording?", timeout=RENDER_TIMEOUT)
    dialog.get_by_role("button", name="Cancel").click()
    expect(header.locator("[data-testid=chip][data-tag=private]")).to_have_count(1, timeout=RENDER_TIMEOUT)
    watch.assert_clean()
```

```python
@pytest.fixture
def mirror_server(tmp_path, big_archive):
    """The 300-recording archive as the NAS mirror's copy, which no Writer will write (spec §9)."""
    copy = tmp_path / "mirror-archive"
    shutil.copytree(big_archive, copy)
    (copy / MIRROR).write_text("the NAS mirror's copy\n", encoding="utf-8")
    started = start_server(tmp_path / "server", archive=copy)
    yield started
    started.stop()
```

Append to `packages/ui/tests/e2e/test_tagging.py`:
```python
def test_the_mirrors_copy_cannot_tag_and_says_why(page: Page, mirror_server, console):
    page.goto(mirror_server.url)
    rows = page.get_by_test_id("recording-row")
    expect(rows.first).to_be_visible()
    expect(page.get_by_test_id("cannot-tag")).to_contain_text("mirror")  # once, in the list header
    expect(page.get_by_test_id("add-tag")).to_have_count(0)
    expect(page.locator(".chip-x")).to_have_count(0)
    expect(page.get_by_test_id("new-tag")).to_have_count(0)
    expect(rows.first).to_have_attribute("draggable", "false")
    rows.first.click()
    page.keyboard.press("t")  # T shows the reason in a toast
    expect(page.get_by_test_id("alert-toast")).to_contain_text("mirror")
    page.goto(f"{mirror_server.url}/#/tags")
    expect(page.get_by_test_id("cannot-tag")).to_contain_text("mirror")


def test_the_built_bundle_portals_toasts_dialogs_and_scrolls(page: Page, big_server, console):
    # why: spec §6. react-dom is bundled beside shinyreact's react-dom/client global, so the
    # built bundle must open the picker (a portal) and close it on an outside click, show a toast,
    # open the dialog, and scroll a list longer than the viewport, with a clean console.
    page.goto(big_server.url)
    rows = page.get_by_test_id("recording-row")
    first = rows.first
    first.hover()
    first.get_by_role("button", name=re.compile(r"^Add tags to")).click()
    box = page.get_by_test_id("picker-input")
    expect(box).to_be_visible()
    page.mouse.click(5, 5)  # outside
    expect(box).to_have_count(0)
    first.hover()
    first.get_by_role("button", name=re.compile(r"^Add tags to")).click()
    box.fill("bundle")
    box.press("Shift+Enter")
    expect(page.get_by_test_id("action-toast")).to_contain_text("Added bundle to 1 recording")
    box.press("Escape")
    page.get_by_test_id("filter-tag").filter(has_text="private").click()
    rows.first.click()
    header = page.get_by_test_id("recording-header")
    header.get_by_role("button", name=re.compile(r"^Remove tag private from")).click()
    expect(page.get_by_role("alertdialog")).to_be_visible()
    page.get_by_role("alertdialog").get_by_role("button", name="Cancel").click()
    page.get_by_test_id("filter-all").click()
    rows.first.focus()
    page.keyboard.press("End")
    last = page.locator("[data-testid=recording-row][aria-rowindex='301']")
    expect(last).to_be_in_viewport()
    expect(last).to_contain_text("Synthetic 000")


def test_a_large_batch_shows_progress_then_its_result(page: Page, big_server, console):
    page.goto(big_server.url)
    page.get_by_test_id("recording-row").first.click()
    page.keyboard.press("ControlOrMeta+a")
    page.keyboard.press("t")
    box = page.get_by_role("dialog", name="Tag 300 recordings").get_by_test_id("picker-input")
    box.fill("bulk")
    box.press("Shift+Enter")
    progress = page.get_by_test_id("progress-toast")
    expect(progress.locator(".sr-only")).to_have_text("Tagging 300 recordings…")  # announced once
    # outside the live region
    expect(progress.locator("[aria-hidden=true]")).to_contain_text("of 300")
    expect(page.get_by_test_id("action-toast")).to_contain_text("Added bulk to 300 recordings", timeout=60_000)
    expect(progress).to_have_count(0)  # the result replaced it
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Teach `CLAUDE.md` the rules**

In `CLAUDE.md`, replace the first bullet under "The rules the code depends on:":
```markdown
- **React stays external,** mapped to `window.shinyreact.React`. Two React copies make
  every hook silently return nothing.
```
with:
```markdown
- **React stays external,** mapped to `window.shinyreact.React`. Two React copies make
  every hook silently return nothing. Only `react` and `react-dom/client` map to the globals;
  the `react-dom` index is bundled, because shinyreact's `ReactDOM` global has no `createPortal`
  or `flushSync` (`docs/upstream-issues.md`). `src/build.test.ts` pins this.
```
After the "Every output is subscribed in `App`" bullet, add:
```markdown
- **Every message handler and input lives in `App`** (through `useTagging`), mounted once:
  shinyreact drops a message that has no handler. Tag requests are only ever sent by a person's
  action: never on load, from a URL or its hash, or from storage.
```
Under "## Archive rules (spec §6)", after the bullet that starts "**Privacy:**", add:
```markdown
- **Tags change only through `recordings.tagops`** (`AddTags`, `RemoveTags`, `RestoreTags`),
  applied by `Writer.mutate` on the tag service's one writer thread
  (`recordings_ui/tagging.py`, `writing.py`). The privacy gate is inside the operations: a
  recording never loses its last private tag unless its ID was confirmed. `tagops.py` imports
  only `recordings.models`, because the Pages demo runs it on Pyodide.
- **Tagging's logs and replies carry fixed phrases,** IDs, counts and exception class names:
  never a tag name, a title or `str(exc)`.
```
Under "## Toolchain", after the `make e2e` bullet, add:
```markdown
- **`make e2e`'s writing tests** (`packages/ui/tests/e2e/test_tagging.py`) get a server each,
  started with `TMPDIR=<tmp_path>`, and read what the app wrote through `Archive`. axe-core runs
  from `packages/ui/frontend/node_modules`, so run `npm ci` first.
```

```markdown
Stage 1 built the read-only Library over a demo archive. Stage 2a moves the real archive in: a

rest-server on the NAS with a monthly restore test, the NAS mirror, alerts, and Status.
`docs/runbooks/first-run.md` is the first real run.

Tagging, part A (`docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-15-tagging-a-design.md`) adds the triage tools:
Finder-style selection, the tag picker (`T`, or the **+** on a row), × on chips, drag and drop
onto sidebar tags, the Tags page grid at `#/tags`, Undo for everything (Cmd/Ctrl+Z), and a
confirmation before any recording loses its last private tag. `make demo` saves tags to its
temporary copy; the Live demo keeps them in memory until you reload.
```

## Self-review (2026-10-15)

**Spec coverage.** Each section of the spec, and where it lands:
- §1 (done means): triage in the grid (Task 14), + without reopening (Tasks 11, 12), drag onto the sidebar (Task 13), Undo while the page is open (Tasks 5, 10), the privacy confirmation (Tasks 2, 5, 10), the same in the real app, `make demo` and Pages (Tasks 6, 16).
- §2 (scope): `RemoveTags` and Undo (Task 2); the service and its stores (Tasks 4, 5); Finder selection and rebuilt rows (Task 12); the picker (Task 11); × (Task 10); drag and drop (Task 13); toasts, Cmd/Ctrl+Z and the confirmation (Tasks 10, 12); Refresh (Tasks 7, 12, 14); the grid (Task 14).
- §3 (decisions): the confirmation rules (Tasks 2, 5); + on rows and the header (Tasks 11, 12); Pages in memory (Tasks 6, 16); the write path over Shiny (Task 7).
- §4 (core): Task 2 (operations, effects, the gate, names, `is_private_tag`), Task 3 (`mutate` through the wrapper, the origin).
- §5 (server): stores (Task 4), the writer thread (Tasks 4, 6), demo mode (Task 6), `can_tag` (Tasks 4, 6, 17), requests, checks, the confirmation, applying, replies, Undo, the record, deltas (Task 5), Refresh and snippets (Task 7), logs (Task 5), Pyodide import (Tasks 4, 16).
- §6 (browser): the build (Task 8), handlers in `App` (Task 10), rows, selection and focus (Task 12), states and rows staying put (Tasks 9, 12), chips, + and the picker (Tasks 10, 11), drag and drop (Task 13), the queue, toasts and the confirmation (Tasks 9, 10), disabled tagging, shortcuts and hints (Tasks 12, 13, 17), phones (Task 15), components (Task 8).
- §7 (grid): Task 14, its phone form in Task 15.
- §8 (errors): fixed reasons and stops (Tasks 4, 5), bad requests (Task 5), Undo's messages (Task 5), a lost connection (Tasks 9, 10), "Still working…" (Tasks 9, 10), the writer thread exiting (Tasks 4, 5).
- §9 (testing): the core (Task 2), the service and its races (Task 5), the wiring and two sessions (Task 7), demo isolation (Tasks 6, 7), Vitest (Task 9), e2e (Tasks 10–15, 17), the large archive (Tasks 12, 14, 17), `can_tag` false (Task 17), accessibility, phones and motion (Task 15), Pages (Task 16), the stale-binding test (unchanged, run by every `make e2e`).
- §10 (main spec and runbook): Task 1.
- §11 (to check): `extended_task` (Task 7), the `react-dom` bundling (Tasks 8, 17), `send_message` on Pages (Task 16), `drag_to` (Task 13), `revisions.origin`'s readers (Task 3), the per-service lock timeout (Task 3).

**Placeholders.** None: every code step carries its code, and every test its assertions.

**Type consistency.** Checked across tasks: `Recorded`, `Effect`, `RemoveTags(tags, may_expose)`, `RestoreTags(effect, may_expose)` (Tasks 2, 3, 5); `Classified`, the phrases and `Store`'s methods (Tasks 4, 5, 7); `TagSession.check/run/library/check_snippets/snippets` (Tasks 5, 7); `Tagging`'s fields (Tasks 10–15); `Sel`, `SelAction`, `targetsOf` (Tasks 9, 12–15); `openPickerFor`, `pickerTitle`, `AddTagsButton` (Tasks 11–15); `useRowOrder` (Tasks 12, 15); the e2e fixtures `server`, `big_server`, `mirror_server`, `console` (Tasks 10, 12, 17).
